# SwegHammer decision ledger — what's been tried (READ THIS before proposing a lever)

**Purpose.** The compact, routinely-read index of what has already been **landed**,
**reverted**, **parked**, or **ruled out**, so neither the worker nor the watchdog
burns a wave (or tokens) re-attempting something already settled. One line per item.

**This file is the index, not the story.** Full narrative detail lives in the archives
(`docs/AUTO_LOOP_LOG_archive.md`, `docs/CURRENT_STATE_archive.md`, `LOOP_QA_ARCHIVE.md`)
and the auto-memory. The live `docs/CURRENT_STATE.md` head holds the current frame + next
levers; this ledger holds the long memory.

**Update rule.** Add exactly one line when a mechanic **lands**, is **reverted**, or is
**parked** — newest-last within each section. Do not narrate here; that is the archives'
job. If you are about to propose a lever, check the Reverted / Parked / Forbidden sections
first.

---

## ⛔ GROUND-TRUTH CORRECTION (2026-06-21) — "loser-displacement" is an INVENTED rule; do not build it
Triple-checked (5-agent run `wf_85cc5a6e`, code verified at `868f9a4`):
- **One-Unit-per-model is TRUE, not a phantom.** `army.py:684-741` builds one `Unit` per physical model; `_assign_army_oc` (`simulator.py:949-1023`) sums per-model Objective Control; the strictly-greater contest is faithful 10e "Level of Control". Keep this framing — it is the documented design, not a misread.
- **"An out-fought melee survivor cannot hold / is displaced from an objective" is NOT a 10e rule** (Wahapedia / datacard.app). The ONLY things that remove/zero a *living* unit's OC are **death**, **Battle-shock** (OC→0, `simulator.py:966-967`), and **moving out of range** (Fall Back). A surviving melee loser keeps full OC. So `DISPLACEMENT_SUBSTRATE_PLAN.md` Stages 2/3 (swarm-charge, cleared-position loser-removal) chase a phantom and **must not be built** — they also contradict the shipped faithful tarpit (`SWEG_DISPLACE_FALLBACK`).
- **Redirect displacement-flavoured work** to two faithful, citable levers: (a) **Battle-shock application rate** after melee attrition; (b) **melee lethality** (under-kill leaves un-killed models' OC in the contest). Positional-spread's only faithful fix is AI massing (`SWEG_MASS`), never an OC knob.
- Stage-1 screening this session showed the stratagem-scope fixes are real fidelity but do NOT move the gated mean absolute error (the over-poles are structural).
- **FAITHFUL-FLOOR DECLARATION (scoped) — 2026-06-21.** A second grounding triple-check (run `wf_a26548fe`) verified the two redirect levers proposed after killing loser-displacement: **Battle-shock application rate** and **melee lethality**. BOTH are faithful-by-construction — the sim already implements the real printed rules correctly (Battle-shock: below-half gate `simulator.py:8908`, 2D6-vs-Leadership `:8489/:8522`, OC-0 + stratagem-exclusion wired; melee: faithful hit/wound/save/damage, and Pile-In/Consolidate/Heroic-Intervention/±1-cap/Fights-First all already closed by the 2026-05-31 audit) — and neither has any external real-meta reference, so adjusting either "rate" is a forbidden tune-to-win-rate dial. **Therefore the ~70-pt melee/Astra-Militarum residual is a faithful floor for displacement + Battle-shock-rate + melee-lethality — do not chase any of these three.** SCOPE CAVEAT: this does NOT assert global Stage-1 convergence; residuals on non-melee-cluster factions may still hold grounded levers or AI-performance improvements.
- **⚠️ THE "FLOOR DECLARATION" ABOVE IS RETRACTED — 2026-06-21 (same day, later).** It was WRONG and the project owner correctly rejected it: the calibration target is REAL-game win rates under the REAL printed rules, so a sufficiently faithful sim MUST be able to reproduce them — a residual is ALWAYS a missing/mis-modelled mechanism, never a "floor". Game-shape signatures (`scripts/diag_signatures`, N=150) localised the real gap: **going-first win rate 66% (real ~50%)**, secondary VP 38.9 (real 22.7), flat round-2-5 trajectory (no attrition decay), **challenger cards not modelled**. The systematic melee-over / shooting-and-horde-under bias is **AI PLAY QUALITY + per-round tempo**, NOT a representation floor. PROOF: a proactive melee-aware **KITING** AI improvement (`SWEG_KITE_MOVE`) dropped going-first **74%→56%** and lowered the melee over-poles — the first lever all session to move the dominant signature. (The objective-blind v1 cratered the shooting armies by kiting off objectives; an objective-aware refinement is built, unscreened.) The melee/Astra-Militarum residual is FIXABLE via grounded **AI-play-quality improvements** — built code-only this session: `SWEG_KITE_MOVE` (objective-aware kiting, priority), `SWEG_CHARGE_FIRSTSTRIKE` (skirmisher strike-first value), `SWEG_CHARGE_BAIT` (overwatch cheap-first sequencing). See `data/_lever_screening_runbook.md`. **Do NOT re-declare a floor.** Also-killed phantom (same investigation): round-5 "second player takes last turn" is ALREADY modelled (`simulator.py:9789` `((first,second),(second,first))` + scoring after both turns `:706-709`) — do not build it.

## ⚠ FRAME-BIAS FINDING (2026-07-07) — check `scripts/diag_frame.py` before trusting any pole
The standing metric reads army-A cells only, and per-faction A/B positional bias runs to ±5 points: on sc57a the gated MAE is 3.08 (A-frame) vs **2.61 (symmetrized)**, and the entire Chaos Daemons pole (5.19) is frame artifact (sym 0.23) while AM (→15.9) and Aeldari (→6.4) are REAL and understated. MEASURED, N=36,960 anchor games. Consequences: the going-second lever family's rejections are confounded (re-screen under the sym lens); the A/B positional asymmetry itself is a Stage-1 fidelity bug (real 40k has no board-side dependence) and is an open root-cause target; frame adoption is an owner decision (re-anchor). See `docs/PROTOCOL_REVIEW_2026-07.md` and `docs/LEVER_PROTOCOL.md` §6.

## ⭐ UNDER-POLE PILOT SWEEP (2026-07-08, owner-ordered) — ALL THREE under-poles are NOT floors; one systemic mechanism found
Full PILOT_PROTOCOL pilots (board-images read, per the owner's "have you ran a manual pilot review of the underpoles?") on the sc59a under-poles came back non-empty everywhere. (1) **TEMPLATE-REALIZATION LOTTERY (systemic, MEASURED)**: the seed walk exhausts its slice on the guaranteed spine, so tail template units field by lottery — Astra Militarum's Kasrkin/Scions/Chimera/Death-Korps/Rough-Riders/Heavy-Weapons at 20-40% of builds (boards: the 8%-crater Astartes loss was decided by a turn-1 whole-army walk into a gunline in a build with NO Scions/Chimera delivery), Emperor's Children's adopted 2x-Prince/2x-Rhino at 0.85/0.75 mean (0 princes in 7/20 builds), while Genestealer Cults realizes 13/13 at 100% — fix building as `SWEG_TEMPLATE_REALIZE`. (2) **Orks residual is horde-vs-GUNLINE, not durability** — per-opponent shape inverts the wall story (better vs bricks than vs shooters); boards show open-ground marching + charge over-concentration onto one brick while soft squads hold markers free; sourced list gap (Snikrot/Kommandos/Zodgrod, repo's own compaudit) building as `SWEG_ORKS_LIST2`. (3) **Emperor's Children reproduces the Daemons round-5 objective-abandonment misplay** (melee leaves 3-round-held bastions to bounce off a Grav-tank) — the existing SWEG_MELEE_HOLD_OBJECTIVE lever's exact firing condition, EC-scoped screen run; plus the lone Daemon Prince is auto-reserved by the faction-blind deploy split and dies on arrival (probe queued). Method lesson re-confirmed a fourth time: batteries and audits missed all of this; reading the boards found it.

- TRADE EVALUATOR v1 (`SWEG_TRADE_EVAL`, composed on the value field) — REJECTED-empirical as priced, 2026-07-10: composed N=40 vs sc62a read gated 2.74 → 3.84 (+1.10 decisively worse) — the exchange tilt surrenders the value field's wall corrections (Imperial Knights +0.8 → +7.7, Chaos Knights reverses) while only Votann of the five overshoots materially recovers (one unplanned win: the Adeptus Mechanicus pole erased +8.9 → −0.7; one new crater: Genestealer Cults −9.1). THE MECHANISM SIGNAL: the walked-into-it rate RISES under trade — the reply term under-deters realized next-turn exposure; that is the named defect for iteration two (re-price the reply, or compose with the charge consumer instead of move). Build sound (17.5% of decisions changed, exact sanity, byte-identical off at eval scale); held on the trade-eval branch. ALSO MEASURED: value-alone vs the sc62a frame reads +0.65 headline but Imperial Knights lands nearly ON target (+0.8 err) — the wall structure transfers frames.

- VALUE-MOVE FACTION MASK v1, five wall factions (`SWEG_VALUE_MOVE=1` + `SWEG_VALUE_MOVE_FACTIONS="Astra Militarum,Death Guard,Imperial Knights,Chaos Knights,World Eaters"`, build fa034ed on the scoping worktree) — MEASURED 2026-07-10, N=40 scoped-paired vs sc62a: **gated mean absolute error 2.60 → 1.95 A-frame / 1.79 symmetrized — BELOW the ≤2.0 Stage-1 target on both frames for the first time in project history** (frame check run per LEVER_PROTOCOL; the sc62a baseline is 2.60/2.42). Eleven decisive movers. The honest mechanism read, recorded BEFORE any adoption: the three over-poles closed hard and correctly (Death Guard −4.08, Imperial Knights −5.14, Chaos Knights −5.48, all toward real), but ASTRA MILITARUM MOVED DECISIVELY AWAY from real (−1.92 on the biggest under-pole, 25.4 → 23.4) and World Eaters drifted away flat (−1.27) — the scoped factions' own value-play is a SELF-HANDICAP against kill-maximizing baseline opponents, and the up-cluster (Chaos Daemons +5.46, Thousand Sons +3.61, Chaos Space Marines +3.33, Emperor's Children +3.07, Orks +2.60, Aeldari +1.97, Drukhari +1.90) is partly closed-matrix redistribution of those five factions' surrendered wins. Global-on's AM lift (+6.58) therefore never came from AM's own piloting — it came from OPPONENTS playing victory-point-focused rather than kill-maximizing, which is an argument that the FAITHFUL shape is global (all real players chase victory points), not scoped. PRE-REGISTERED NEXT CELL (in flight at write time): mask v2 = "Death Guard,Imperial Knights,Chaos Knights" only (drop the two factions whose own pole the mask decisively/directionally hurt; mechanism rule stated in advance — a faction belongs in the mask only if its OWN value-play moves its OWN pole toward real). Decision rule: v2 headline ≤ v1 (≤1.95 A-frame) with no new decisive wrong-direction mover → prefer v2 (smaller mask, no wrong-way poles); v2 decisively worse than v1 → AM/WE membership was load-bearing via self-nerf redistribution, the compensating-error smell, and the owner decides the doctrine question (enable-mask = playstyle modelling vs target-fitting; the standing anti-knob note on the value-field entry applies). ADOPTION IS THE OWNER'S CALL either way — this entry records evidence, not a flip.

- VALUE-MOVE FACTION MASK v2, three factions ("Death Guard,Imperial Knights,Chaos Knights") — MEASURED 2026-07-10, DISQUALIFIED by its own pre-registered rule despite the lowest headline in project history: N=40 vs sc62a read gated 1.72 paired / **1.49 A-frame / 1.47 symmetrized**, with Imperial Knights INSIDE noise for the first time (46.0 vs real 47.7) and Astra Militarum recovering to flat-positive (+1.01) — BUT World Eaters, which sat ON its real target at the anchor (45.2 vs real 44.9), broke to a brand-new gated-6.10 pole (sim 54.4, paired +6.00 decisive). CORRECTION recorded: the initial paired-table read of "World Eaters toward real" was wrong — the raw target table shows the opposite. MECHANISM NOW LEGIBLE: World Eaters' v1 membership was a compensating PAIR — its own value-play self-handicap offset its feasting on the three softened durable factions; removing it leaves the feast unpaid. This falsifies the v1-era membership rule ("in the mask only if own-play helps own pole") as incomplete: membership also prices the member's matchups against other members. Per the pre-registered disqualifier (no new decisive wrong-direction mover), v2 is NOT preferred. Genestealer Cults −1.85 decisive paired but inside its noise band (gated 0) — noted, not disqualifying.

- VALUE-MOVE FACTION MASK v3 = FOUR factions ("Death Guard,Imperial Knights,Chaos Knights,World Eaters" — drop ONLY Astra Militarum from v1) — PRE-REGISTERED 2026-07-10 before launch, THE FINAL MASK CELL regardless of outcome (grid then complete: global / five / three / four, bracketing both disputed memberships; any further mask search is the knob slope and is forbidden). Mechanism: keeps World Eaters' self-handicap (which held it on-target in v1) and removes only the Astra Militarum self-harm (v1's sole decisive wrong-way mover). EXPECTATION: Death Guard / Imperial Knights / Chaos Knights close as in v1-v2; World Eaters stays inside its noise band; Astra Militarum lands baseline-or-better (in v2 it read +1.01 with three softened opponents); headline at-or-below v1's 1.95. FALSIFIERS (any one fails the cell): Astra Militarum decisively away from real; World Eaters beyond its noise band; any anchor-calibrated faction acquiring a decisive wrong-direction move beyond noise. If it passes it becomes the adoption candidate ON THE OWNER'S DOCTRINE CALL (enable-mask vs global-refit — the global-shape argument stands: real players of ALL factions chase victory points, so the mask may be a stopgap for v1 field pricing, and the honest long-run fix is repricing the global field so it stops breaking the kill-tempo factions). Residual ladder at v2 for context (A-frame gated): Astra Militarum 14.56 — now roughly half the remaining error mass and untouched by any mask — then Aeldari 4.15, Adeptus Mechanicus 2.38, Death Guard 2.33, Chaos Knights 1.74.

- VALUE-MOVE FACTION MASK v3, four factions ("Death Guard,Imperial Knights,Chaos Knights,World Eaters") — MEASURED 2026-07-10, FAILED its pre-registered falsifier: headline lowest yet (paired 1.67; raw **1.46 A-frame / 1.36 symmetrized**; Imperial Knights inside noise at 47.3 vs real 47.7; Astra Militarum decisively TOWARD real for the first time under any mask, +1.50) — but World Eaters broke BEYOND its noise band in the DOWNWARD direction (38.1 vs real 44.9, gated 3.45). Falsifier 2 fired; the cell fails; THE MASK GRID IS CLOSED (global / five / three / four all measured, no further cells permitted).

- MASK-GRID SYNTHESIS (2026-07-10, the owner's decision memo). The four cells decompose into one ROBUST signal and one UNSTABLE artifact. ROBUST: value-aware movement for the durable trio — Death Guard, Imperial Knights, Chaos Knights — closes their over-poles in EVERY configuration (each −4 to −6.4 toward real regardless of other membership); that is a stable, mechanism-consistent finding: those three win in the sim by out-killing, but win in reality by out-scoring, and giving them the real playstyle fixes their poles. UNSTABLE: World Eaters' pole is hypersensitive to compensating interactions — on-target ONLY in the exact v1 configuration (its own self-handicap offsetting soft masked opponents: 43.9 in v1, 54.4 in v2, 38.1 in v3) — and Astra Militarum's pole is UNTOUCHABLE by any mask (gated 13.6-14.6 in all cells; its improvement always comes from OPPONENTS playing victory-point-focused, maximal under global-on). SURVIVING ADOPTION CANDIDATE by pre-registered rules: v1 five-mask alone (1.95/1.79, sub-target both frames; known cost, Astra Militarum −1.92 deeper into an already-broken pole). PRINCIPLED ALTERNATIVE: adopt no mask and reprice the GLOBAL field (the faithful shape — every real player chases victory points; global-on already lifts Astra Militarum +6.58 and its failure mode is specifically breaking five kill-tempo factions, which is a PRICING defect, not a scope defect). Recurring minor drift noted for the frame ledger: Genestealer Cults −1.8 decisive-paired in v2 and v3 but inside its noise band both times. OWNER DECIDES: (a) adopt v1 mask as a stopgap and re-anchor sub-2.0, (b) reject masks, fund the global-field reprice lane, or (c) both — v1 now, reprice later and retire the mask when global passes. Either adoption batches with the stratagem once-per-phase flip in one re-anchor.

- VALUE-FIELD OFFENSE TERM (`SWEG_VALUE_OFFENSE`, owner-directed 2026-07-10: "damage output as a factor in movement", the global-field reprice lane from the mask-grid synthesis) — PRE-REGISTERED before build. MECHANISM: V(p) gains a move-class-conditional offense value — the unit's expected wounds dealt from candidate destination p, converted to points in the field's existing exchange currency and discounted over the scoring horizon — where the conditioning uses only what the resolution engine actually enforces (verified in code before registration: Heavy +1 to hit only when `moved_this_round` is false, code/units.py ~3466; the Advance shooting lockout with the Assault-weapon exemption; Big Guns Never Tire both clauses). Remain-stationary candidates keep the Heavy bonus, normal-move candidates lose it, Advance candidates count Assault weapons only. Knob-free: all constants inherited from the field's existing reference constants and the audited expected-wounds helpers. EXPECTATION (stated in advance): global-on + offense should RECOVER the five kill-tempo factions that global v1 broke (Emperor's Children, Chaos Space Marines, Custodes, Votann, Drukhari — their win condition is output positioning, which v1 priced at zero) while PRESERVING the durable trio's over-pole closure and Astra Militarum's opponent-side lift; World Eaters should sit inside noise with no mask. FALSIFIERS (any one fails the global cell): a durable-trio pole re-opens beyond noise; any anchor-calibrated faction breaks beyond noise; headline worse than the anchor's 2.60. MECHANISM INSTRUMENTS: fraction of move intents changed by the term, and gunline hold-rate (Heavy-weapon units should hold MORE with the reflex gates untouched — the term should re-derive, not fight, the adopted holds). SCREEN PLAN, pre-registered: cell 1 = global `SWEG_VALUE_MOVE=1 SWEG_VALUE_OFFENSE=1` (no mask) N=40 vs sc62a — THE decision cell; cell 2 (only if cell 1 fails on specific poles) = five-mask + offense. No other offense-term cells without a new registration.

- VALUE-FIELD OFFENSE TERM cell 1, global (`SWEG_VALUE_MOVE=1 SWEG_VALUE_OFFENSE=1`, build bf03929) — MEASURED 2026-07-10, ALL THREE FALSIFIERS FIRED: N=40 vs sc62a read paired gated 2.60 → 2.81 / raw frame 3.68 A / 3.79 symmetrized. Signature: Imperial Knights +5.30 decisively AWAY (59.2 → 64.5 vs real 47.7 — a durable-trio pole made WORSE than baseline), Tyranids +6.59 and World Eaters +3.92 broken beyond noise, Necrons +3.90 — ALL high-volume multi-target shooting platforms, exactly the over-credit the builder pre-flagged in its design note: the offense summed expected wounds over ALL reachable targets (positional coverage), over-crediting central multi-target cells and driving big gun platforms into aggressive midfield positions. ATTRIBUTION: the sum-over-targets form was the ORCHESTRATOR'S brief specification, a deviation from the codebase's established exchange convention (`_trade_our_return`, best-single-target); the builder flagged the departure explicitly at delivery. PARTIAL HYPOTHESIS CONFIRMATION worth keeping: the five kill-tempo factions that global value-alone broke (Emperor's Children +0.78, Chaos Space Marines +0.38, Custodes +1.14, Votann −0.39, Drukhari −2.72) all read roughly FLAT vs anchor — pricing output does rescue them from the value field's self-handicap; the defect is the multi-target inflation, not the concept. Chaos Daemons +4.20 landed nearly on its real target (52.6) as a side effect. ITERATION 2 PRE-REGISTERED (the pre-flagged correction, one iteration then the lane STOPS per the trade-lane discipline): reprice offense to the established best-single-target convention. EXPECTATION: the multi-target inflation collapses (Imperial Knights / Tyranids / Necrons / World Eaters return toward their anchor values), the kill-tempo five stay recovered, headline at-or-below 2.60. FALSIFIERS: unchanged from cell 1 (durable-trio pole beyond noise the wrong way; any calibrated faction broken; headline above 2.60). If iteration 2 fails: STOP — the offense lane parks, and the mask v1 five-faction cell (1.95/1.79) stands as the only surviving sub-target candidate for the owner's doctrine call.

- EXCHANGE-RATE GROUNDING LANE (owner-funded 2026-07-10, option c of the job-lane stop memo) — PRE-REGISTERED before any data: fit the wounds-to-victory-points exchange (`_trade_vp_per_wound`, the constant every layer composition inherits and the named cause of all three 2026-07-10 failure signatures) from evidence instead of assertion. METHOD: (1) extend `--log-games` with per-game margins (final capped victory points both sides, points-value destroyed both sides — additive `--log-margins` flag, anchor-log format byte-unchanged without it); (2) one instrumented collection run on the sc62a frame (defaults, no layer gates — the exchange rate must be measured on the FAITHFUL baseline, not on any layer's own behaviour); (3) fit — METHOD AMENDED 2026-07-11 BEFORE any collected data was seen (the originally registered logistic form was degenerate on identification grounds: the victory-point margin DETERMINES the winner by construction, so its coefficient is unbounded and the ratio meaningless): the well-posed fit is a LINEAR regression of final victory-point margin on kill margin (points destroyed differential) across games — the slope β is the measured victory-point value of destroying one enemy point, and the grounded exchange substitutes as measured-vp-per-wound = β × target points-per-wound (replacing the asserted `_trade_vp_per_wound` scale). Cross-read: a logistic fit of win outcome on kill margin ALONE (sanity direction check only, no ratio claimed). Report per-faction and per-matchup slope stability as the sanity check (roughly universal expected; strong spread is itself a finding). Confounding caveat stated in advance: the observational slope conflates "killing causes scoring" with "winning armies do both" — the paired common-random-number structure and within-matchup slopes are the honest partial controls, and the constant remains a decision-heuristic price, not a causal estimate. HONEST CAVEAT, stated in advance: sim-log fitting is semi-self-referential — it measures how much killing buys winning UNDER CURRENT PILOTING, so the grounded constant may need one fixed-point iteration (re-fit after any adopted piloting change); it is nonetheless anchored to the sim's real win-condition arithmetic rather than asserted, which is the defect being cured. CONSUMERS on success: re-screen the parked job layer (f7a5cd4 + its speed pass) and the value+offense composition with the measured rate substituted — new registrations at that point, falsifiers to be stated then. Speed passes (mainline hot path + job-layer channel caching, both byte-identity-gated) run first so the collection and re-screens are cheap.

- ASTRA MILITARUM ASSASSINATION BLEED (2026-07-12, hand-pilot per PILOT_PROTOCOL, MEASURED vs sc63a) — the dominant Astra Militarum under-pole (23.8% A-frame / 24.2% symmetrized, real 45.3 — pole REAL not frame artifact) is NOT a durability floor and NOT a piloting gap: in its close losses Astra Militarum ends with EQUAL/MORE points-remaining and loses the victory-point race (seed-24 vs Astartes: 1188 vs 1136 points left, lost 21-46). Primary is near-even (AM 29 vs Astartes 33); the loss is SECONDARY, and per-card (N=15) it is almost entirely ASSASSINATION — Astartes secondary 26.7 = assassination 22.5, Imperial Knights 27.1 = assassination 19.0. The feed is Astra-Militarum-SPECIFIC not a general over-score (Astartes scores 26.9 sec vs AM but 8.0 vs Custodes / 11.9 vs T'au; horde armies feed, elite starve), because Astra Militarum fields ~10 standalone CHARACTERs (5 Command Squads + 2 Castellans + Lord Solar + Ursula + Commissar in the 31-unit build) and the sim does NOT model 10e Leader attachment (`code/secondaries.py:356` "attached-Leader inclusion not separately modelled") — every character is a free Assassination target that the real Leader rule would hide inside a bodyguard squad. SIZING PROBE (N=40 paired, suppress Assassination-off-AM = perfect Leader protection, UPPER BOUND, `scripts/_am_assassination_probe.py`): Astartes 5%→55% (+50.0pp, 20-0 flips), Imperial Knights 7.5%→40% (+32.5, 13-0), Sororitas +5, T'au +5, Genestealer Cults +0 (its crater is the SEPARATE primary-blowout disease, opp primary 43 vs 19) — the largest Astra Militarum lever effect in the campaign, larger than value-move's +6.58. No piloting call touches it: contest tactics −5pp (bodies die on exposed markers), holds-off washes, chase_vp +2.5 sub-noise. LEVER DIRECTION (candidate, unbuilt): model 10e Leader attachment (attached CHARACTER not an eligible Assassination target until its bodyguard is destroyed) — faithful, citable, GLOBAL, disproportionately lifts the most character-dense army; directly attacks the known secondary-VP over-production signature (38.9 vs real 22.7). PENDING before build (rule 10, verify don't invent): (a) the 10e Leader / Assassination target-eligibility rule, (b) the Cadian Command Squad CHARACTER keyword + whether the archetype over-fields characters (list-realism component), (c) a proper N=80 sc63a screen (the probe is mechanism/upper-bound evidence, not a rate). Full narrative `docs/PILOT_FINDINGS.md` (2026-07-12 section).

- LEADER ATTACHMENT + SECONDARY PER-UNIT COUNTING (2026-07-12, BUILT + gated + byte-identical off, tests +0 failures) — the AM Assassination bleed's two faithful fixes. `SWEG_LEADER_ATTACH`: 10e Leader/Attached-unit protection — an attached CHARACTER can't be selected as a ranged OR melee target while its bound host squad has a living bodyguard model; [PRECISION] bypasses; multi-leader hosts allowed. REPLACES the fabricated `simulator.look_out_sir` gate ("Look Out, Sir" is NOT a real 10e rule — verified absent from Wahapedia core; its 12" clause is Lone Operative's; look_out_sir citation marked deprecated). `SWEG_SECONDARY_PER_UNIT`: Assassination counts one score per destroyed CHARACTER UNIT (squad_id group) not per model — fixes the 1.83x AM over-count (5-model Cadian Command Squad scored 5x); mirrors the existing No Prisoners/Cull per-squad handling; Bring It Down is also per-unit in CA-2025 (pending, near-zero impact). New: `code/attachment.py`, `LeaderAbility.attach_keys` (decouples attach target from the aura host_keys gate — for army-wide-aura leaders that still attach: Hive Tyrant, Abaddon, Syll'esske). Cited `simulator.leader_attachment` (Precision-verbatim backed). Combined quick A/B N=20: AM worst matchups ~5%->~15% (per-unit counting is the larger lever). NEXT PHASE (turnkey, see docs/LEADER_ATTACHMENT.md): (1) refactor `lookup_ability` to disambiguate cross-faction identical names (Sorcerer x3, Master of Executions x2 — currently mis-route) + pin SM Captain/Chaplain/Librarian variants (all wrongly inherit `_MARINE_HOSTS`); (2) apply the all-22-faction BSData-validated gap-fill (host_keys/attach_keys for every missing Leader) + fix the bug register (Typhus->wrong hosts, Ynnari->Drukhari routing, Hive Tyrant/Swarmlord absent, Mephiston Lone-Op mis-match, Guilliman/Cawl likely-fabricated hosts); (3) fold Bring It Down per-unit; (4) N=80 re-anchor screen then flip defaults + delete Look Out Sir. Necron multi-leader confirmed (Cryptek + Overlord co-attach; Cryptothralls is NOT an attach target). Imperial Knights correctly need no entries (Lone Op + Titanic). Full dataset in the four gap-fill agent transcripts + `docs/LEADER_ATTACHMENT.md`.

- LEADER-HOSTED LIST FIX (2026-07-12, BUILT + gated + byte-identical off) — the missing key that UNLOCKS attachment. Measured: the AM archetype under-fields host squads (leaders = ~100% guaranteed spine, their bodyguard infantry = 20-40% lottery tail; char:host-squad ratio 2.34:1) so most leaders are orphaned and attachment can't protect them. `SWEG_LEADER_HOSTED` (`code/archetypes.py::_ensure_leader_hosts`): after the template lottery, guarantee each attaching Leader ceil(leaders/2) host squads (2-per-squad cap) — "no list takes a Leader it cannot put in a squad"; adds hosts to seed counts, `_random_fill` absorbs so points stay ~2000 (ratio 2.34->1.79). USES data/leader_attach_targets.json (372 leaders), so global. FULL-STACK AM screen N=20 (OFF/attach+count/FULL): mean 20.8->22.5->30.0 = **+9.2 over OFF, ~5x the attach+count-only +1.7** — Assassination craters break open (Astartes 5->30, Chaos Knights 10->35, Imperial Knights 0->25), World Eaters regression RECOVERS (35->45); new mirror Adepta Sororitas -20 (character-heavy opponent, its leaders now protected too). Three complementary gates: SWEG_LEADER_HOSTED (list) + SWEG_LEADER_ATTACH (protection) + SWEG_SECONDARY_PER_UNIT (counting). ADOPTION = owner N=80 anchor gated-MAE (global change). See docs/LEADER_ATTACHMENT.md.

- EXCHANGE-RATE FIT RESULT (2026-07-11, per the amended registration; tool `scripts/fit_exchange_rate.py`, data `data/_margins_sc62a_n40_log.json`, 18,480 games on faithful defaults) — **MEASURED β = 0.015248 victory points per enemy point destroyed** (pooled ordinary-least-squares slope of final victory-point margin on kill margin). The ASSERTED constant (`_VALUE_VP_PER_ROUND_REF × srr / _TRADE_POINTS_REF` = 0.114 at four rounds remaining) OVER-PRICED violence four-to-seven-fold — a 200-point kill was priced at ~23 victory points and actually buys ~3 — which is the quantified form of the kill-dominance that sank all three 2026-07-10 layer compositions. Sanity checks all pass: per-faction slopes near-universal (mean 0.0188, standard deviation 0.0023, range 0.0151-0.0241, n=1,680 each), win-rate-by-kill-margin deciles cleanly monotone (0.166 → 0.831), R² = 0.214 — kills explain only a fifth of scoring variance, so the sim's win condition is already objective-dominated and only the asserted decision prices believed otherwise. Per-faction INTERCEPTS are independent pole diagnostics: Astra Militarum −13.2 (loses even at even kills — the deficit is in the non-kill game), Drukhari +14.0 / Genestealer Cults +13.5 (score without killing), Imperial Knights −8.4 (kill without converting). WIRING (registered): all three job-layer channels re-quoted in one measured currency — `_trade_vp_per_wound` relevance becomes the flat measured β (the srr shape retires with the assertion; the pooled headline 0.015248 is the registered number, per-faction spread noted as sensitivity), and the SURVIVE channel's raw-points pricing converts through the same β so cross-channel comparison is consistent. RE-SCREEN registered: `SWEG_JOB_LAYER=1` N=40 vs sc62a on the layer branch. EXPECTATIONS: the HOLD share rises from ~2 percent to material double digits; the Adeptus Astartes and Astra Militarum craters close; the aggression inflation deflates. FALSIFIERS: unchanged from the proposal (any calibrated faction beyond noise, Astra Militarum decisively away, headline above 2.60). Owner's standing overnight instruction: keep iterating the layers within registered discipline, then open the sync pull request.

- ALLOCATION-AWARE THREAT FIELD (owner-funded 2026-07-11, the lane named by the saturation finding; owner's design rule, verbatim in spirit: an enemy with one eligible target sends everything at it, an enemy with several splits the RISK across them even though the realized fire may still concentrate) — PRE-REGISTERED before build. MECHANISM: each enemy E's projected output is divided across its ELIGIBLE targets (range, line of sight, charge reach — the melee gradient already carries reach probability) instead of counted in full against every cell: threat to me at candidate p from E = output_E × w_E(me@p), weights summing to 1 over E's eligible targets and degenerating to 1 when I am E's only eligible target (the owner's isolated-unit case reproduces today's summed field exactly). v1 WEIGHTS: attractiveness-proportional — w = ew(E→me@p) / (Σ over my other units of ew(E→t at current positions) + ew(E→me@p)) — the per-pair expected-wounds math the field already computes, so enemies are assumed to shoot where they do damage; no new constants. Gate `SWEG_THREAT_ALLOC` default-off, byte-identical off; when on, every consumer of `_threat_field_at` (threat-charge, staging, value contestability, job-layer kill discount and survive channel — all default-off) prices the allocated field. INSTRUMENT-BEFORE-BUILD (both ship as diag tools): (1) the measured allocation-concentration curve from instrumented battles (share of an army's realized output landing on its top target as a function of eligible-target count — the sim's adopted focus-fire makes realized fire sharper than uniform; this validates or refutes the ew-proportional form); (2) the CALIBRATION instrument — per unit per turn, field-predicted expected incoming versus realized incoming: the summed field should read far above 1 (the saturation, quantified), the allocated field must read near 1. SUBSTRATE FALSIFIER (before any consumer screen): the allocated field's mean predicted-to-realized bias must land within [2/3, 3/2] per probe faction while the summed field reads above 2 — if ew-proportional misses the band because realized concentration is sharper, ONE registered iteration may sharpen weights toward the MEASURED concentration curve (grounded, not tuned), then the lane stops for the owner. CONSUMER SCREENS on substrate pass (registered now, run in order): (a) `SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE` + allocated field vs sc62a — its 2026-07-10 falsifier (walked-into-it must not rise) re-tested on the un-saturated field; (b) `SWEG_JOB_LAYER` + allocated field — expectations: the universal-caution degeneracy lifts (KILL channel share recovers from ~0, the VALUE-fallback share falls from 71 percent), the knight craters close, walked-into-it falls; falsifiers unchanged from the proposal. Variance note recorded in advance: expectation-based threat under-protects against concentrated fire even when unbiased — a tail-risk term is registered only if walked-into-it says expectation under-protects.

- ALLOCATION-AWARE THREAT FIELD — SUBSTRATE MEASURED 2026-07-11 (builds 31ef032 instruments + 2aa4e8f field, on the layer branch), REGISTERED BAND MISSED, LANE PAUSED FOR THE OWNER WITH THE RESIDUAL MECHANISM ISOLATED: the calibration instrument quantifies the saturation — the SUMMED field over-predicts realized incoming by 6× to 301× raw (3-14× capped) across factions; the ALLOCATED field (attractiveness-proportional eligibility weights, per-enemy denominators cached per board state) collapses that to 1.1-7.9 raw / 1.1-4.9 capped — a massive de-saturation, but inside the registered [2/3, 3/2] band for only Imperial Knights (1.12/1.10) and Orks (capped 1.11). The registered single iteration (sharpen weights toward measured concentration) DOES NOT APPLY: the measured concentration curve came back FLATTER than ew-proportional (top-pick frequency 0.53 at two eligible targets vs the 0.85 weight), so weight shape is not the residual. THE RESIDUAL IS PARTICIPATION: 773 of ~1,240 enemy activations dealt zero damage — the field grants every enemy its full output every turn, while in reality enemies die before acting, sit out of range after their REAL movement, or are locked in melee. That is a per-enemy output-realization question needing its own registration (candidate structures: decompose the zero-damage causes via the instrument, then either a structural term — survival × reachability × not-locked — or a measured realization curve, out-of-sample validated). Also flagged by the builder: `_charge_field_post_denominator` (the threat-charge consumer) does NOT route through `_threat_field_at` and needs separate wiring if the allocated form should reach it. Substrate assets permanent: two instruments (`diag_fire_allocation`, `diag_threat_calibration`), the allocation machinery (byte-identical off, all three off-path digests unchanged; three hand-computed fixtures incl. the two-Berzerker re-price: isolated 1.296 vs supported 0.639), and the concentration curve. Fixture and digest evidence in the build report; performance +0.3 s/battle on the job path.

- THREAT-FIELD PARTICIPATION RATE (registered 2026-07-11 on the decomposition evidence, build 37458c2's instrument; the lane's second and FINAL registered structure — after it, the lane stops for the owner regardless): the zero-damage decomposition over living activated enemy slots reads damage 15.1% / whiffed 24.8% / melee-locked 3.0% / out-of-reach-after-real-move 30.0% / chose-not-to 27.0%. The structural product's honest live factors (not-locked, within-turn survival) span almost none of the gap; the dominant residual — ~57% of living slots routed to the objective game instead of output positions — is ONE behavioural quantity: attack propensity. REGISTERED FORM: the allocated field's per-enemy contribution × ρ, where **ρ = the MEASURED share of living enemy slots that ATTEMPT output (damage + whiffs = 0.399 on the fit battery)** — whiffs count as attempts (their failure is already priced by expected wounds; the exclusive classification guarantees no double-count). One flat measured constant, the exchange-rate precedent, behaviour-dependent caveat carried (fixed-point re-fit after any adopted piloting change). OUT-OF-SAMPLE FALSIFIER: ρ is fitted on the existing 8-battle battery and validated on a FRESH battery (different seeds, same protocol): the allocated×ρ field's mean predicted-to-realized capped ratio must land within the registered [2/3, 3/2] band per probe faction. HONEST PRE-STATED RISK: the bias varies by faction (Imperial Knights already calibrate at 1.10 — a flat ρ may OVER-correct them to ~0.44) — if out-of-sample fails on SHAPE (some factions under, some over), the single registered fallback is propensity conditioned on the enemy's own attractiveness-weighted eligibility (a measured curve from the same instrument, still grounded, not tuned), then STOP. Also in this build: wire `_charge_field_post_denominator` (the threat-charge consumer's own per-pair loop, flagged as not routing through `_threat_field_at`) to the allocated×ρ form under the same `SWEG_THREAT_ALLOC` gate, so consumer screen (a) is runnable. CONSUMER SCREENS unchanged from the lane registration, gated on the out-of-sample pass.

- FLAT PARTICIPATION RATE (build 18e7f0e) — FAILED out-of-sample ON SHAPE 2026-07-11, exactly as pre-stated (Imperial Knights over-corrected to 0.45 capped, Orks 0.63, while Tyranids-facing threat stayed over-read at 2.81; fresh battery 11/16 in band with five over-misses). THE REGISTERED FALLBACK IS FUNDED (orchestrator decision per the registration, the lane's last step before it stops regardless): per-faction flat propensities are UNSTABLE across batteries (Death Guard 0.250 → 0.124, Astra Militarum 0.331 → 0.187) but the OPPORTUNITY-CONDITIONED curve is stable (propensity by rising best-expected-wounds bucket: fit 0.003/0.393/0.525/0.510/0.614, fresh 0.009/0.335/0.498/0.446/0.634 — units with zero opportunity never attempt; propensity saturates near 0.6). FALLBACK FORM: ρ(E) = the measured step curve over E's own best-expected-wounds opportunity, fitted on the seed-base-0 battery ONLY, VALIDATED on a genuinely untouched THIRD battery (seed base 200 — bases 0 and 100 both informed this decision and are burned for validation purposes). Same [2/3, 3/2] capped band per probe faction. PASS → the registered consumer screens run (threat-charge, then job layer). FAIL → the lane STOPS with all evidence recorded and the owner decides. Also banked in 18e7f0e regardless: the threat-charge consumer (`_charge_field_post_denominator`) is now wired to the allocated form (its own off-path digest proven unchanged), and ρ costs nothing (per-battle time flat).

- OWNER OVERRIDE + THE COMPOSED STACK SCREEN (2026-07-11, owner decision "a" on the lane-close memo, with the owner's composition rule: "the layers need to be on together"): the substrate screen gate is OVERRIDDEN on the behavioural evidence (walked-into-it falls for every probe faction on both consumers — the program's original falsifier passing for the first time), and the registered decomposed screens are REPLACED by the composed decision cell: `SWEG_JOB_LAYER=1 SWEG_THREAT_ALLOC=1 SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE=1` — movement decided by the job channels on the calibrated field with the measured exchange rate, charges decided by the threat-projection consumer on the same field. `SWEG_TRADE_EVAL` deliberately stays off (rejected on its own registration; its one-ply exchange function is superseded by the kill channel's risk discount). N=40 vs sc62a from the layer branch head 435e38e. EXPECTATIONS: the aggressive cluster deflates (its realized-exposure rate halved in the instrument battery), the gunline craters recover, the knights stabilise. FALSIFIERS unchanged from the job-layer proposal: any calibrated faction beyond noise; Astra Militarum decisively away; headline above 2.60 blocks adoption. Decomposition arms run only if the composed cell's attribution demands them.

- THE DENIAL PROGRAM + CANARY LOOP (owner-directed 2026-07-11: "run a full investigation... build and test... focus on just Astra Militarum vs Death Guard as our canary... use the canary to iterate and tune, get it closer fast, then screen to confirm at the end") — REGISTERED. This is a SANCTIONED TUNING MODE: iteration is allowed inside the canary cell, and the honesty clause is explicit — tuning on one matchup can overfit it, so THE FULL SCREEN AT THE END IS THE HOLDOUT and nothing adopts without it. Constants remain measured-or-rule-derived throughout (the design spec inventories every asserted constant in the adjacent code so none are inherited silently). CANARY BASELINE (instrument validated against the anchor: 35.0 percent measured vs 36.2 anchor): Death Guard deep-strikes 8.3 units per game, 7.5 INSIDE Astra Militarum's deployment zone — despite 15.5 Astra Militarum units standing in that zone at round-1 end (presence without SPACING: squads are points, the more-than-nine-inch exclusion is verified enforced in `_pick_arrival_point` against all alive units, so denial requires deliberate spread); 0.65 home-marker losses per game with 69 percent never recontested; 6.3 sub-twenty-percent-expected-wounds charges per game with 37 percent of chargers dead by game end; shooting misallocation measured NEAR ZERO in aggregate (the board-read's artillery magnet was intermittent, not systemic — the shoot-value consumer is DEPRIORITISED to last on this measurement). BUILD ORDER (per the implementation specification): (1) `SWEG_JOB_DENY` — fourth job channel: deep-strike exclusion priced as fraction of the enemy's currently-legal landing cells removed × enemy reserve points pending × the measured exchange rate, plus marker denial via the mirrored control brackets; charge-lane blocking DEFERRED (no path geometry exists in the sim — honest scope cut); (2) `SWEG_HOLD_REACTIVE` — urgency term raising a COMMITTED holder's value under threat at the marker (today's contestability actively pushes holders away from threatened markers) plus forced assignment release on a controller flip; (3) `SWEG_SHOOT_VALUE` held in reserve. Suicide charges are addressed in the canary by COMPOSING the existing `SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE`+`SWEG_THREAT_ALLOC` consumers, not by new code. CANARY METRICS per iteration (the crank `scripts/diag_canary_am_dg.py` + the paired cell runner joining the anchor's 160 games at the exact pair-seed convention): Astra Militarum cell win rate (36.2 → toward parity), deployment-zone arrivals, never-recontested marker losses, suicide-charge count. All iterations logged here; the final full screen confirms or kills the package.

- CANARY LOOP ITERATION LOG (running; all cells are 160-game paired vs the anchor unless marked N=20 crank). BASELINE 36.2 percent Astra Militarum. ITERATION 1 (builds 954ffb4/2ce8c2e/59272aa): `SWEG_JOB_DENY` + `SWEG_HOLD_REACTIVE` built clean (byte-identical off, 16 fixtures); paired cells read stack 5.6 / stack+denial 4.4 — the denial package lands on a collapsed foundation. ITERATION 2 (decomposition cells): charge-only (`SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE+ALLOC`, legacy movement) 31.2 ≈ flat; job-movement-only 5.6 — JOB-LAYER MOVEMENT IS THE ENTIRE POISON in this cell; the collapse hid in the full composed screen because other cells compensated (Astra Militarum read flat globally while losing 96 percent to Death Guard — the canary's whole justification, demonstrated). ITERATION 3 (instrument + two fixes, f73b960/d525bdc): the instrument decomposed the turtle — (1) PRIMARY ROOT: frac-at-risk divides threat by the individual MODEL's health (=1 for Guard infantry), so any threat ≥ 1 expected wound saturates to certain death and zeroes every midfield marker for the whole infantry army from round 2 (contestability = 0 exactly), while the real question is SQUAD survival (10 models facing T=2.2 lose two and keep the marker) — the same durability-representation amplification as the knights finding, seen from the fragile end; (2) the value fallback piled all units onto one marker (no dedup) — FIXED (spread via assignment claims, verified); (3) tie-and-deny contest value was unpriced — FIXED (enemy-VP-denied × survival, fixture-pinned); (4) DENY reach was NOT indicted (identical to HOLD's). Post-fix crank: suicide charges halved (3.9 → 1.95), deep-strike exposure held at 0, win rate still 0/20 — the saturation root outranks everything downstream. ITERATIONS 4-7 (builds c1d31bc, bd05229, d62141a, b68324a+a240e95 — all crank-verified, all digest-clean): (4) squad-aggregate survival pricing landed — the centre marker prices 12.8 where it read 0 — but routing gated it away from 95 percent of decisions; (5) the marker option became a PEER in the unassigned argmax with spread claims — routing works end-to-end, the turtle broke (23 → 18.8 in-zone), and the line was slaughtered walking open ground at a melee army (alive decisions 125→77, Death Guard charges +5 per game); (6) transit priced — arrival-delay discount (scoring rounds lost to walking) plus survival compounded across waypoint threat — and the CASTLE-THEN-CONTEST-LATE timing shape EMERGED from pure arithmetic (value share 57 percent in the cold round-1 field, 5 percent in the hot round 2, recovering round 3) — the first faction-authentic temporal behaviour the layer program has produced without a posture flag; (7) enemy advance priced at waypoints (reach expansion by t × their move, no pathing guess) plus the VICTORY-POINT LEDGER instrument. THE ESCALATION RULE FIRED: win rate 0/20 with movement pricing now fully honest, and the ledger shows the deterministic race — Astra Militarum holds 1.2 markers per scoring round vs Death Guard's 3.0 (it cannot even hold its own two: ~1 home-marker loss per game, 80-94 percent never retaken), primary compounds 48-vs-22, and A THIRD OF ITS SHOOTING GOES INTO POXWALKERS — damage-optimal, zero marker leverage, actively steered by the adopted `_screen_target_bonus`. CORRECTION to the iteration-0 measurement: shooting misallocation was measured near-zero in FORFEITED DAMAGE, which was the wrong metric — the forfeiture is in VICTORY-POINT RELEVANCE. Movement iteration STOPPED per the rule; the loop pivots to the two indicted axes: ITERATION 8 (dispatched) = the reserved `SWEG_SHOOT_VALUE` consumer (target argmax = kill value + marker-eviction relevance + threat relevance, measured currency, both wiring branches); garrison depth queued behind it. Original iteration-4 registration follows for the record: squad-aggregate survival pricing across the whole job path (squad effective health as every frac-at-risk denominator; squad points at stake in the survive/fallback duals via the measured exchange; value_projection's own SWEG_VALUE_MOVE path untouched — job-path wrapper only). Pre-stated arithmetic: the 10-model Cadian squad at the centre, T=2.2, prices 25 × 0.78 ≈ 19.5 instead of 0 — the ~330-points-equivalent marker trade becomes visible to the argmax for the first time.

- CANARY LOOP ITERATIONS 8-9 + CLOSE (builds 0cac781, af7febc): (8) `SWEG_SHOOT_VALUE` built — shoot-target argmax = kill value + marker-eviction relevance + threat relevance, measured currency, zero constants, both wiring branches, independent of the job layer. On faithful movement it HALVED the cell's primary gap (26.5 → 13.25), compressed the marker split 1.2-vs-3.0 → 1.55-vs-2.24, pulled secondaries to parity, produced the program's best marker-defence and fewest suicide charges — and the paired 160 read 36.9 percent vs the 36.2 baseline: the scoring gains do NOT convert to wins in this cell. HONESTY NOTE recorded: head-to-head parity was always an ASSUMPTION (the ground truth is overall rates only); the cell appears near its arithmetic floor. (9) garrison depth — the pile-on cap's enemy side re-priced from current-enemy to PROJECTED contest (one-enemy-turn reach + full reserve pool where a legal arrival cell exists on the disc, self-regulating since the garrison's own bodies deny those cells): home losses 1.0 → 0.55 per game, recontests doubled, turtle guard held. Job-arm win rate unmoved (1/20). **THE LOOP CLOSES with its verdict: all play axes (movement, charging, shooting, garrisons) are now honest and instrumented, and the Astra Militarum-vs-Death Guard residual survives them all — the binding constraint is the kill/scoring arithmetic of the cell (Death Guard banks ~47 primary vs ~25 with optimal play on both sides), a Stage-1 fidelity/economy question for the owner, delivered with the cleanest evidence trail of the project.** THE HOLDOUT SCREEN (registered, running): `SWEG_SHOOT_VALUE=1` ALONE vs sc62a, N=40 full frame — the loop's standalone graduate. EXPECTATIONS: under-poles that lose marker races lift; over-poles holding markers with durable chaff deflate. RISK stated in advance: the replacement argmax bypasses the legacy rule-grounded target biases (Oath of Moment compounding, Prioritised Efficiency, Harbingers of Dread) when on — if Adeptus Astartes or Votann or Chaos Knights break beyond noise, that is the named suspect and the fix is folding those rule triggers into the priced score. FALSIFIERS: headline above 2.60 blocks adoption; calibrated factions beyond noise flagged. The job-layer stack (nine iterations of machinery) stays parked as research assets on the layer branch regardless.

- SHOOT-VALUE HOLDOUT SCREEN (2026-07-12, N=40 vs sc62a, the canary program's registered end) — ADOPTION BLOCKED: paired gated 2.60 → 2.76 (+0.16), raw 3.09/3.29. MIXED POLES, and the failure mechanism is legible: TOWARD real — Adeptus Astartes −2.46 (the pre-named Oath-bypass risk moved it the RIGHT way), Adeptus Mechanicus −2.56, Chaos Daemons +3.21 (toward its 52.6 target); AWAY — **World Eaters +6.13 (was ON target, now broken) and Chaos Knights +4.56 (deeper over)** — the durable melee factions PROFIT when their opponents shoot for eviction, because the threat-relevance term prices a target by its threat × the fraction removable THIS ACTIVATION: against a Disgustingly-Resilient monster that fraction is a few percent, so value-shooters stop chipping the closing monster entirely and it arrives intact — while real players chip it down over the horizon it threatens. THE NAMED FIX CANDIDATE (not built — the registered program end was reached): the threat-relevance term should carry the same multi-round horizon every other term uses (the target's threat is denied for the remaining rounds it would have projected, so chip damage prices proportionally against the full horizon, not one activation). Astra Militarum overall: exactly FLAT (+0.06) — its frame-wide pole was never a shooting-economy artifact, consistent with the canary's arithmetic-floor verdict. Death Guard exactly flat. OWNER DECISION: (a) one further registered cycle — horizon-fix the threat term, re-crank the canary, re-screen; (b) close the program here and park shoot-value HELD-faithful with the rest; (c) pivot to the durability-economy fidelity question the canary isolated (Death Guard banks 47-vs-25 primary under optimal play on both sides — arithmetic, not artificial intelligence).

- SHOOT-VALUE ITERATION 10 RE-SCREEN (2026-07-12, horizon-fixed threat term, build bbc67ab) — BLOCKED AT 2.67 (from 2.76; the bar is 2.60), AND THE PRE-REGISTERED FALSIFIER FAILED: World Eaters +5.97 (essentially unmoved from +6.13) and Chaos Knights +4.00 (from +4.56) — the chip-fire hypothesis EXPRESSED (the canary crank showed Mortarion entering the top-three shot table without displacing eviction) but was NOT their mechanism. The three toward-real movers PERSISTED (Adeptus Astartes −3.14, Adeptus Mechanicus −2.19, Chaos Daemons +3.04) and Death Guard/Astra Militarum stayed exactly flat. REVISED SUSPECT for the World Eaters/Chaos Knights inflation, recorded for the next investigator: the gate applies to every faction, and Chaos Knights is itself a shooting faction (War Dog guns) whose own value-priced targeting adds to an already-over-rewarded chassis, while World Eaters likely profits from opponents redirecting fire to evictions and away from whatever legacy priorities suppressed it — DISENTANGLING NEEDS A CANARY-STYLE CELL INSTRUMENT ON A WORLD EATERS MATCHUP (who shoots what, before/after), not another term guess. STATUS: `SWEG_SHOOT_VALUE` parks HELD-faithful at its best measured form (headline +0.07, three poles toward real, two away, the strongest behavioural instrument record of any consumer); the canary program's option-(a) cycle is complete. The owner's option (c) — the durability/scoring-economy fidelity audit the canary isolated (47-vs-25 primary under honest play) — stands as the registered next direction, with the World Eaters cell instrument as the cheap first step it shares with the shoot-value reopen.

- THE KILL-ARITHMETIC HAND-AUDIT (2026-07-12, option c, the canary program's terminal question) — **THE PER-ACTIVATION ARITHMETIC IS FAITHFUL.** Ten canonical Astra Militarum↔Death Guard pairings computed three ways (hand tabletop math with every real ability — Lethal Hits, Heavy, Blast, First Rank Fire Second Rank Fire; the sim's expected functions; N=200 realized resolutions): every one reconciles within Monte Carlo noise, both directions. Feel No Pain scope correct (the fabricated army-wide version was already caught in DURA-AUDIT-D4), invulnerable saves correct, Contagion single-application and correctly asymmetric. THE SCORING ECONOMY IS ALSO FAITHFUL AND RUNS THE OTHER WAY: Poxwalker objective control per point is HALF of Cadian Shock Troops' (0.15 vs 0.31), and the Death Guard deep-strike share is sourced and dual-capped correctly. The grip is the DURABILITY STACK working exactly as the real rules say: only ~58 percent of raw allocated damage on Feel-No-Pain bearers becomes health loss, Contagion suppresses attacker wound rates over an aura growing to 9 inches, and every Death Guard weapon carries correctly-implemented Lethal Hits (+16-33 percent over naive math). TWO REAL DEFECTS FOUND, both non-explanatory: (1) **the Rogal Dorn Battle Tank and Basilisk fire mutually-exclusive wargear simultaneously** (code/units.py:2091-2199 has mutex grouping only for alt-fire-mode suffixes, not wargear replacements; the Dorn realizes ~3.1× its legal main-gun output, ~14 extra expected wounds per activation) — a genuine fidelity bug that INFLATES Astra Militarum, so fixing it lowers the under-pole (fidelity-first candidate regardless); (2) Mortarion's flattened top-level profile has attacks=0 (inert in live play under SWEG_PERMODEL; catalogue hygiene). Also: the stale receive_damage docstring claiming Plague Marine Feel No Pain. TWIN FINDING from the World Eaters instrument (same day): `_screen_target_bonus` (strategy.py:693, UNCONDITIONAL and ungated) mislabels Khorne Berzerkers (2 wounds, objective control 2) as chaff-screens, so every opponent over-prioritises shooting them by 1.4× — the anchor's in-band World Eaters reading may be calibrated-by-bug, and the shoot-value consumer's "breakage" may be the removal of a compensating error. THE SYNTHESIS FOR THE OWNER: with play honest (ten canary iterations), arithmetic faithful (this audit), and economics faithful, the Astra Militarum-vs-Death Guard cell is what the sim's WORLD produces — and the largest deliberately-unfaithful world surface remaining is TERRAIN AND LINE OF SIGHT (flat ~46 percent cover tax, no true line-of-sight blocking, the parked issue-52/threat-proposal caveat; real boards let gunlines castle behind walls and force melee through lanes). Decisions open: (i) fix the wargear-mutex bug (cited, gated, fidelity-first); (ii) the screen-bonus tightening + World Eaters calibration re-examination; (iii) whether to open the terrain/line-of-sight program — major, sign-uncertain, a re-anchor event, and now the best-evidenced candidate for the durability over-reward's true home.

- WARGEAR-MUTEX LANE (owner-funded 2026-07-12, option i) — PRE-REGISTERED: the catalogue fires mutually-exclusive wargear replacements simultaneously (audit finding: the Rogal Dorn realizes ~3.1× its legal main-armament output; the Basilisk carries heavy bolter AND heavy flamer; the mutex grouping at code/units.py:2091-2199 understands alt-fire-mode suffixes only). LANE SHAPE: scope-audit first (how many catalogue units carry replacement-exclusive profiles as independent shooters — expect many, across factions), then the fix: mutex-aware loadout selection (one option per wargear slot, defaulting to the sourced-list choice where the archetype documentation names one, else the datasheet default), gate `SWEG_WARGEAR_MUTEX` default-off byte-identical-off, per-unit citations of the Wahapedia wargear-options text, N=40 screen. HONEST DIRECTION NOTE: this deflates every stacked-wargear unit including Astra Militarum's tanks — the under-pole gets WORSE — adopted on fidelity-first grounds if screened non-catastrophic, per the no-knobs ruling.

- ⛔ GROUND-TRUTH CORRECTION (2026-07-12) — "THE SIM HAS NO LINE-OF-SIGHT BLOCKING" IS FALSE AND HAS BEEN SINCE 2026-05-16. `Map.has_line_of_sight` (code/map.py:163-225, faithful to the cited Ruins/Woods rules) has filtered shooting candidates in `_do_shoot` (simulator.py:15374-15384) since commit 7a5ff80e; the phase-1 instrument measures **73 percent of in-range candidate shots denied by visibility on the production maps**, and a fully-denied gun does not fire. The contrary claim in docs/TERRAIN_REALISM_PROPOSAL.md, docs/THREAT_LAYER_PROPOSAL.md's caveat, and multiple ledger entries (including the 2026-07-11 composed-stack synthesis naming terrain "the largest deliberately-unfaithful surface") propagated a documentation error — the code was right, the docs were wrong. Terrain DENSITY is also faithful: the production layout blocks MORE than the sourced Pariah Nexus tournament standard (73 vs 64 percent denial). The flat cover tax is approximately right in magnitude (fires 48.9 percent vs an angle-aware 56.7). Do not build "add line of sight" — it exists.

- TERRAIN PROGRAM PHASE-1 VERDICT + PHASE 2 (2026-07-12, build 0b0af15: instrument `scripts/diag_los_blocked.py` + `docs/TERRAIN_LOS_SPEC.md`): the genuinely open items are SMALL and re-scoped by the spec: (a) **`SWEG_COVER_ANGLE`** — angle-aware Benefit of Cover via a new `cover_between(attacker, target)` (fixes the intervening-cover miss the instrument measured at 10.1 percent, the 2.3 percent false-cover grant, and the latent Woods-grants-no-cover bug), sized small, sign uncertain, registered as phase 2a; (b) **THREAT-FIELD LINE-OF-SIGHT CONSUMPTION** (phase 2b, the spec's one-geometry-both-consumers rule): the threat/offense fields price threat THROUGH walls that resolution would block — enemies behind Obscuring ruins project threat they cannot deliver — a candidate component of the allocated field's residual 1.5-2.4× over-prediction, testable directly with the existing calibration instrument; (c) THE DURABILITY HYPOTHESIS RE-POINTED: the geometry fragile armies need EXISTS — what is missing is the artificial intelligence HIDING behind it (the threat proposal's parked move-intent consumer 2), and with resolution-faithful line of sight plus a field that respects it, hide-behind-walls becomes priced play in the existing layer machinery. Phase-2a and 2b dispatched under this registration; the instrument's cover/blocked rates and the durability-wall poles are the falsifier set.

- TERRAIN PHASE 2B DELIVERED (build f3042f2 on the layer branch): line of sight folded into every allocated-field site (threat field, allocation denominators — numerator/denominator consistent so weights stay unbiased, job threat scan, offense term in reverse, and the charge-consumer's local per-pair form, which the digest matrix caught even though the spec text missed it; melee gradients deliberately never blocked — chargers route around walls). CALIBRATION RESULT: nearly every over-predictor moved toward the band (Necrons 2.18→1.62, Thousand Sons 2.54→1.99, Astra Militarum and T'au and Death Guard and Drukhari and Daemons into band on at least one battery; in-band counts 5→6 and 10→11 of 16) — the through-walls threat was real and is gone. THE RESIDUAL NAMED: the stubborn over-predictions are the SWARM DEFENDERS (Tyranids 3.55/2.10, Genestealer Cults barely moved) and the mechanism is DISTRIBUTION-VERSUS-CONCENTRATION — the smooth allocation share spreads each enemy's output across a horde's many eligible bodies while real fire concentrates to kill a few units outright; a concentration-aware allocation structure would be a NEW registration (owner decision — the participation lane's stop rule stands). Secondary cost signal recorded: two already-under rows pushed further under (Imperial Knights 0.44, Orks 0.63 on the fit battery — hidden-but-committed melee armies read less incoming than they take). Field cost +14 percent on the battery, carried by the engine's cached grid.

- THE 2026-07-12 ADOPTION BATCH (screens complete, batch re-anchor in progress): (1) `SWEG_STRAT_ONCE_PER_PHASE` — wash +0.01, World Eaters +1.61 toward real (screened 2026-07-10); (2) `SWEG_WARGEAR_MUTEX` (build 64430ba, ten Wahapedia-verified units) — dead wash +0.02, Astra Militarum's expected tank deflation flat −1.65, Thousand Sons +0.56 the only decisive mover; adopted fidelity-first (verbatim-cited illegal loadouts removed at zero headline cost; 129 further affected units documented for a follow-up lane); (3) `SWEG_COVER_ANGLE` + the Woods fix (build 47b2941) — **gated 2.60 → 2.47 (−0.13), frames agreeing at 2.46/2.47**, Drukhari +1.03 toward real, Death Guard −0.83 toward real, Custodes +1.58 the one watch-item (slightly past its band). SEQUENCE: cherry-pick both builds to the rolling branch, composed N=20 smoke (three gates on — cross-system interaction check), flip all three defaults in one commit, fresh N=80 anchor on the new frame (sc63a), state banner, checkpoint pull request.

- TERRAIN AND LINE-OF-SIGHT PROGRAM (owner-funded 2026-07-12, option iii) — OPENED: the largest deliberately-unfaithful world surface (flat ~46 percent positional cover tax, no true line-of-sight blocking, shots never blocked by walls; the parked issue-52 caveat from docs/THREAT_LAYER_PROPOSAL.md and docs/TERRAIN_REALISM_PROPOSAL.md), now the best-evidenced candidate for the durability over-reward's true home (with play honest and arithmetic faithful, the open board is what remains). PHASE 1 (registered, running): investigation + instrument — the sim's current terrain/map geometry and the rejected routing build's `code/sim/los.py` remnants; the real 10th-edition visibility and Benefit of Cover rules verbatim; the tournament-standard terrain layouts the Warp Friends data was actually played on (density, obscuring-ruin footprints); an instrument measuring what fraction of the sim's resolved shots WOULD be blocked under real visibility on current maps (the saturation-style quantification before any build); and the build specification (gate, resolution changes, map-layout upgrades if current density is unfaithful). MAJOR RE-ANCHOR EVENT, sign uncertain, stated in advance. Build phases follow the specification under their own registrations.

- THE COMPOSED STACK SCREEN (2026-07-11, the owner-overridden decision cell: `SWEG_JOB_LAYER+SWEG_THREAT_ALLOC+SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE`, N=40 vs sc62a) — FAILED all falsifiers, paired gated 2.60 → 5.51 / raw 9.76 A / 9.80 symmetrized, **AND PRODUCED THE PROGRAM'S TERMINAL FINDING**: a completely NEW signature — the durable melee factions now DOMINATE (Imperial Knights +16.02 to a 75.3 win rate, Chaos Knights +11.99, World Eaters +10.95, Death Guard +3.77) while Adeptus Astartes craters again (−15.84) and Astra Militarum sits flat. Compare the risk-consistent cell on the SATURATED field, where the same knights CRATERED (Chaos Knights −12.01): un-saturating the threat field swung the knights ~25-28 points, because honest threat pricing tells them the truth — almost nothing on the board can efficiently hurt them — so they commit correctly and become unstoppable, while honestly-priced fragile armies correctly decline to trade into them and starve. **INTELLIGENCE AMPLIFIES THE DURABILITY OVER-REWARD.** The week's layers were never the disease: dumb play was MASKING the sim's underlying durability economy, and every increment of play quality re-exposes it (this is the owner's original 2026-07-08 hypothesis — "the durability representation is crap... we're mimicking real life, it must be something" — returned with mechanism attached). The real world holds knights to 47.7 percent with tools the sim's smartest AI still lacks or under-rewards: mission-starving them (score everywhere they are not — note the fit's intercept diagnostics: Astra Militarum loses at even kills, Drukhari/Genestealer Cults score +14 without killing), screening their charges, and alpha-damage allocation. THE LAYER PROGRAM IS CLOSED — six lanes, all registered, all stopped; the composed cell answers the owner's option (a) empirically. THE DECISION THE FINDING POSES (for the owner, no registration made): either the durability/damage economy is a Stage-1 rules-fidelity question after all (every audit said faithful — but the audits checked rules text, not the SCORING economy'S reward for survival), or the missing piece is mission-play tooling (the starve-the-knight pattern) — and the intercept diagnostics say the real factions that beat knights do it by out-scoring, not out-killing. Runtime note for any future stack work: the composed cell cost ~3.6× a baseline screen because units surviving longer means more activations to price — the per-round field caching remains unbuilt.

- OPPORTUNITY-CONDITIONED PROPENSITY CURVE (build 435e38e) — FAILED out-of-sample 2026-07-11, **THE ALLOCATION LANE IS CLOSED** per its registration (no further iterations by anyone): base-200 untouched battery read 10/16 factions in the [2/3, 3/2] band, six misses ALL over (max 2.38), ZERO under — the flat rate's shape pathology is fully cured (Imperial Knights 1.04 dead-centre, Orks 0.81) leaving a one-sided conservative over-read. STRUCTURAL CAUSE RECORDED (the builder's closing insight): the fitted curve's zero-opportunity bucket (ρ=0.003, a fifth of all measured slots) can NEVER apply at field-evaluation time — an enemy with any positive field contribution has positive opportunity by construction — so the effective curve range on projecting enemies (0.393-0.614) sits above the whole-population attempt rate; a selection effect, not noise. LANE LEDGER, closed with three grounded structures measured and two permanent instruments: the summed field over-reads realized incoming 6-300× raw (the saturation, quantified); allocation (owner's risk-splitting rule) cures the bulk of it and its two-Berzerker/isolated-unit fixtures behave exactly as designed; flat participation fails on shape as pre-stated; opportunity-conditioned participation cures shape and leaves a one-sided ~1.5-2.4× residual on six factions. Everything parked byte-identical-off on the layer branch (head 435e38e), all off-arm digests proven unchanged through five stacked builds. THE OWNER'S DECISION (memo to follow with fresh walked-into-it evidence): (a) accept the one-sided conservative residual as adequate for RELATIVE pricing and override the screen gate — the consumers compare cells and candidates, and a uniform-direction over-read partially cancels in comparisons; (b) fund a new lane on the selection-corrected curve (renormalize over the projecting-enemy population — a designable fix but a NEW registration); (c) park the layer program with its six attributed mechanisms and return to the mainline residual ladder (Astra Militarum ~14.6 remains half of all error).

- JOB LAYER + RISK-CONSISTENT KILL CHANNEL (build 919728f, the registered final overnight iteration) — FAILED 2026-07-11, OVERNIGHT BUDGET EXHAUSTED, THE LAYER PROGRAM PARKS WITH FIVE ATTRIBUTED MECHANISMS: N=40 vs sc62a read paired gated 2.60 → 3.86 / raw 7.95 A / 7.80 symmetrized — the best job-layer cell yet (4.65 → 4.27 → 4.23 → 3.86 across the four iterations) and still a decisive fail with a ROTATED signature: the durable knights now crater (Chaos Knights −12.01, Imperial Knights −5.05, Thousand Sons −7.72, Grey Knights −7.60) while the fast aggressors still inflate (Tyranids +13.45, Drukhari +10.89). THE BUILD'S OWN INSTRUMENTS EXPLAIN IT — **the threat field SATURATES**: it sums expected wounds from EVERY enemy as though all of them will target this one unit, so on a dense board nearly every cell prices lethal (frac-at-risk pins to 1), the survival discounts flatten all channel differences (KILL collapsed to ~0 for most factions; 71 percent of decisions routed through the value fallback), and the layer degenerates to universal caution — which the fast skirmishers exploit and the commit-dependent knights pay for. Positive signals recorded: walked-into-it FELL for two of three probe factions (T'au decisively, 0.227 → 0.167); end-of-game survivor counts rose sharply (units stopped suiciding); pile-on kept falling. THE NAMED NEXT DEFECT (registered as a lane, NOT run — owner decision): the threat field must become ALLOCATION-AWARE — each enemy's projected output divided across its plausible targets rather than counted in full against every cell simultaneously (the current form over-counts incoming threat by roughly the enemy-count factor, which is exactly the saturation observed). That one fix un-saturates every survival discount in the stack at once (threat-charge consumer, staging, value contestability, kill discount, survive channel) and is the highest-leverage single registration left in the layer program. THE PROGRAM'S LEDGER, five mechanisms each isolated by a falsified registration: (1) uniform utility cancels (mask grid); (2) zero-value units must approach through the objective game, not the enemy (v1 march); (3) the price of violence was asserted four-to-seven-fold high (measured β = 0.0152, now wired); (4) channels must price risk consistently or the argmax selects the risk-blind one; (5) the summed threat field over-counts incoming fire by the allocation factor. Everything is parked byte-identical-off on the layer branch (head 919728f) with both speed passes.

- JOB LAYER + MEASURED EXCHANGE RATE (wiring c34295d) — FAILED 2026-07-11: N=40 vs sc62a read paired gated 2.60 → 4.23 / raw 7.90 A / 7.69 symmetrized — statistically indistinguishable from the pre-reprice failure (4.27/8.10) with the same signature (Tyranids +12.24, Drukhari +8.37, Chaos Daemons +7.44, World Eaters +7.32 up; Adeptus Astartes −11.63, Astra Militarum −7.19 down). VERDICT ON THE EXCHANGE HYPOTHESIS: the measured rate is real and stays (it grounds the currency), but the kill-price LEVEL was not the binding defect — KILL and SURVIVE scale together, HOLD is assignment-capped, so the per-unit argmax still lands on KILL at any price level. THE ISOLATED DEFECT (the fourth screen's triangulation): the KILL channel is RISK-BLIND — it prices raw offense at the destination with no threat term — while HOLD carries a survival discount (contestability = 1 − frac-at-risk). An argmax across channels that price risk inconsistently systematically selects the risk-blind channel, so every kill-job unit stands in its maximum-offense cell regardless of exposure: the aggressive cluster inflates, the walked-into-it class persists, and the gunline factions that need caution crater. REGISTERED FINAL OVERNIGHT ITERATION (consistency fix, not a knob — the SAME discount HOLD already uses, applied to KILL): kill channel value at destination p becomes offense(p) × (1 − frac_at_risk(p)) with frac-at-risk from the threat field at p, identical form to `value_projection`'s contestability. EXPECTATIONS: the aggressive cluster deflates toward anchor; walked-into-it falls (the threat proposal's original falsifier, finally with the right consumer); Adeptus Astartes and Astra Militarum recover their craters. FALSIFIERS: unchanged (any calibrated faction beyond noise; Astra Militarum decisively away; headline above 2.60). If this fails, the overnight iteration budget is exhausted: record, write the night summary, open the owner-ordered sync pull request, and the layer program waits for the owner with four cleanly-attributed failure mechanisms on the ledger.

- JOB/COMMITMENT LAYER v1 CORRECTED (build f7a5cd4, the single permitted fix: zero-value routing falls back to the value field) — REJECTED-empirical, LANE STOPPED 2026-07-10 per the pre-registered stop rule: N=40 vs sc62a read paired gated 2.60 → 4.27 / raw 8.10 A / 8.27 symmetrized. The correction worked as specified (Adeptus Astartes' crater halved −14.92 → −9.52; the marchers now flow through the objective game — Tyranids 66 percent routed-to-value) but the STRUCTURAL signature is unchanged: aggression-rewarded factions inflate (Tyranids +11.90, Drukhari +9.08, Necrons +9.06, World Eaters +8.75, Genestealer Cults +7.09, Votann +7.55), combined-arms factions crater (Adeptus Astartes −9.52, Astra Militarum −6.51 — WORSE than v1, Chaos Knights −5.79, Custodes −3.34). DIAGNOSIS FOR THE RECORD, the through-line of the whole 2026-07-10 program: the KILL channel out-prices HOLD for nearly every unit (the build's own flagged property), so argmax specialisation collapses to kill-routing — and this is the THIRD failure signature in one night that traces to the same constant: value-alone breaks kill-tempo factions (kill under-priced at zero), value+offense cancels (kill re-priced at parity per-unit), job argmax kill-dominates (kill over-priced vs markers). Every composition inherits `_trade_vp_per_wound` — the wounds-to-victory-points exchange rate — and that constant has NEVER been independently calibrated against anything. It is now the named, single deepest open question of the layer program: until the exchange rate is grounded (e.g. fit from real game evidence about how much killing actually buys winning, or derived from the sim's own win-condition arithmetic rather than asserted), every layer composition will fail in a direction set by its error. The layer machinery itself (fields, channels, assignment, persistence, instruments) is validated and parked on the worktree branch. OWNER DECISION STANDS as the mask-grid synthesis stated, now with the job lane closed: (a) adopt the five-faction mask stopgap (1.95/1.79, the only sub-target configuration), (b) hold at sc62a 2.60 + the stratagem once-per-phase flip, (c) fund the exchange-rate grounding as the next registered lane before any further layer composition.

- JOB/COMMITMENT LAYER v1 (`SWEG_JOB_LAYER`, build cd46a3d on the layer-stack branch; docs/JOB_LAYER_PROPOSAL.md) — MEASURED 2026-07-10, ALL FALSIFIERS FIRED, the worst read of the program and the most LEGIBLE: N=40 vs sc62a read paired gated 2.60 → 4.65 / raw frame 8.74 A / 8.29 symmetrized, fourteen decisive movers. The pattern is one failure, not fourteen: every aggression-rewarded faction inflated (Tyranids +12.06, World Eaters +10.82, Drukhari +10.65, Necrons +10.13, Votann +7.12) and every combined-arms defensive faction cratered — ADEPTUS ASTARTES −14.92, the adopted ranged-hold lever (+14.03 at adoption) INVERTED, plus Astra Militarum −4.69, Custodes −2.96, Chaos Knights −4.61. CAUSE (flagged in the build report as a deliberate deviation): when the KILL channel prices zero (nothing killable this activation), v1 marches the unit toward the NEAREST ENEMY — so every out-of-range gunline abandons its line and advances, reproducing the exact pre-ranged-hold misplay at global scope; with HOLD under 3 percent (kill out-prices one marker, the build's own flagged pricing note) and the museum bypassed, the whole game became a brawl. Mechanism instruments were healthy where the channels actually priced (Astra Militarum job table split as predicted, pile-on fell, Mortarion case proven) — the specialisation architecture is not refuted, the zero-value default is. THE ONE PRE-REGISTERED CORRECTION (per the proposal's stop rule, this is the single permitted fix, then the lane stops): a unit whose KILL channel prices ≤ 0 falls back to the VALUE FIELD's destination (marker-oriented approach — the existing `SWEG_VALUE_MOVE` consumer arithmetic, which is measured sane and closed the durable trio), never a march at the nearest enemy; approach happens through the objective game, as real players approach. EXPECTATION for the corrected screen: the gunline craters recover (Adeptus Astartes back toward anchor), the aggressive inflation deflates, the trio's derived-holder closure gets its first fair test. FALSIFIERS: unchanged from the proposal. If the corrected v1 fails: LANE STOPS, owner decides between the mask stopgap and a redesign.

- VALUE-FIELD OFFENSE TERM iteration 2 (best-single-target reprice, build af3cea8) — HELD-faithful, LANE STOPPED 2026-07-10 per the pre-stated rule (two pricings measured, no third): N=40 global value+offense vs sc62a read paired gated 2.60 → 2.64 (flat) but raw frame 3.26 A / 3.40 symmetrized (decisively worse than the anchor's 2.60/2.42), and Grey Knights broke beyond its noise band away from real (43.6 → 40.4 vs real 46.7). THE REPRICE ITSELF WORKED — the multi-target inflation collapsed exactly as pre-registered (Imperial Knights +5.30 → +0.14, Necrons +3.90 → 0.00, World Eaters +3.92 → +1.49 flat) — but it exposed the structural result: AT GLOBAL SCOPE THE TWO TERMS SUBSTANTIALLY CANCEL. The offense term hands the durable trio their kill-play back, erasing the value field's trio-pole closure (Death Guard/Imperial Knights/Chaos Knights all FLAT vs anchor instead of closing −4 to −6), while the kill-tempo five stay rescued and small new drifts (Grey Knights −3.23, Genestealer Cults −3.36) tip the frame worse. Positive side-signals for the record: Orks +3.44 lands almost exactly ON real (45.3) and Chaos Daemons +3.16 moves decisively toward real — the offense term correctly prices horde/monster positional aggression. STANDING RESULT OF THE WHOLE VALUE PROGRAM: mask v1 five-faction (1.95/1.79) remains the ONLY sub-target configuration; the offense term (sound build, byte-identical off, faithful conditioning) parks HELD-faithful beside threat-charge. REOPEN only by new registration — the untested composition is five-mask + offense (cell 2 of the original registration, superseded by this stop) and mask + offense + threat-charge; the owner may fund either explicitly.

- THREAT-PROJECTION LAYER consumer 1, charge-target scoring (`SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE`) — HELD-faithful 2026-07-10 (screened + falsifier-read, NOT adopted): N=20 scoped-paired vs sc62a read gated 2.60 → 2.55 (−0.05, wash inside the ±0.2 band; Necrons UP +1.30 the only decisive mover; nothing broken, wall poles flat).
