But the layer's pre-registered falsifier — the walked-into-it rate "must REDUCE" — came back a wash-to-fail at 18 games/faction: World Eaters 0.231 → 0.232 (flat), T'au 0.204 → 0.194 (down, good), Chaos Daemons 0.194 → 0.204 (UP — the same magnitude the trade-eval iteration treated as a fail, applied consistently here). Mechanism remains real and validated (11.3% charge-target re-picks; the two-Berzerker supported-target blindness fixed), and the honest structural note stands: a charge-target chooser alone touches too few activations to move army-wide exposure — the walked-into-it class lives in the MOVE/commit decisions (consumers 2/3, unbuilt). REOPEN: (1) the value-field composition (SWEG_VALUE_MOVE + SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE, the threat-coupling path the value entry names), or (2) consumer 2 (move-intent destinations) where the falsifier has a fair shot. Do not adopt consumer 1 alone on a failed own-falsifier — that would make pre-registration decorative.

- STRATAGEM ONCE-PER-PHASE (`SWEG_STRAT_ONCE_PER_PHASE`, the 10e core restriction "you cannot use the same Stratagem more than once in the same phase", reviewer-spotted by Ed watching a sample game) — SCREENED 2026-07-10, ADOPT-candidate awaiting the batch re-anchor: N=20 scoped-paired vs sc62a read gated 2.60 → 2.61 (+0.01, dead wash) with World Eaters — a wall under-pole — DECISIVELY UP +1.61 toward real (45.2 → 46.8); no faction decisively worse. This is a rules-fidelity fix with a verbatim citation (`simulator.stratagem_once_per_phase`): the sim was provably violating the rule (Command Re-Roll firing up to 6× per round; legal max 1 per phase instance). Fidelity-first + wash + a wall pole moving toward real = flip default-on at the next re-anchor batch. Committed default-off at 8e72196.

- TRADE EVALUATOR iteration 2 (both pre-specified reply re-pricings) — REFUTED-hypothesis, LANE STOPPED 2026-07-10 per the pre-stated stop rule (two candidates, falsifier-gated, no third pricing invented): candidate (a) drop the relevance discount only (reply = raw points-per-wound, ~8.75× heavier at survival-ratio 4) FAILED the walked-into-it gate on Death Guard (+0.006) and World Eaters (+0.012); candidate (b) expected-remaining-value-at-stake (death probability × full points cost, the value field's own frac-at-risk form) FAILED on Astra Militarum (+0.026) and World Eaters (+0.030). Structural diagnosis from the paired value-realisation reads: under any heavier reply, value-realisation CRASHES for all three probe factions while walked-into-it does not fall — the move consumer only chooses BETWEEN markers, and in a contested midfield every candidate is exposed, so a heavier reply scrambles the value ranking without routing anyone out of danger. The walked-into-it class lives in the COMMIT-AT-ALL decision (deliberately never frozen, per the blanket-block rejections) and in the CHARGE consumer — the move-consumer exchange reply is structurally the wrong lever for realized exposure. REOPEN: only via the charge-consumer composition or a commitment/job layer, not by a third reply pricing. No screens run (falsifier gated them; instrument batteries at 18 games/faction — small-N noted, criterion pre-stated). Candidate (b) ships as the held default-off form on the trade-eval branch (head b16bf5a; sanity 1e-9, byte-identical off, mechanism 15.1% re-route).

- VALUE FIELD + move consumer (`SWEG_VALUE_MOVE`) — HELD-foundation 2026-07-09, THE PIVOTAL MEASUREMENT OF THE LAYER PROGRAM: at N=40 vs sc61a ALL FIVE durability-wall poles moved DECISIVELY toward real simultaneously (Astra Militarum +6.58 — the first lever ever to lift it; Death Guard −7.32; Imperial Knights −7.47; Chaos Knights −9.17; World Eaters −5.74) — the both-sides signature no prior lever produced, empirically confirming the wall is a HORIZON ARTIFACT. Headline +0.11 (HOLD, not adopt): v1's blunt pricing decisively breaks five calibrated factions (Emperor's Children −13.07, Chaos Space Marines −7.87, Custodes/Votann/Drukhari ~+7.3 away). REOPEN = the researched stack (trade evaluator + threat-coupling refine the commit pricing); work on the value worktree branch; do NOT per-faction-tune the field (knob).

- Terrain collision, ROUTING shape — REJECTED-empirical 2026-07-09 (second and final collision shape): the route-around rebuild fixed stranding completely (392→0, all mechanism checks green) yet screened **gated 3.71 → 8.30 (+4.59)** — Imperial Knights −35.9 / Chaos Knights −26.6 / World Eaters −25.4 DECISIVE (overshooting far UNDER their real rates) while every gunline inflated (+7 to +15). MEASURED conclusion: movement-blocking terrain without terrain-aware deployment and approach planning is a pure detour tax on ground forces — real players plan around ruins, the sim's AI cannot yet. Work preserved on the worktree branch; REOPEN only after the value/threat layer stack provides deployment-and-approach planning (or the terrain density is verified against real competitive layouts).

- `SWEG_CHARGE_TRADE` (upstream charge-trade veto, pull request 87) — REJECTED-empirical, flipped default-OFF 2026-07-09 (owner decision after the audit + kill-screen). MEASURED (full-frame N=20 vs sc61a, veto killed): gated 3.71 → 3.19 (−0.52), World Eaters −11.98 DECISIVE (lands on real 44.9 — that pole was entirely this lever), Chaos Knights −5.02 DECISIVE toward real, Death Guard/Imperial Knights/Emperor's Children all directionally toward real. It was the seventh instance of the settled faction-neutral family, shipped by ruling without a screen; re-anchor to sc62a queued. ## THE NIGHT-BUILD WAVE (2026-07-09) — six substrate builds integrated; verdicts on sc61a
All seven night commits cherry-picked conflict-free, collective off-check 0-flip, pushed. MEASURED verdicts (N=20 full-frame unless noted): `SWEG_TERRAIN_COLLISION` **REJECTED-empirical** (gated +2.24, Imperial Knights −31.6 / Chaos Knights −23.6 DECISIVE — wall-clamping without route-around pathfinding strands big non-INFANTRY units behind ruins; REOPEN only with a routing implementation); `SWEG_THREAT_CHARGE` HELD (−0.05 flat, genuine redistribution, falsifier fires 2/3 factions — the field works, the metric doesn't pay it yet; consumers 2-3 + value-field composition are the reopen path); `SWEG_PRIMARY_DECK_FULL` HELD-fidelity (near-flat; measured primary VP 34.6→34.9 — the primary over-score is NOT mission-card modelling); `SWEG_TRANSPORT_PLAY` HELD (clean wash on both scoped factions); `SWEG_TAC_TRACK_LIVE` HELD (settled-family confirmation). Health fix dd0bf01 merged as faithful data — INERT under --use-archetype (Great Unclean One fielded 0/500; matters for Stage-2/random-fill). ## TEMPLATE-FEASIBILITY WAVE (2026-07-08, the pilot sweep's build-out) — verdicts
The realization-lottery finding resolved into TEMPLATE INFEASIBILITY: the Astra Militarum and Emperor's Children templates are cost-infeasible unions (~2,080 / ~2,920 points at competitive sizes vs the 2,000 budget) so the sim fields degraded random subsets; Genestealer Cults' fits and realizes 100% (the healthy model). MEASURED verdicts: draw-mechanism fixes `SWEG_TEMPLATE_REALIZE` shape 1 (seed-side breadth, AM −2.78* / EC −3.74*) and shape 2 (fill-side at normal sizing, AM flat / EC −6.36*) both REJECTED-empirical — realization mechanics either shrink squads or homogenize builds; commits 04d2a9f/0cb8f75 held default-off. Template-side re-derivations: `SWEG_ORKS_LIST2` (Snikrot+Kommandos+Zodgrod, sourced, 1,920pt feasible) **ADOPTED default-on (anchor sc60a): N=80 +5.03 DECISIVE, Orks 37.6 → 42.6, gated 2.85 → 2.61, Orks pole CLOSED (gated 0.00), reproduction 0-flip**; `SWEG_AM_2K` (Taurox trim to fit) −2.34 DECISIVE wrong-sign REJECTED (the trim cut a load-bearing transport — AM remains open); `SWEG_EC_LIST3` (exact Kyle Sams 1,865pt list) inconclusive at N=40, HELD. Also: the EC-scoped `SWEG_MELEE_HOLD_OBJECTIVE` screen was FLAT (−0.19, 19 flips) — the board-read abandonment misplay is real but too rare to be a rate driver; lever stays held.

FILTER AUDIT (`scripts/diag_astartes_filter.py`): the shared rDPA≥2.0 & range≥18" filter holds 73 units, every one a fire platform (Repulsor, Predator, Vindicator, Gladiator/Ballistus/Redemptor, Land Raiders, Whirlwind, Desolation, Devastator, Eradicator, Eliminator, Suppressor) or a ranged CHARACTER (Captain, Lieutenant, Librarian, Techmarine — the analog of the Thousand Sons psyker / Sororitas Morvenn Vahl holds), and holds ZERO core bolter or assault infantry (Intercessors rDPA 1.67 below the floor, Tacticals 1.95, Assault Intercessors 0.73, Scouts, plus every [ASSAULT] unit — Hellblasters, Inceptors, Heavy Intercessors, Terminator Assault, Vanguard, Bladeguard stay free) — so the objective-grabbing infantry core is untouched and NO infantry narrowing is needed. **N=20 Adeptus-Astartes-scoped vs sc52a: Adeptus Astartes +11.24 DECISIVE UP (40.9 → 52.1, CI 5.70, 138 flips — highly active); correct direction (closes the −5.6 under-pole vs real 47.6) with a MODEST +4.5 overshoot.** This is the Chaos Knights (+11.27, ~+5.6 over) / Votann (+10.37, ~+5.3 over) FIDELITY-FIRST profile, NOT the Tyranids massive-overshoot: the faction's OWN absolute error IMPROVES slightly (5.6-under → 4.5-over) because far fewer durable bricks qualify (Hellblasters [ASSAULT]-excluded), so the overshoot is the sim's structural durability-over-reward amplifying the held-alive fire platforms — the same class that let CK / Votann bank a small frame mean-absolute-error at N=80 via opponent over-poles deflating toward real. The N=20 opponent rows (Drukhari −50, Chaos Space Marines −40, Death Guard −35 …) are the asymmetric-scoped-eval artefact (2-10 flips, CI 13-31 each, gate changes only Marine moves), NOT readable collateral; the `gated MAE +2.07` is likewise the noisy scoped-record artefact the Sororitas / Tyranids screens discounted. RECOMMENDATION: promising, precedent-backed, correct-direction, own-error-improving — ADVANCE to the N=80 Adeptus-Astartes-scoped adoption screen (the deciding measurement; not run here per the ≤N=20 scoped-probe instruction), fidelity-first ADOPT lean matching CK / Votann. Two coupled pieces behind one faction-neutral gate: (1) charge coordination + wrap placement (`Battle._do_charge` / `_cage_charge_target` / `_cage_charge_end`) redirects already-committed melee chargers onto a durable brick (Toughness ≥ 10 or ≥ 15 Wounds) within reach of ≥ 2 melee-capable friendlies, landing each on the side farthest from existing cage members; (2) fall-back block (`pick_move_intent`) makes a caged brick HOLD per the verbatim 10e surround rule, so it stays engaged shooting only the cage at the Big Guns Never Tire −1. In the sim's honest economy a brick is a NO-WIN INTERACTION NODE — both ignoring it (uptime channel) and engaging it (sunk output) lose — whereas the real 44.7-48.5 percent Knight rates say real bricks are roughly break-even to interact with. The residual on the durable over-poles (Death Guard +15.4, Imperial Knights +14.1, Chaos Knights +13.9) is the damage-in versus points-out substrate of durable platforms, not any piloting behaviour. The durability-in-contest structural remodel is now an OWNER DECISION with a complete evidence file. Do NOT build further behavioural anti-brick levers.

The structural remodel is therefore a FIDELITY AUDIT program, never a design program: no durability discount factors, no tuned save or damage rates, no dial of any kind (this extends the existing forbidden tune-to-win-rate doctrine from the Battle-shock/melee-lethality declaration). VERDICT: the secondary economy is FAITHFUL end-to-end, sc52a stands as-is, do NOT touch the deck/shedding/actions. **THE UNIFYING FINDING: both the durable OVER-poles (Death Guard +22.5, Imperial Knights +15.7, Chaos Knights +11.1) and the horde UNDER-poles (Orks −8.7, Genestealer Cults −7.0) now trace to the SAME root — the PRIMARY / KILL economy over-paying survivor uptime** (durables survive and bank objective control every Command phase; hordes are ground down and lose late-game control). This is audit D's survivor-snapshot over-reward, now confirmed from BOTH poles. It has ALREADY passed the damage-math audit (1,233 sequences faithful) and the points-out audit (divergences fixed) and the scoring-economy audit (secondary faithful) — so the remaining suspects are NARROW: army-composition/points fidelity (do the sim's lists match real tournament lists in durability-per-point) or the Stage-1/Stage-2 boundary (faithful rules + real lists genuinely producing this equilibrium, which is what the points equation exists to price). This is the owner-decision fork — it may be the edge of Stage 1.
- THE ARMY-LIST FIDELITY AUDIT — CLOSED, THE LISTS ARE FAITHFUL, THE AXIS IS MODEL-COUNT NOT DURABILITY (2026-07-03, night; owner-chosen surface; two audits: docs/_LIST_AUDIT_DEATHGUARD.md vertical + docs/_LIST_AUDIT_FILL_MECHANISM.md horizontal, both read-only, winner-reproduction 100 percent). BOTH CONVERGE ON A NEGATIVE: army-list fidelity is NOT the answer for the durable over-poles. **Horizontal (all 22 factions, 45 builds each):** durable-brick point share does NOT track the pole (r = +0.23, not significant, non-monotone); the two falsifiers are decisive — Astra Militarum is the biggest UNDER-pole (−12.4) yet carries the highest non-Knight durable-brick share (0.50, Toughness-12 tanks), and Aeldari is a +10.5 OVER-pole with ZERO durable bricks (all Toughness-3 Aspect Warriors). The real correlate is **wounds-per-point at r = −0.49 NEGATIVE — the over-poles are LOW-MODEL ELITE armies, the under-poles are HIGH-body HORDES**; the reward axis is model-count concentration, not durability. The fill introduces no durability skew beyond the curated seed (`SWEG_FILL_TEMPLATE_POOL` draws from the template pool, and where it deviates it skews toward CHEAPER bodies — the opposite of the over-durability prior). **Vertical (Death Guard, the +22.5 top pole):** the sim fields a durability-per-point faithful list — marginally BELOW real (wounds-per-point −7.0 percent vs the sourced Virulent Vectorium list, −5 percent vs the Tacoma-winner vehicle swarm), only 1.2 percent off-template models (all cheap support characters, ZERO durable phantoms), and in reproduced wins every surviving objective-banking pillar is a real-list unit. There is no over-durable unit to remove; the only surfaced list tweak (dropping Mortarion's 100 percent force-seed) would push the win rate UP toward the real swarm, a fidelity fix not a lever. A PRICING SIGNAL fell out: the real Tacoma list costs 2145 SIMULATOR points — it does not fit 2000 because the sim already prices durable hulls ABOVE GW. **THE UNIFIED VERDICT:** representation faithful, damage faithful, secondary economy faithful, AND the army lists faithful — the residual poles are the MODEL-COUNT / survivor-snapshot primary-scoring economy (low-model elite over-rewarded, high-body horde under-rewarded), which every Stage-1 fidelity audit has now cleared. This is the STAGE-1 FLOOR / STAGE-2 PRICING BOUNDARY: with rules and lists both faithful, the remaining elite-over-horde equilibrium gap is exactly what the points equation exists to price (Goal C), unless a ground-truth-anchored scoring-representation divergence can be named (the attrition-rate / objective-resolution subtlety — but with no real per-game casualty-rate ground truth to anchor it, tuning it would be a forbidden tune-to-win-rate dial, the retracted-floor lesson). OWNER FORK: declare the Stage-1 floor and begin Stage-2 pricing, or name a ground-truth-anchored scoring-representation hypothesis to keep chasing in Stage 1.

- PRIMARY OVER-SCORE — DIAGNOSED FAITHFUL EQUILIBRIUM, NO STAGE-1 LEVER (2026-07-04; docs/_PRIMARY_OVERSCORE_DIAG.md, cherry-picked ccfbc5a). The 35 is partly a diag artifact (diag runs all Take-and-Hold; under the real production mission-deck mix the anchor uses, primary is **33.7**, not 35); the residual to ~29 is the survivor-snapshot UPTIME equilibrium — the sim plays five full rounds with a FLAT round-2-to-5 primary trajectory and NO round-5 attrition decay, whereas real games attrit/concede/time-out and bias real primary DOWN. **KEY REFRAME: sim TOTAL VP ≈49.2 is slightly UNDER real 51.7 — the over-score is a primary/secondary MIX shift (primary high, secondary low), NOT total inflation.** So primary is at the faithful floor and the real remaining headroom is the SECONDARY channel (14.2 vs 22.7, still ~8 short) — where the challenger-card mechanic and the deck-completeness work aim. FIDELITY-COMPLETENESS ITEM that falls out (not a metric lever, faction-neutral, would not close the durable over-pole): 5 of the 10 deck missions (Linchpin, Burden of Trust, Unexploded Ordnance, Hidden Supplies, Supply Drop) fall back to Take-and-Hold scoring instead of their real rules. - CHALLENGER-CARD (Gambit) CATCH-UP — RECONCILED: ALREADY WIRED + ADOPTED DEFAULT-ON (wave 252), NOT AN ORPHAN; RULE CONFIRMED BUT THE COMPETITIVE REFERENCE EXCLUDES IT; SCREENS METRIC-NEGATIVE → RECOMMEND REVERT TO DEFAULT-OFF + RE-ANCHOR (2026-07-04, Fable). **RECONCILIATION:** the `SWEG_CHALLENGER_CARDS` mechanic is FULLY wired at base — run-loop `_decide_challenger_draw` (round-start eligibility, deterministic crc draw) + `_score_challenger_card` (scored through the Tactical achievement scorer `_score_one_card`), a third VP track in `_capped_vp_pair`/`_decide_winner` — gate DEFAULT-ON since wave 252 (commit bc9159d) and FIRING in production; it is NOT an orphan citation. `scripts/diag_signatures` #8's 'NOT MODELLED' and the 'NOT MODELLED AT ALL' phrase in the SCORING-SIGNATURE RE-OPEN entry above were STALE hardcoded text that ignored the wave-252 build — both corrected (diag #8 now MEASURES the trigger rate; the citation completed with verbatim rule text). **ANCHOR INTEGRITY:** byte-identical-off does NOT hold — `SWEG_CHALLENGER_CARDS=0` flips ~300 of 9240 matched games vs sc53a (Astartes 19, Tyranids 28, Votann 24, GSC 21, Necrons 20, AdMech 20…), CONFIRMING the standing sc53a anchor (and every anchor sc13a→sc53a) CONTAINS challenger firing by design — the adopted default, not accidental leakage. It does NOT contaminate other gates' screens (challenger was on in BOTH arms of every post-wave-252 pairing), but the standing gated 3.96 INCLUDES challenger's effect. **VERBATIM RULE** (Wahapedia CA 2025-26 + Flipside + Tournament Companion): trailing by 6+ VP at battle-round start → draw one Challenger card, max 12 VP lifetime (both match the code's `_CHALLENGER_GAP=6` / `_CHALLENGER_LIFETIME_CAP=12`); 9 cards, each 3 VP on a complete-the-mission-OR-use-as-stratagem choice (the sim's variable Tactical-achievement scoring is a bounded proxy for the flat 3 VP). **DECISIVE FIDELITY FINDING:** the competitive Chapter Approved TOURNAMENT COMPANION — the ruleset the May-2026 Warp Friends aggregate is drawn from — EXPLICITLY EXCLUDES Challenger cards ('we have not included the Asymmetric War cards, the Twist cards or the Challenger cards'), so the mechanic is OUT-OF-REFERENCE and default-on trips the EVAL_PROTOCOL rule-9 'no rate dial without an external real-meta reference' guardrail. **SCREEN** (=0 vs sc53a, N=20 matched subset): removing challenger IMPROVES gated MAE 4.95 → 4.64 (−0.30) — METRIC-NEGATIVE on the current frame (the wave-252 −1.04 gain has evaporated as the rest of the sim became faithful). Driver: Adeptus Astartes 52.1 → 47.1 (challenger-on was inflating it +5 over its real 47.0); the Genestealer Cults horde under-pole is DEEPENED by challenger (−3.1), refuting the 'challenger compresses the poles toward real' hypothesis. Signature (N=150): 86.7% of games draw a card but mean challenger VP/player/game only 0.99 (fragile trailing armies cannot achieve the drawn card); margins compress (median 19→15, blowouts 5.4%→2.1%) yet secondary barely moves (13.7→14.0), so it does NOT fill the 14→22.7 secondary headroom. **RECOMMENDATION:** REVERT to default-off and re-anchor (owner call; N=80 not run — the −0.30 is the N=20 estimate). This commit lands FIXES ONLY (citation verbatim + competitive-exclusion; diag #8 measures; docstrings corrected; one broken test `test_off_gate_unset_no_draw_no_score` fixed — it asserted unset==off, false since wave 252); runtime default LEFT on to preserve the sc53a anchor invariant pending the revert+re-anchor.
- CHALLENGER CARDS REVERTED TO DEFAULT-OFF + RE-ANCHORED (2026-07-04, commit a6948b7) — NEW STANDING ANCHOR `data/_anchor_sc54a_n80_log.json`, **gated 3.60** (from sc53a's 3.96, −0.36 at the full N=80 frame — better than the −0.30 N=20 estimate; the best faithful-frame result). The flip to `SWEG_CHALLENGER_CARDS` default-off (`=1` re-enables) is fidelity-first (the competitive Tournament Companion the Warp Friends target is drawn from EXCLUDES challenger cards — EVAL_PROTOCOL rule 9) AND metric-positive. Full-frame movers: **Adeptus Astartes 56.1 → 51.4 (−4.63 toward real 47.0 — largely undoes the self-inflicted ranged-hold over-pole, its gated contribution 6.95 → 2.32); Genestealer Cults +3.59 and Tyranids +4.01 toward real (under-poles ease); T'au lands ON real 54.3; Votann −1.78 toward real.** Small losers (challenger was compressing them toward real): Adeptus Mechanicus +3.22 away, Chaos Space Marines +2.64 away, Thousand Sons −2.18 (deeper under). Committed-default reproduction verified. THE sc54a RESIDUAL: 11 of 22 factions now WITHIN their noise band; the gated error is concentrated in the model-count / survivor floor — Death Guard +19.0 (gated 16.4, alone ~45 percent of the total), Astra Militarum −16.6, Imperial Knights +12.0, Orks −12.6, Chaos Knights +10.3, Aeldari +9.6 — every one audited faithful (rules, damage, scoring, lists) and out-of-reach of Stage-1 fidelity. - THE DEATH GUARD CRATER — DECOMPOSED (2026-07-04, owner "who is cratering into Death Guard?"; mechanism diagnostic docs/_DG_CRATER_FINDINGS.md cherry-picked 12a83a9, reproduction 160/160 all cells; + a Thousand Sons pilot-observation). Death Guard's +19.0 over-pole (~45 percent of the whole gated error) is NOT uniform — it is the ELITE LOW-MODEL INFANTRY class cratering: Death Guard beats Thousand Sons 85.3 / Grey Knights 79.1 / Emperor's Children 77.2 / Adeptus Astartes 77.2 / Custodes 76.6 / Orks 74.7, while mobile-shooty armies hold near even (T'au / Votann / Sororitas / Adeptus Mechanicus ~53) and TWO armies BEAT it (Imperial Knights 47.8, Genestealer Cults 42.2). CROSS-CUTTING: the craters ARE under-poles (Thousand Sons −7.6, Emperor's Children −10.6, Orks −12.6), so the same matchup inflates the top over-pole AND deflates several under-poles. **MECHANISM (decisive): it is a SCORING / OBJECTIVE-SPREAD loss, NOT attrition.** Damage is near-even (132 dealt / 126 taken) and Thousand Sons ends with MORE survivors (18.4 vs 11.6), yet Death Guard scores 1.84 markers/round vs 0.93 — it fans durable bodies across three objectives while the elite gunline's SHOOTY core is PINNED by the hold-and-shoot heuristic (`strategy.py:3748`), camping its own two markers. **CONTAGION RULED OUT by ablation:** removing the whole D1/D2/D3 army rule barely moves the crater (85→79 percent), a ~6-8pt amplifier only (its biggest effect is on T'au, an EVEN matchup); the D-pieces are faithful, DO NOT revert. Controls confirm: Genestealer Cults beats Death Guard because it OUT-SPREADS it (bodies + ambush contest the majority, 1.60 vs 1.41 markers/round); T'au holds even because its firepower actually breaks Death Guard where Rubrics (rDPA 0.94) cannot. **THE THOUSAND SONS PILOT (parallel):** the observed misplay is real (Scarab Occult dumps whole-squad fire into Toughness-12 bricks for 0 while crackable Plague Marines go unshot; the one Thousand Sons win was the crackable-focus game) and the scoped `SWEG_TSONS_TARGET_ECONOMICS` redirect fixes it — but it recovers only 1 game in 40 (Death Guard cell 10→12.5 percent), a FAITHFUL FLOOR on the targeting axis. **VERDICT: a structural durability/model-count objective over-reward (the floor → Stage-2 pricing) UNDER an addressable AI amplifier.** THE NAMED LEVER (the real cross-cutting one, from the mechanism diagnostic — objective-contest, not targeting): when a gunline/elite army is BEHIND on the primary marker count, relax the Shooty hold-and-shoot pin and push a REDUNDANT scoring body (a second unit doubled on a held marker) to contest an enemy-held/uncontested objective — the objective-aware cousin of the ranged-hold family, cross-cutting (deflates Death Guard + lifts Thousand Sons / Emperor's Children) but BOUNDED (reduces not eliminates the crater; toggling the Thousand Sons ranged-hold off moves it only 85→79; Grey Knights is essentially all floor). BUILD QUEUED pending the Grey-Knights/Custodes and Orks pilots.
- THE DEATH GUARD CRATER PILOTS — THREE FLOOR CONFIRMATIONS ACROSS THE TARGET/CHARGE AXES (2026-07-04, all HELD default-off / do-not-adopt; lever code preserved on the worktree branches at the SHAs below, not merged — do-not-adopt floor confirmations, recoverable if the durability over-reward is structurally corrected): **(1) `SWEG_TSONS_TARGET_ECONOMICS`** (worktree 35fb39c, Thousand Sons shooting-target redirect) — the misplay is real (Scarab Occult dump whole-squad fire into Toughness-12 bricks for 0 while crackable Plague Marines go unshot; the one win was the crackable-focus game) but the fix recovers 1 game in 40 (Death Guard cell 10→12.5 percent, Thousand Sons +0.24 inside noise) — FLOOR on the shooting axis. **(2) `SWEG_ELITE_ANTIBRICK`** (worktree b9319ee, Grey Knights / Custodes charge-target redirect) — the misplay is real (premium melee charges Daemon Princes / Mortarion / Bloat-drones in the open, dies without cracking, abandons objectives) but the diagnostic kills the axis: of 232 elite charge picks only ~20 percent of the won't-crack-brick charges had a crackable soft target ALSO in reach (Death Guard's fast bricks SCREEN the soft bodies — a faithful floor), and the redirect screens METRIC-NEGATIVE on its own factions (Grey Knights −7.63) — the melee mirror of the rejected `SWEG_MELEE_CAGING`: engaging AND ignoring a brick both lose. **(3) `SWEG_ORKS_TARPIT_HORDE_ONLY`** (worktree ef727ce, narrows the over-broad `_ork_tarpit_charge_bonus` so it fires only for HORDE-role Boyz, freeing Deffkoptas / Tankbustas specialists) — the misplay is real (specialists tarpit-charge bricks instead of shooting chaff) but the Death Guard cell is UNCHANGED (12/40 both arms, Orks −0.73 away) — the −12.6 Orks under-pole is the model-count/survivor floor, a THIRD-angle confirmation of the WAAAGH-timing and caging rejections. **NET: all three target/charge axes are the durability-in-contest floor — no shooting- or charge-target AI lever moves the Death Guard crater.** The one untested axis is OBJECTIVE-CONTEST MOVEMENT (the mechanism diagnostic's named lever, `SWEG_OBJECTIVE_CONTEST`, in build) — the gunline over-camping its own markers when behind; if that too is bounded-to-nothing, the Death Guard crater is definitively pure floor → Stage-2 pricing.
- THE MANUAL PILOT — A HUMAN CANNOT BEAT DEATH GUARD WITH THOUSAND SONS (2026-07-04, owner-requested: "make all the moves and try to beat Death Guard"; scripts/pilot_manual.py tactic-battery, faithful winner determination, N=40 Thousand Sons vs Death Guard). BASELINE (artificial-intelligence plays Thousand Sons) 17.5 percent. Every good play tested individually then COMBINED — the real test the isolated agent screens could not do. RESULT: **the best combined human game plan tops out at 22.5 percent — still a crushing loss (a real matchup should be near 47).** Per-tactic: `focus_threat` (shoot Death Guard's CRACKABLE scoring bodies — Plague Marines / Poxwalkers, skip the Toughness-12 bricks) **+5.0 (2 games gained, 0 lost — clean, the ONE real gain)**; charge_futile / surgical_contest flat or churn; **`contest_spare` −10.0 and `kite` −12.5 — the intuitive plays BACKFIRE**; the FULL combined plan (focus + futile + contest + kite) is −15.0, WORSE than letting the artificial intelligence play. WHY (rendered seed 2, hand-read): Death Guard parks its durable bricks (Mortarion / Daemon Prince / Bloat-drone / Plagueburst Crawler) ON the central and north objectives, Thousand Sons cannot shift them, so Death Guard holds 3 markers to Thousand Sons' 2 south holds and out-scores 15-to-10 on primary every round. The "contest the majority" strategy that lets Genestealer Cults BEAT Death Guard does NOT transfer: Genestealer Cults has ~79 bodies to spend contesting; low-model Thousand Sons sending its few Rubrics to contest just gets them ground down piecemeal AND cedes its home holds — model count is the whole difference. **DEFINITIVE VERDICT: the Death Guard crater is a genuine durability-in-contest FLOOR, confirmed by hand — no combination of play-quality moves beats it; it belongs to Stage-2 pricing.** The one actionable residual: `focus_threat` (+5.0, the finish-crackable-scoring-bodies target rule — sharper than the agent's `SWEG_TSONS_TARGET_ECONOMICS` +0.24) is the single lever that helps and is worth a proper gated build+screen if the last increment is wanted, but it does NOT crack the crater (22.5 percent ceiling). NOTE (scratch): scripts/pilot_manual.py is an untracked harness scratch file, no repo impact.
- THOUSAND SONS DEVASTATING WOUNDS — CHECKED vs the crater, MODELLED AND FAITHFUL (2026-07-04, owner hypothesis "Thousand Sons' Devastating Wounds should help vs Death Guard"). The offense-fidelity check none of the play-quality pilots did (they assumed the datasheets faithful). RESULT: Devastating Wounds IS modelled on 12 Thousand Sons units, and it is FAITHFUL to BSData: the standard Inferno boltgun (Keywords "-") and Inferno combi-bolter carry NO Devastating Wounds; only the **Soulreaper cannon** (Keywords "Devastating Wounds", 1 heavy weapon per Rubric squad) and the psychic characters (Magnus / Exalted Sorcerer, basket fraction 1.0) do. The Rubric Marines basket fraction 0.18 (Scarab Occult 0.10) is QUANTITATIVELY correct — ~1 Soulreaper (≈4 shots) among ~9 Inferno boltguns (≈18 shots) ≈ 18 percent of the squad's shots carry Devastating Wounds. The crit-wound→mortal save-bypass path is present (`code/units.py` ~5215, gated on the basket fraction) and Death Guard's Feel No Pain is faithful (audit D stripped the fabricated army-wide version), so the mortals are not wrongly eaten. IMPLICATION: the owner's tactical intuition is sound in principle but rests on a rules misconception — real Thousand Sons BULK shooting (inferno boltguns) has NO Devastating Wounds, so it cannot Devastating-Wounds-crack Death Guard; the limited real Devastating Wounds (Soulreaper + psychics) helps modestly against Death Guard's save-reliant Plague Marines, which is precisely where the manual pilot's one positive lever (`focus_threat` +5.0, finish crackable scoring bodies) draws its gain. The offense side of the crater is now verified faithful — this STRENGTHENS the durability-floor verdict rather than reopening it.
- OBJECTIVE-CONTEST-WHEN-BEHIND `SWEG_OBJECTIVE_CONTEST` — BUILT + HELD default-off, NEAR-INERT, the LAST play-quality axis confirmed floor (2026-07-04, worktree 86dc6f0). The mechanism-diagnostic's named lever: a pinned SHOOTY/HEAVY gunline whose army is BEHIND on the live primary-marker count relaxes its hold for a REDUNDANT scoring body (a second holder that leaves without surrendering its marker) and contests the nearest winnable enemy/uncontested marker in one move. Faction-neutral, four guards (never surrender a solely-held marker; catch-up-only; objective-AWARE winnable target not the blind kite; forward-only). Byte-identical off (0/9240). **N=20: 0/9240 flips ALL factions, gated 4.64 → 4.64; Death Guard over-pole FLAT, crater under-poles FLAT, no mobile-army collateral; objective-spread unchanged to the decimal (fires ONCE across 3 cells × 10 seeds).** WHY inert: low-model crater factions barely double up (redundant holders rare) and their contestable markers are unwinnable (Death Guard out-Controls them, the floor) or unreachable (10-42 inches vs ~9 inch reach). **VERDICT: the Death Guard elite-infantry crater is NOT an addressable objective-contest artificial-intelligence gap — all four play-quality axes (shooting-target, charge-target, Ork specialist, objective-contest movement) are floor.**
- ⚠ THE ONE UNTESTED AXIS — THOUSAND SONS LIST-COMPOSITION / MISSING MAGNUS (2026-07-04, owner "best 4 tools Sons have to shift Death Guard"). All the crater pilots tested PLAY quality and assumed the list faithful; the tool-damage ranking into a Toughness-12 2+/4++ Death Guard brick (Daemon Prince of Nurgle) shows the best anti-brick tools are **Magnus the Red ~6.2/activation (Gaze of Magnus: 3D3 shots ≈ 6, Strength-11, AP-2, Damage-3 per shot [NOT 18 — that is the aggregate 6×3], Devastating Wounds, Psychic; + Blade of Magnus melee 7 attacks Strength-16 AP-3; + Doombolt), Chaos Vindicator ~4.7, Mutalith Vortex Beast ~3.2 (Devastating Wounds), and the Cabal-of-Sorcerers psychic mortal wounds (ignore Toughness + saves)** — Magnus verified faithful to BSData (3D3/S11/AP-2/D3). BUT the sim's Thousand Sons ARCHETYPE fields **Magnus 0 percent and the Chaos Vindicator / Defiler engines 0 percent** across 25 builds — it fights Death Guard WITHOUT its two hardest-hitting anti-brick tools, using only the mid-tier (Mutalith 100 / Scarab Occult 100 / Exalted Sorcerer + Ahriman + Infernal Master 100 / Rubric Marines 100). The list-fidelity audit (docs/_LIST_AUDIT_*) checked Death Guard's DURABILITY-per-point, never Thousand Sons' ANTI-BRICK OFFENSE composition. OPEN LEAD (owner steer requested): if real 2026 competitive Thousand Sons run Magnus (commonly a centerpiece), the sim under-guns them versus Death Guard and part of the 85 percent crater is a LIST gap, not pure floor — reopening the Thousand Sons floor with an asterisk. COUNTER-CAVEAT: Magnus is an 18-wound model Death Guard would farm for Bring It Down (the Canis-Rex-liability precedent for Imperial Knights), so adding him may not crack the crater and could backfire — needs a sourced-list check + a Magnus-included-template screen to settle, NOT yet run.
- ⭐ MAGNUS THE RED ADOPTED — THE DEATH GUARD CRATER WAS A LIST GAP, NOT A DURABILITY FLOOR (2026-07-04, `SWEG_TSONS_MAGNUS` default-on, `=0` kill-switch, cherry-picked fce96c8→a809f3d; NEW standing anchor `data/_anchor_sc55a_n80_log.json`, gated **3.51**, best faithful frame). SOURCED: real May-2026 competitive 2000-point Thousand Sons run Magnus as a common centerpiece (undefeated Rubricae Phalanx + Magnus, LVO 5-1, three Grand Coven top placings; a ~50/50 split with Ahriman Rubric-spam). The template excluded him on STALE 1000-point-budget logic and the EPIC-HERO random_fill bar, so he was fielded 0 percent at the 2000-point eval; the fix seeds him via the epic-hero anchor guarantee (Mortarion/Khârn mechanism, ~100 percent), RUBRICAE affinity preserved (detachment stays Rubricae Phalanx 25/25). **N=80 Thousand-Sons-scoped vs sc54a: Thousand Sons 46.3 → 53.0 (+6.70, landing on real 53.9), gated 3.60 → 3.51 (−0.09); Death Guard −1.02 DECISIVE toward real (the CROSS-CUTTING deflation — fixing the opponent's list chips the over-pole from the other side); the Death-Guard-vs-Thousand-Sons cell collapses 90 → 52.5 percent.** Bring-It-Down instrumented: Magnus survives 60 percent and Death Guard's secondary VP DROPS −1.97/game with him present (his body removes more Death Guard scoring than his death feeds) — outcome (A), a clean fidelity gain, NOT the Canis-Rex liability. **THE MAJOR REVISION: the four play-quality pilots + the manual hand-pilot all concluded the Death Guard crater was a durability FLOOR — but they were ALL playing the Magnus-LESS list. The crater was the sim fielding Thousand Sons WITHOUT its best anti-brick tool. Adding the real list collapses it (10 → 47.5 percent vs Death Guard). The "pure floor" verdict was an artifact of an under-built list; the owner's list-composition line of questioning (Devastating Wounds → best tools → missing Magnus) cracked the worst crater in the game.** IMPLICATION: Death Guard's +19 over-pole (45 percent of the gated error) is partly a LIST-COMPOSITION artifact of its opponents being under-built, NOT pure durability floor — every opponent list-fix deflates it from the opponent side. The Emperor's Children missing-detachment build (running) is the direct sibling. Committed-default reproduction verified before push.
- `SWEG_ASTARTES_RANGED_HOLD` — Adeptus Astartes ranged-hold ADOPTED default-on (watched-replay lever, 2026-07-03, `=0` kill-switch; owner chose piloted work on non-durable under-poles; NEW standing anchor `data/_anchor_sc53a_n80_log.json`, gated 3.96). The EIGHTH scoped ranged-hold (`_ad_astartes`), derived from three watched games: the Marine non-[ASSAULT] fire line Advanced its opening rounds and marched into the enemy (Orks seed 1 a 6-67 blow-out — the two Repulsors that HELD killed 16/19 Boyz per activation; Necrons seed 2 Eradicators Advanced in the Tactical-Doctrine round, which lifts only the Fall-Back lockout not the Advance lockout, forfeiting melta shots). RULE (Slaanesh's Due): pledge enemy-units-destroyed-this-round; on meeting the pledge gain Pact points = pledge (else Warlord takes D3 mortal wounds); EMPEROR'S CHILDREN units gain four CUMULATIVE tiers by running Pact total — 1+ re-roll Hit 1s, 3+ re-roll Wound 1s, 5+ melee [LETHAL HITS]+[SUSTAINED HITS 1], 7+ Critical Hit on unmodified 5+ (all four mapped onto existing attack machinery; the ranged crit-threshold generalised, default-6 byte-identical). Bespoke Battle hook `_settle_ec_pact_points` tracks the Pact total; `Unit.attack` reads it. APPROXIMATION (documented, honest): the pledge is idealised as competent play — gain one Pact point per enemy unit destroyed while the Warlord lives; the D3-mortal-wound miss-downside is omitted (a competent pilot pledges conservatively to avoid it). - THE SYSTEMATIC COMPOSITION AUDIT — RANKED (2026-07-04, two read-only agents, docs/_COMPAUDIT_UNDERPOLES.md + docs/_COMPAUDIT_OVERPOLES.md, cherry-picked 94fe507+c452fca). - COMPOSITION-AUDIT BUILDS — TWO REAL WINS BANKED, THE REST CONFIRMED STRUCTURAL (2026-07-04). **Orks Ghazghkull (`SWEG_ORKS_GHAZGHKULL`, cherry-picked 01d2c3d) BUILT + HELD default-off — METRIC-NEUTRAL, the Astra Militarum parallel.** Sourced (real War Horde centerpiece), mechanism identical to Magnus (0 → 100 percent via the epic-hero anchor guarantee), byte-identical off — but N=20 Orks-scoped vs sc56a is FLAT (31.8 → 31.3, gated 3.37 → 3.36; Death Guard cell 30.0 → 32.5 non-decisive). Ghazghkull alone is flat because he is one durable body, NOT the tool the Ork list actually lacks. ⚠ CORRECTION (owner-directed manual pilot, superseding the premature "structural" call below): the full manual Ork pilot (`scripts/pilot_manual` battery vs Aeldari, the 13-percent worst matchup, N=40) shows NO tactic recovers it — focus/contest/kite all neutral-to-harmful, the play-quality floor — BUT reading the losses found the real cause is a LIST MIS-COMPOSITION, not a pure floor: (1) the loss shape is a LATE-GAME OBJECTIVE FADE (Orks lead rounds 1-2, get overtaken 3-5 as the mobile opponent re-contests markers — a close scoring loss, 49-55, NOT an attrition wipe); (2) the sim's Ork archetype fields **9 Killa Kans in 100 percent of builds** (watched sitting INERT at "HELD @99in-off" the whole game) + 12.6 Tankbustas + 18 Nobz, and **ZERO mobility** (no Trukks / Battlewagons / Stormboyz / Kommandos) — a static Kan-heavy list where the real 2026 competitive Orks are a MOBILE War Horde. The mis-composition explains all three observations (inert Killa Kans = wrong units; late fade = no mobility to re-contest; Ghazghkull flat = a body when the list needs mobility). LIST-RESHAPE DISPATCHED (source the real list, drop the Killa Kan spam, add the transport/mobility package, screen) — the Magnus/Emperor's-Children recipe. Ghazghkull held default-off pending that reshape. **`SWEG_ORKS_REALISM` ADOPTED default-on 2026-07-04 — the reshape confirmed. Sourced: 3 tournament War Horde lists (Rose City Rampage 1st, rank-1 UK Orks 2nd, Bolter-and-Chainsword) all Ghazghkull-centred + mechanised (Trukks/Battlewagon/Stormboyz), ZERO Killa Kans. Dropped the Killa Kans, added Ghazghkull + Beastboss + Beast Snagga Boyz + 3× Trukk + Battlewagon + 2× Stormboyz (the sim DOES model transport embark/disembark, so the mobility is real). N=80 Orks-scoped vs sc56a: Orks 31.8 → 36.6 (+4.86 DECISIVE toward real 45.3), gated 3.37 → 3.08 (−0.29); the mobile matchups the pilot flagged all rise (Aeldari, T'au, Votann, Necrons), the durability-floor cells stay flat (Death Guard 10→10, Thousand Sons 10→10 — correctly not pretending to crack the floor); collateral mostly toward-real (Aeldari −0.77, Chaos Knights −0.58 deflate; Astra Militarum −1.04 the one small cost). NEW standing anchor `data/_anchor_sc57a_n80_log.json` (gated 3.08, best faithful frame). THE THIRD list-composition win of the day (Magnus, Emperor's Children, Orks); the manual pilot the owner insisted on was ESSENTIAL — the flat Ghazghkull-alone screen had hidden the reshape exactly as the flat play-quality pilots hid the Thousand Sons Magnus gap. Committed-default reproduction verified.**
- CHAOS DAEMONS MANUAL PILOT — DURABILITY FLOOR, NOT A LIST GAP (2026-07-04, owner-directed, the same recipe as the Orks pilot). Chaos Daemons −8.2 (44.2 vs real 52.6) is a BROAD SHALLOW under-pole (near-even vs most of the field; loses only to the popular durables — Death Guard 37.5, Imperial Knights 43, Chaos Knights 43 — and BEATS the hordes/weak: Orks 60, Sororitas 60, Genestealer Cults 65), NOT a crater. `scripts/pilot_manual` battery vs Death Guard (N=40): NO tactic recovers it (focus −7.5, surgical −7.5, heavy_hold −27.5, kite −32.5; contest_spare +2.5 within noise) — the play-quality floor. The Death Guard loss is the durability-in-contest floor (Death Guard out-holds the objectives with tougher bodies). LIST CHECK — UNLIKE ORKS, THE LIST IS ALREADY FAITHFUL: the mono-god rotation is reasoned-faithful (real 2026 Daemons ARE typically mono-god), and the strongest sourced meta variant — the **Be'lakor Scintillating Legion** — turned out to be ALREADY DEFAULT-ON (the `SWEG_DAEMONS_BELAKOR` gate reads default "1"; the archetype's "default OFF" comment was STALE, now corrected), fielding ~20 percent of builds. So Chaos Daemons already fields all its real variants and the −8.2 exists WITH the faithful list active. ⚠ VERDICT RETRACTED — the "floor" call was PREMATURE (owner catch: "did you actually play trying to win?"). It was made from the battery + objective-control TEXT without reading the per-round board IMAGES — the exact shortcut the new `docs/PILOT_PROTOCOL.md` now forbids. Reading a close loss (seed 2, a Khorne list) round-by-round revealed a SPECIFIC decision error: tied 39-39 at round 4 holding objectives, then in the DECISIVE round 5 the entire Khorne melee force (Bloodcrushers / Flesh Hounds / Bloodmaster) MOVED OFF the held objectives to futile-charge a Toughness-12 Plagueburst Crawler for ZERO damage — Daemons scored 0 in round 5 and lost a tied game 39-60. A human keeping those units on the objectives wins. The battery missed it because no fixed tactic expresses "do not abandon a held objective to charge a brick." So Chaos Daemons is NOT confirmed floor — there is a fixable objective-abandonment misplay (distinct from the rejected charge-target redirect: this is a MOVEMENT / hold-the-objective decision in the scoring round). NEXT: confirm the pattern across several seeds/gods, then test a narrow "hold a controlled objective in the final rounds instead of moving off it to charge an un-crackable brick" lever. The manual pilot correctly distinguished Orks (list gap, +4.86); the Daemon list is faithful, but the pole is a PLAY misplay, not the floor. ONE UNTESTED JUDGMENT CALL left open (only if the meta proves Be'lakor-DOMINANT rather than diverse): field Be'lakor as the sole representative build at ~100 percent, the Orks-War-Horde treatment — but the design's diverse-mono-god rationale is sourced and defensible, so this is not pursued without fresh sourcing. **DATA-FIX ADOPTED (ungated override): Ghazghkull's invulnerable save 2+ → 4+** — the mapper leaked his 2+ ARMOUR value into the invuln field, overstating durability; corrected to the canonical 4+ (Iron Gob) per BSData/Wahapedia (`data/overrides.json`). Byte-identical for the current eval (Ghazghkull fielded 0 percent with the gate off); matters for Stage-2 pricing. **Adeptus Astartes battleline broadening (`SWEG_ASTARTES_BATTLELINE`, cherry-picked dd2a210) BUILT + SCREENED + REFUTED, HELD default-off.** The audit's theory (thin battleline → stacked firepower suppresses scoring) did NOT hold: broadening to 2× Intercessors + Infiltrators + Scouts (census: premium-shooting 36.4 → 24.7 models, battleline 7.6 → 35.6) moved Adeptus Astartes the WRONG WAY at N=20 (51.1 → 57.4, +6.30, WIDENING the +4.3 over-pole toward +10.4); an Intercessor-only diagnostic still rose +3.25 — the core mechanism is that **trading firepower for more objective-control bodies makes the sim's Astartes STRONGER**, a FRESH confirmation that the objective-control / victory-point economy OVER-REWARDS durable model-count (the same survivor-uptime substrate). NET COMPOSITION-AUDIT CONCLUSION: the list-fidelity sweep found its TWO real wins (Magnus +6.70, Emperor's Children +3.34 — both where a specific missing tool addressed a specific loss shape); Astra Militarum / Adeptus Astartes are FAITHFUL-LIST-BUT-STRUCTURAL (the durability/objective economy); ORKS is NOT — the manual pilot found a genuine list mis-composition (Kan-heavy static vs the real mobile War Horde; reshape dispatched, see the correction above); the Imperial Knights / Chaos Knights / Aeldari over-poles are faithful lists that deflate only as their opponents get fixed. LESSON RE-LEARNED: do NOT conclude "structural" from a flat single-lever screen without the full manual pilot — the flat Ghazghkull screen hid the list gap exactly as the flat play-quality pilots hid the Thousand Sons Magnus gap. The residual split is still being re-measured as list-fidelity work lands.
- DEEP-STRIKE ANTI-TANK DELIVERY REFUTED AS A LEVER (2026-07-03, 60-arrival instrument): placement is ALREADY adequate — 60/60 arrivals landed within 12 inches of a Knight (avg 11.1, the 9-inch minimum enforced every time; the mass-drop anchor incidentally targets sparse Knight armies well), 10% die to Fire Overwatch on landing, and of those that fire, **85% shoot the adjacent War Dog instead of the Knight they dropped on — the same min-health picker bias, operating at point-blank range**. Capability is concentrated in 5 factions (Daemons/Grey Knights/Genestealer Cults ~70% of their anti-brick budget, Necrons 48%, T'au 15.5%) and is ZERO for Astra Militarum and both Knight factions. Do not build arrival-placement as a metric lever; if ever built for fidelity, scope to the 5 real-capability factions and bundle with the targeting fix family. Side findings: reserve selection is Objective-Control-blind (simulator.py:8707), the round-2 drop gate reads Knights as unscreened (strategy.py:4034), T'au fusion at 9-11 inches clears full range but misses the 6-inch melta half-range (57% uplift unrealized). **With this, the last non-structural frontier item closes: everything remaining is the durability-in-contest trade asymmetry + the owner-directed per-faction extraction wave (in build).**
- THE CAPSTONE LEVERS MEASURED — BOTH DOWN; THE AI CHANNEL IS CLOSED (2026-07-03): `SWEG_ANTITANK_ADVANCE_DISCIPLINE` (commit 0014a02, the narrow anti-brick hold) **REJECTED at N=40 full-frame: 4.02 → 6.37 (+2.35)** — the N=10 direction inverted at scale; every durable/shooty army banks hold-and-shoot harder than the anti-tank it was built for (Grey Knights +13.91, Tyranids +11.18, Imperial Knights +8.49, Adeptus Mechanicus +8.46) and Astra Militarum craters −11.58; kept default-off, DO NOT ADOPT (the generic-family curse, now proven at every scoping). `SWEG_PERSISTENT_NOMINATION` (commit 3264682, with the contest-pool nominee fix) **HELD with the deepest proof of the program: the field nominates the Knight in 98.8% of shooting phases — the pointing gap is CLOSED at the mechanism level — and Knights dead per game goes DOWN (0.448 → 0.365)**, because pointing is symmetric and the durable side banks it harder (Astra Militarum −8.33 as its hulls become everyone's standing nominee). **PROOF BY EXHAUSTION COMPLETE: budget sufficient, conversion clean (1.34x), range reached, terrain dense, pointing closed — every artificial-intelligence, rules, and data channel is measured and the durable side wins them all. The wall is the trade asymmetry itself.** THE TWO REMAINING FRONTIER ITEMS: (1) DEEP-STRIKE / RESERVE DELIVERY of anti-tank (faithful, unexplored at the mechanism level, and aimed exactly at the quantified bars — converts the out-of-range 25.4% into half-range melta without crossing the kill zone and dodges the dead-on-approach 24.8%; note the generic reserve probe's +1.67 rejection was ARMY-WIDE reserves, not targeted anti-tank delivery); (2) the durability-in-contest scoring remodel (the long-parked structural item — an owner decision). Diagnostic for (1) dispatched.
- THE FINAL DECOMPOSITION — THE INVESTIGATION'S CAPSTONE (2026-07-03, waterfall instrument, N=12+12 paired): **conversion is CLEAN (realized/theoretical = 1.34x — attack() in battle EXCEEDS the picker's single-profile estimate; every defect candidate cleared: cover, invuln, brackets, transients, spillover). The anti-Knight gap is ACTIVATION-ALLOCATION: of 77.4 theoretical expected wounds/game, only 11.4% is ever pointed at a Knight** — DEAD-before-delivering 24.8%, OUT-OF-RANGE 25.4% (the ranged-hold-created geometry; short-range melta/lances never reach), SUPPRESSED 25.4% (no-line-of-sight 54% of that, **the AI ADVANCING anti-tank units that had a shot 31%** — Necrons 51%, fell-back 13%), POINTED-ELSEWHERE 13.0% (the min-health picker 73%, the contest-pool filter dropping off-objective Knights 27%). **MECHANICAL ROOT of the focus-fire/target-economics away-readings: kill-fraction scoring is BACKWARDS for durable bricks (a 26-wound Knight gets the floor bonus, a killable War Dog the cap) — those gates are DO-NOT-ADOPT-AS-BUILT for anti-brick pointing.** TWO BUILDABLE LEVERS (dispatched): (1) anti-tank advance-discipline in _do_move — never Advance a unit whose weapon can wound an in-range durable brick (recovers ~31% of suppressed; the narrow faithful cousin of the retired generic advance-discipline); (2) PERSISTENT multi-round focus nomination — reshape the existing _nominate_focusfire_target (simulator.py:12996, currently gated on an unreachable one-phase 22-expected-wound crack) into cross-round commitment of anti-tank onto one Knight until dead. HONEST CEILING: both together roughly double kills (0.42 → ~1.0/game; real ~2) — **the remaining ~60% is attrition + range, the durability/standoff structural wall, now QUANTIFIED: the field's anti-tank dies or lacks reach before pointing is even a question.**
- STANDOFF GEOMETRY REFUTED; THE CONVERSION GAP ISOLATED (2026-07-03, geometry instrument, N=40+40 paired) — anti-tank output is IN RANGE fast (57-70% round 1, >85% round 3, all four diverse opponents), Knights sit ON objectives (median 4-8 inches, no standoff; the old "@99in-off" log line was a stale artifact), and Knights out-score primary AND secondary AND kills simultaneously. THE COUNTERFACTUAL: `SWEG_CK_RANGED_HOLD=0` swings Chaos Knights 87.5 → 65.0 (22.5 points!) with in-range fraction UNCHANGED — the hold's value is SHOOTING-PHASE UPTIME (never forfeiting a shooting phase to Advance; faithful — that is exactly the cited rule trade — but its magnitude rides on the conversion gap below), and opponent anti-tank survival nearly doubles when Knights advance. THE RESIDUE, now fully isolated: **even at 65% (hold off) vs real 44.7, in-range anti-tank fire converts to only 12.5-17.8% of the Knight roster dead per game vs the real ~50%+ — the per-shot math is verified correct (accounting diagnostic), the range is there, the pointing is built: something between "in-range activation" and "Knight wounds removed" eats the fire.** Next instrument dispatched: per-activation realized-vs-theoretical decomposition for in-range anti-tank into Knights — attribute the loss among (a) the picker STILL choosing another target despite focus-fire (class-bias interactions), (b) attacker death before activating (within-round alpha order), (c) overkill/spillover waste, (d) any conversion defect in the save/damage chain under real battle state (contrast with the isolated-attack() budget that read sufficient).
- FOCUS-FIRE COMPLETION BUILT + HELD; THE DECISIVE NEGATIVE (2026-07-03) — `SWEG_FOCUS_FIRE` (wounded-target completion 1.0-3.0x in both pickers, commit 11a8277 cherry-picked; byte-identical off via motion-proof fingerprint): probe +1.05 away AND **dead Knights per game UNCHANGED at 0.625 both arms** — pointed fire still does not convert to kills. With the budget proven sufficient and the pointing now built, the failure is one level below concentration: **RANGE/STANDOFF GEOMETRY** — the sim's ranged-hold Knights castle at extreme standoff behind 48-72 inch guns while the field's anti-tank (melta 12-24, lances 24-36) must cross the kill zone to answer, and dies on approach; real missions force Knights forward. UNMEASURED until now; the direct instrument dispatched: per-round fraction of the field's anti-tank output whose range actually covers the Knights' positions, and what the Knights' scoring looks like from standoff (how does a castled Knight army WIN — kills or points?).

- SECONDARY PURSUIT PACKAGE `SWEG_SECONDARY_PURSUIT` BUILT + HELD default-off (2026-07-02, branch `claude/secondary-pursuit-ai`) — the three-part revisit the previous entry (line 112) queued, built as ONE gated package so the parts are validated together: **(a) Establish Locus positional filter** (`Battle._assign_establish_locus_actions`) — assignment now re-uses `_score_establish_locus`'s own positional test (enemy deployment zone or within 6" of the battlefield centre) so a spare unit is only committed to the action when it is already standing where the card pays, instead of any spare unit regardless of position. **(b) Card-pursuit movement AI switched on** — the package gate implies the existing, previously held-off `SWEG_TAC_PURSUE` (`Battle._assign_card_pursuit` / `_board_pursuit_goals`, built wave 121/180), and adds a new Engage on All Fronts branch (spare chaff routed to the nearest unoccupied table quarter) the existing layer never covered. **(c) Voluntary-discard, your-turn-only** — the package gate implies `SWEG_TAC_VOLUNTARY_DISCARD`; when both are on, the command-point grant moves from the round-end batch (which could not tell either army's turn from the other's) to a new `Battle._apply_tactical_voluntary_discard_own_turn`, called once per army from `_run_round_vanilla_turns` at the true end of that army's own turn, and the discard heuristic changes from `age >= 1` to an achievability read (`_tac_discard_card_cannot_pay`: structurally impossible to ever pay, or a full previous round at zero). Each base gate (`SWEG_TAC_VOLUNTARY_DISCARD`, `SWEG_TAC_PURSUE`) keeps its own original, independently-screened semantics unchanged when the package gate is off. VALIDATION: both-off byte-identical on a 328-game Astra-Militarum/Orks scoped paired check (0 flips) and on the full pytest suite (identical 35 pre-existing failures on and off, none new); `scripts.audit_rules` green; `run.py --cli` clean in all four gate combinations. SIGNATURES (`scripts.diag_signatures --pairs 20 --seeds 5`, N=50 pairs): secondary mean 11.9 → 13.7 (real 22.7 — still under, smaller lift than hoped). PER-CARD ACHIEVE-RATE (`scripts.diag_tacdeck_achieve`, the same probe the line-112 diagnostic used, four under-shooter factions): overall held-card achieve rate roughly doubled (21-32% → 40-47%); `defend_stronghold` 9-12% → 25-31%, `extend_battle_lines` 7-19% → 19-33%, `area_denial` 19-38% → 23-53%, `cleanse` 20-30% → 35-48%; `engage_on_all_fronts` and `secure_no_mans_land` were already near ceiling and stayed flat. Establish Locus (part a) is invisible on the default frame — the card only enters the deck when the SEPARATE, already-held-off `SWEG_ACTION_ECONOMY` gate is also set — so it was verified in isolation (`SWEG_ACTION_ECONOMY=1` alone vs plus the package): achieve rate 5-12% → 38-60%, confirming the positional filter works exactly as designed even though it contributes nothing to the headline numbers above until `SWEG_ACTION_ECONOMY` is separately adopted. **HELD default-off pending a full N=80 screen against the standing anchor** (this session ran targeted, cheap, scoped/signature checks per the eval protocol, not a decision-grade N=80 arm) — the achievability half of the line-112 diagnosis is now addressed, but the lift (11.9→13.7) is still well short of real (22.7), consistent with the wave-180 finding that several board cards are additionally capped by map objective layout (most rotation maps place no markers in either deployment zone), a representation gap this package does not touch.
- (Drukhari Tormentors, above) **METHODOLOGY NOTE:** levers that consume EXTRA RNG (forced Battle-shock tests, mortal-wound d6 rolls) shift the downstream RNG stream and break the paired common-random-number pairing → the screen gets noisy (142 flips here are mostly stream divergence, not balance changes); judge such levers on the mean win-rate delta + the gated-MAE, NOT the flip count. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- ANTI-TANK WEAPON-CHOICE BASKET `SWEG_CHOICE_TARGET_BASKET` — BUILT + parsed.json regenerated with the gate ON and committed as the new catalogue data (2026-07-02, `claude/antitank-choice-baseline`; gate default-OFF reproduces parsed.json byte-for-byte, proven by an off-regen cmp, so the identity story is the git history exactly like the choice-group fix). The mapper's weapon-CHOICE scorer `expected_damage_through_baseline` had NO Strength-versus-Toughness wound roll at all — a weapon's Strength was ignored entirely — so every anti-tank option lost to the higher-volume anti-infantry option in its group (`scripts/diag_antitank_pick.py` blast radius = 56 biased groups across 7 factions; the canonical case is the Drukhari Ravager firing Strength-6 Disintegrator Cannons, not Strength-12 Dark Lances). The fix scores every choice option against a representative three-class target basket — light infantry 15 % (Toughness 3 / save 5+), heavy infantry 51 % (Toughness 4 / save 3+), monster-or-vehicle 34 % (Toughness 10 / save 3+), the class census of the `code/archetypes.py` templates weighted by their template counts — WITH the wound roll, routed through one `_choice_score` dispatch so every scorer (single-model groups, multi-model picker, legacy path) is consistent; a one-shot weapon is discounted to one fifth (fires once per battle) so hunter-killer missiles and the Reductus Saboteur's demolition charges stop being picked as primaries. Regen changes 185 units (64 primary / 115 secondary / 38 melee flips), all toward higher Strength (Ravager → Dark Lance, Knight Crusader → Thermal cannon, Tyrannofex → Rupture cannon, superheavy twin-heavy-bolter secondaries → Lascannon); zero units lose a weapon in the default per-model path (model_loadouts byte-identical count everywhere; only two Tyranid legacy top-level headlines drop a distinct name when a high-Strength EXTRA-ATTACKS melee weapon is promoted). Knight Despoiler committed gun-build override untouched and still wins at load. **DECISIVE + HONEST NEGATIVE RESULT: the fix did NOT deflate the Knights — the Chaos-Knights-scoped probe (SWEG_CK_REALISM=1, `--use-archetype`, N=20, paired base-vs-new in one environment) moved Chaos Knights 84.9 % → 88.6 % (real 44.7), +3.7 pp the WRONG way.** Sharpening the field's anti-tank ALSO sharpens the Knights' own guns, and their opponents' now-anti-tank shots still bounce off the durability substrate (the 72.8 %-of-shots-deal-zero finding is a save-and-wounds-in-contest problem, not a weapon-choice problem). This REFUTES the "anti-tank weapon-choice baseline is the convergent Knights-deflation lead" hypothesis (was the top lead after the third terrain refutation); it CONFIRMS the line-114 diagnosis that the durability-in-contest substrate is the real multiplier. The fix is kept as a faithful catalogue correction (fidelity-first; the Ravager genuinely should fire Dark Lances), NOT as a metric lever. Unit-test failure set identical to the unmodified base (three wave-232/233 secondary-keyword-parity guards re-anchored to units whose picks the basket leaves in place: Death Guard Helbrute for the hazardous secondary, Adepta Sororitas Exorcist for the indirect-fire secondary). See [[project-antitank-picker-bias]] and [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- RECIPROCAL RULE EXONERATED FOR THE ASTRA MILITARUM UNDER-POLE — DO NOT BUILD A SCREEN-MANAGEMENT LEVER (2026-07-03, pilot-observation + instrument `SWEG_RECIP_INSTR`, read-only, byte-identical off): the re-anchor note (this section, durability-wave entry) attributed Astra Militarum's −14.0 to "the reciprocal shooting rule turns its own screens into gun-blockers" and named screen-management as the next piloting target. **That attribution is REFUTED by an isolated measurement.** THE COUNTERFACTUAL (needs no code — the existing `=0` kill-switch): `SWEG_BGNT_RECIPROCAL=0`, Astra-Militarum-scoped N=20 paired vs the standing anchor, moves Astra Militarum's aggregate win rate by **+0.24 (30.5 → 30.8), 46 flips netting ~zero, gated MAE −0.79** — the reciprocal rule owns essentially NONE of the −14 (the re-anchor's −3.86 was the NET of the whole nine-divergence durability wave, prose-attributed to this one rule; the rule's own adoption probe was already logged net-neutral, WE −0.13 / CK +0.61). THE MECHANISM (instrument, 12 games/opponent, reproducible): the reciprocal filter FULLY empties an Astra Militarum shooting activation's target pool ≤0.2 times/game (it re-targets — the guns keep firing), causes a focus-fire DOWNGRADE (best target dropped for a weaker shot) ≤1.5 times/game costing ≤1.4 expected wounds/game vs melee opponents (Emperor's Children 0.22, World Eaters 1.39), and **exactly ZERO footprint vs a gunline opponent (Imperial Knights) that never engages the screens** — negligible against a gunline dealing hundreds of wounds/game. WHY NO FALL-BACK LEVER: a screen that Falls Back to unblock guns that are ≈never blocked would remove a bodies-forward objective holder to chase a ≤1.4-wound/game gain — the SIXTH hold-type rejection would-be, and `SWEG_TANK_SCREEN` already proved Astra Militarum −3.94 when its platforms hold to screen (REVERTED section). The Astra Militarum −14.0 is the durability-wave SCORING/defensive changes (opponents' newly-faithful invulnerables, Rotate Ion Shields, Smokescreen, secondary economy), not the reciprocal shooting rule — chase the secondary-economy audit (durability-wave gauge entry), not screen piloting. Landed: the `SWEG_RECIP_INSTR` instrument + `scripts/diag_recip_block.py` for future under-pole sizing (no rule citation — read-only, same class as `SWEG_SHOOTLOSS_INSTR`).
- `SWEG_EC_RANGED_HOLD` — Emperor's Children ranged-hold BUILT + HELD default-off pending an eighty-battle confirmation (watched-replay lever, 2026-07-03, `=1` opt-in; byte-identical off, 0/36,960 flips vs sc52a; audit green, `run.py --cli` clean). The eighth faction-scoped entry point (`_ad_ec`) on the shared advance-suppression block, cloning the adopted Astra Militarum / Chaos Knights / Leagues of Votann / Thousand Sons / Adepta Sororitas logic. Derived from three watched piloted games under the current `SWEG_EC_REALISM` archetype (Chaos Knights seed 0, Orks seed 1, Necrons seed 2): the Noise Marines line (ranged damage per activation 4.66, range 18 inches) Advanced round 1 and, in two of the three games, round 2 as well, forfeiting its Shooting phase both times before firing a single shot; the Sorcerer (5.34/18) and the Tormentors troop choice (2.27/24, itself able to shoot AND still charge off a Normal move) showed the same pattern. The shared rDPA ≥ 2.0 & range ≥ 18 inch filter holds exactly Noise Marines, Sorcerer, Tormentors and the Chaos Rhino transport (2.67/48). **It does NOT hold the Defiler** — the single most dramatic misplay across all three games (Advanced rounds 1-2 every game, forfeiting a 12.0-rDPA fire platform, then dealt 16-29 damage per activation once finally held before usually dying in melee) — because the Defiler's top-level `range_inches` reflects the mapper's best-single-expected-value weapon pick (Heavy baleflamer, a 12-inch torrent), while its dominant output rides its SECONDARY profile (Hades battle cannon, 48 inches, rDPA ≈ 12.0) and extra_ranged_profiles (Ectoplasma destructor / Hades lascannon / Heavy reaper autocannon, 36-48 inches) that this shared filter does not read; closing that gap needs either a secondary-profile-aware reach check or a data-correctness weapon re-pin (precedent: the wave-95 Knight Tyrant `range_inches` override, and the already-adopted `world_eaters_defiler` ATK-BIAS-1 weapon pin in `data/overrides.json`, which the identically-statlined `emperor_s_children_defiler` and `chaos_space_marines_defiler` catalog entries do not share) — FLAGGED, NOT built this wave. ADJACENT FABRICATION FOUND (not built, same scope discipline): `emperor_s_children_defiler`'s override sets a bare top-level `devastating_wounds: true`, which the loader merge applies to whichever weapon is primary (the baleflamer, which has no printed Devastating Wounds) rather than the Heavy reaper autocannon the override's own notes describe (which already carries `devastating_wounds: true` natively from the mapper, needing no override) — the exact fabrication class `SWEG_WE_DW_RANGED_FAB` already fixed for `world_eaters_heldrake` / `world_eaters_master_of_executions`, but that gate's scope list does not include either Defiler entry. **N=20 Emperor's-Children-scoped vs sc52a (screening size per this wave's protocol; not an adoption-grade sample): Emperor's Children +5.50 DECISIVE (46.2 → 51.7, toward the live `warpfriends_rolling.json` real target 53.32 — the stale 47.9 hand-transcribed figure this wave's brief quoted is superseded, see `scripts/evaluate_vs_meta.py` line ~90), gated MAE 4.23 → 4.10 (−0.14, best standing frame), 169/840 games flipped.** Small decisive collateral, all toward-real or flat: T'au Empire −0.36, Chaos Space Marines −0.36, World Eaters −0.40, Drukhari −0.35 (no faction pushed away from its target). RECOMMENDATION: ADOPT — closes roughly three-quarters of the Emperor's Children gap with clean collateral and zero overshoot risk (unlike the Chaos Knights / Leagues of Votann / Tyranids ranged-holds, which land or would land past real); confirm with the standard N=80 before flipping the default at the next wave close. Cited `simulator.ec_ranged_hold` (`data/rule_citations.d/emperors_children.json`).
- **EC ranged-hold N=80 VERDICT — HELD default-off, the agent's N=20 was optimistic (2026-07-04): on the current sc53a frame the N=80 Emperor's-Children-scoped screen reads Emperor's Children +20.88 (42.2 → 63.0), gated 3.96 → 3.92 (−0.04, WITHIN NOISE), off-path byte-identical (0/840 flips vs sc53a — the three-way merge with the Astartes lever is verified clean).** The N=20/N=80 gap (+5.50 → +20.88) is explained: the agent measured on the pre-Astartes base where Emperor's Children sat at 46.2; adopting the Astartes hold dropped it to 42.0 and the lever's effect is larger on this frame. VERDICT: this is the TYRANIDS profile, NOT the Astartes one — a HARD OVERSHOOT (63.0 vs live real 53.3 = +9.7 over) for a NEGLIGIBLE gated gain (−0.04 vs the Astartes −0.27 that justified adoption). Holding it would convert a clean −11 under-pole diagnostic into +9.7 over-pole noise for a noise-level metric gain — the model-count / survivor over-reward economy expressing through yet another elite army (Emperor's Children joins Tyranids as a faithful ranged-hold that overshoots on this floor). HELD default-off, kept in-tree; reopens ONLY if the model-count / survivor scoring economy is structurally corrected (same reopen condition as the Tyranids gauge). The Defiler-not-held limitation and the `emperor_s_children_defiler` Devastating-Wounds fabrication remain the flagged follow-ups regardless.
- Chaos Daemons Scintillating Legion pilot-observation — INVESTIGATED, NO MISPLAY LEVER FOUND (2026-07-03; three watched piloted games under `SWEG_DAEMONS_BELAKOR`: Orks seed 5, Imperial Knights seed 10, Necrons seed 17, all seeds pre-selected to land the Be'lakor/Kairos/Lord-of-Change/Great-Unclean-One four-flagship sub-archetype). Be'lakor, Kairos Fateweaver, the Lord of Change and the Great Unclean One already Advance only round 1 (when nothing is in range, correct) and hold-and-shoot from round 2 onward in all three games — no repeat of the Emperor's Children Defiler-class "Advance every round regardless" misplay was found on the shared rDPA ≥ 2.0 & range ≥ 18 inch filter roster check (Pink Horrors rDPA 1.33 sits just BELOW the family's 2.0 floor, by design — the same zone Cadian Shock Troops occupy — so its cheap-troop "wander toward no objective in particular" behaviour in the Necrons game is the already-validated let-weak-bodies-advance doctrine, not a fixable hold; building a Pink-Horrors hold would repeat the decisively-rejected `SWEG_GUNLINE_HOLD` / `SWEG_SCREEN_AI` / `SWEG_MASS_BODIES` family). The two losses (Imperial Knights 51-82, Necrons 31-57) trace to matchup/list shape — an 18-36 model archetype anchored on four expensive Greater Daemons losing the attrition race to a denser, faster, or more durable opponent — not a piloting defect; the one win (Orks 63-44) shows the same Greater Daemons holding and shooting for 20+ damage per activation from round 2. OBSERVED BUT UNRESOLVED (flagged, not chased): in all three games EVERY Scintillating Legion unit deployed on-board round 1 despite all seven checked unit keys carrying `deep_strike=True` — the deploy-AI's 50%-of-points reserves cap (`SWEG_DEPLOY_AI`, `simulator.py` ~9383) may be structurally blocked by this archetype's top-heavy Greater-Daemon cost distribution, echoing the line-213 note that the reserve split is faction-blind; whether real Scintillating Legion play's flexible Daemonic Ritual arrival timing is being lost to this is a genuine open thread but requires its own instrument, not built here. Null result is valid and expected per this wave's brief: both factions sit near the noise floor (Chaos Daemons gated residual ≈0.4 against the live 52.6 real target — the 50.8 hand-transcribed figure this wave's brief quoted is likewise the stale table `scripts/evaluate_vs_meta.py` superseded). No code changed for Chaos Daemons this wave.

- Emperor's Children list realism 2 (`SWEG_EC_LIST2`, ADOPTED default-on 2026-07-08, anchor sc58a gated 3.01) — sourced composition corrections (2nd winged Daemon Prince, previously structurally capped at 1 by the fill logic; 3x Infractors; 2x Rhinos; 1x Tormentors; Sorcerer dropped; Lord Exultant seeding-order fix). Adopted fidelity-first (five independent tournament sources); screens MEASURED mildly positive but non-decisive at every N (N=20 +0.89 / N=40 +2.18 / N=80 +0.76, EC 45.2 → 45.9 toward 53.3, gated 3.08 → 3.01); promotion reproduction check 0-flip.
- Aeldari fate-fidelity pair (`SWEG_AELDARI_FATE_FAITHFUL` + `SWEG_AELDARI_BF_DISCARD`) — BUILT + HELD default-off 2026-07-08, both faithful (verbatim rule texts cited) and both FLAT (scoped N=20: −0.01 with 85 flips netting zero; 0 flips): the six-die Fate pool was always the binding constraint, so the per-squad-budget over-modelling never mattered. Fold in as free fidelity at the next re-anchor; the Aeldari over-pole (+6.4 symmetrized) SURVIVES this audit and remains open (thin suspects: Blitzing Firepower 12-inch gate, or unhypothesized).

- Side roll-off (`SWEG_SIDE_ROLLOFF`, ADOPTED default-on 2026-07-08, anchor sc59a gated 2.85 A-frame / 2.62 sym) — per-game deployment-side roll-off in the eval harness (slot swap + winner re-orientation), fixing the measured ±5-point per-faction positional estimator bias at source (all five rotation maps verified 180-degree symmetric; the fixed side assignment was the unfaithful piece). MEASURED: N=20 prediction test landed on the symmetrized estimator; N=80 re-anchor collapsed all 22 factions' side bias to within ±1.7 and erased the Chaos Daemons pole (0.95/0.00 — it was measurement artifact); committed-default reproduction 0-flip. Riders adopted in the same re-anchor: `SWEG_AELDARI_FATE_FAITHFUL` + `SWEG_AELDARI_BF_DISCARD` (both fidelity-faithful, flat-screened).

## REVERTED / REJECTED (tried, made it worse or proven wrong — do NOT re-attempt)
- Astra Militarum tank forward-staging (`SWEG_AM_STAGE_FORWARD`, a `pick_move_intent` branch = FREECONTEST minus its shootable-from-marker guard, scoped AM + role HEAVY + VEHICLE + not-indirect) — BUILT + SCREENED + REJECTED + REVERTED 2026-07-14 (anchor sc65a). Instrument-before-build CLEARED the frequency gate (`scripts/_am_tank_camp_probe`: 73% of AM battle tanks never touch a marker all game; 70% of scoring-round states off-all-markers with a reachable non-AM marker — vs the ~4% that flat-screened Cadia Stands), so it was worth screening. N=8 looked +1.1 for AM (noise, CI 2.98). **N=40 paired vs sc65a DECISIVE: Astra Militarum −0.91 (29.8 → 28.8, DOWN, away from real 45.3), gated MAE 2.22 → 2.37 (+0.15 WORSE); only CI-clear movers Adeptus Astartes +1.37 and Death Guard +0.71 UP.** Off byte-identical by construction (branch inside `if env=="1"`). MECHANISM + LESSON: **AM tanks camping and shooting is CORRECT play, not a misplay** — forcing them onto forward objectives exposes them off their fire lines and the durable factions (Astartes / Death Guard) punish it, so the built-for faction gets WORSE. The AM under-pole is NOT a tank-positioning AI gap; with chase_vp (already −0.65, HELD) and the four prior AM piloting channels, the **AM tank / objective-AI lane is exhausted**. The productive thread is an implementation-mistake hunt on how AM's BODIES lose (infantry midfield exchange vs durable shooting), not more tank AI. Branch reverted (bandage removed per the systematic-not-bandages preference; strategy.py back to sc65a state). See [[project-ai-frozen-under-mae-first]].
- Astra Militarum charge-discipline (`SWEG_AM_CHARGE_DISCIPLINE`, the pinned faction-agnostic SWEG_CHARGE_DISCIPLINE block — suppress charges whose expected melee damage is below 0.5 — army-scoped to Astra Militarum per the Principle-2 recipe) — BUILT + SCREENED + REJECTED + REVERTED 2026-07-01. AM-scoped N=80 vs sc34a: **Astra Militarum −2.41 DECISIVE (38.1 → 35.7, AWAY from real 45.3), gated 2.02 → 2.17 (+0.15 WORSE)**; only other mover Grey Knights −0.28. Off-arm byte-identical (0 flips) before the screen. Unlike the advance-discipline sibling (+5.75), blocking "futile" charges strictly hurts the built-for faction: a no-damage charge still ties a melee threat in Engagement Range, eats its activation, and contests ground — the sim's Astra Militarum converts those charges into value. Same empirical class as the rejected officer front-line. A do-not-re-attempt comment now sits on the generic block in code/simulator.py _do_charge.
- Grey Knights Force Edge (`SWEG_GK_FORCE_EDGE`, Brotherhood Terminator Squad melee AP+1 vs non-VEHICLE/MONSTER, name-scoped in Unit.attack) — BUILT + REVERTED 2026-07-01 as INERT: 0 flips even gate-ON (the Teleport Strike Force archetype's Terminators evidently do little relevant melee, or deep-strike late). Also low value: GK is only 0.69 gated (field-weighted 41.8 vs real 46.7, noise floor 4.22). Not worth pursuing; the other GK candidates (Hammerhand, Attuned Onslaught) are the same low-gated faction and were not attempted. LESSON: verify a name-scoped datasheet lever actually FIRES (instrument or check flip count > 0) before investing — and check the faction's GATED (not raw) residual, since a high noise floor can make a big raw gap nearly worthless.
- Astra Militarum officer front-line (`SWEG_AM_OFFICER_FRONT_LINE`, suppress the DUAL melee-charge intent in pick_move_intent for AM OFFICER units in AM_OFFICER_NAMES so they stay within 6" to keep issuing Voice-of-Command Orders) — BUILT + REJECTED + REVERTED 2026-07-01. AM-scoped N=80 vs sc28a: **Astra Militarum −2.19 (39.5 → 37.3, AWAY from real ~45), gated 2.43 → 2.57 (+0.14 WORSE)**, 13 of 21 matchups dipped (Adeptus Astartes −8.12, Aeldari −6.25, Imperial Knights −5.00). Holding officers back from melee makes them PASSIVE — the "preserve the Order economy" theory does not pan out (the existing SWEG_OFFICER_FOLLOW already pulls back drifted officers; forcing officers to never charge just removes their melee contribution for no Order-economy gain). A faithful-sounding piloting change that empirically HURTS the built-for faction. Do NOT re-attempt. strategy.py reverted to the committed pick_move_intent. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- T'au guided-fire target-bias (`_tau_guided_target_bonus` / SWEG_TAU_GUIDED_TARGET_BIAS, a 2x shooting-target score multiplier concentrating fire on the Spotted / Markerlit target, companion to the ADOPTED [IGNORES COVER] rule) — BUILT + REJECTED + REMOVED 2026-07-01. T'au-scoped N=80 vs sc24a: rule-only gated 2.50 → 2.49, but rule+bias gated 2.50 → 2.51 (**+0.02 WORSE**) because the bias OVER-CONCENTRATES fire — 137 → 279 flips, with decisive collateral Adeptus Mechanicus −0.60 and Drukhari −0.61 as T'au beats them harder. **THE BATCH LESSON (target-selection biases):** a target-selection bias that raises a near-50 faction's win rate necessarily LOWERS its opponents' (closed-matrix symmetry), so it washes or drags the metric even when it lifts the built-for faction; the faithful RULE the bias exploits is the value, not the bias. This predicts the PURE-bias levers — Leagues of Votann Prioritised-Efficiency target-bias (SWEG_VOTANN_PE_TARGET_BIAS) and Chaos Knights dread-cascade targeting — will also drag/wash (screen them, but expect no adoption); the rule-bearing Adeptus Custodes Assemblage of Might is the one with faithful-rule value. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Wave-259 Chaos Knights cluster — ALL THREE REFUTED (built, screened, NOT adopted; code stashed `ck-cluster-refuted-wave259`, recoverable; specs in `data/_lever_specs.json` keys 2/6/10). The Wahapedia-primary audit's three hypotheses for the **Chaos-Knights-under (−7.2) while same-chassis Imperial-Knights-over (+6.8)** gap ALL FAILED: (1) Infernal Lance / Malefic Surge `SWEG_CK_INFERNAL_LANCE` — **−8.66 DECISIVE WORSE** (Chaos Knights 37.3 → 28.6). The screen first read 0 flips (byte-inert) because the curated Chaos Knights archetype PINS Iconoclast Fiefdom (`self.detachment` set), defeating the `is None` guard; fixing the `resolve_detachment` override to force Infernal Lance revealed it CRATERS Chaos Knights — Iconoclast's Dread Tyrants re-roll-1s aura is far stronger, and the Malefic Surge mandatory self-mortal-wounds (D3 on a failed Leadership test) outweigh the [SUSTAINED HITS 1] gain. The minimal model wires only the Diabolic Power offensive leg + the downside (not Unnatural Fortitude 5++ / Unholy Hunger +3"), but −8.66 is decisive: do NOT swap the Chaos Knights default to Infernal Lance without BOTH a fuller Malefic Surge model AND the competitive-list citation the verifier already flagged unverifiable. (2) Knight Abominant Warp Storms + Vortex Terrors `SWEG_CK_ABOMINANT` (−0.28 wash — faithful psychic, fires, no net help). (3) War Dog datasheet abilities (Huntsman/Stalker/Executioner/Brigand/Karnivore) `SWEG_CK_WARDOG_ABILITIES` (−0.00, 400-flip churn for zero net).
