CONCLUSION: the Chaos Knights under-pole is NOT a detachment/ability-modelling gap — it is structural (archetype composition or representation) and remains the open Chaos Knights question. All five lever specs verbatim-confirmed on Wahapedia and not phantoms — they are simply faithful-yet-unhelpful (or, for Infernal Lance, faithful-yet-worse-than-the-better-detachment).
- Wave-253 combat-model / scoring fidelity sweep — ALL REJECTED (faithful but wrong-direction or wash; do NOT re-attempt). The leverage is ONLY "remove durable-survivor over-credit" (the adopted tie-draw); these screened worse: round-5 going-second end-of-turn scoring `SWEG_R5_SECOND_LAST` (+0.35 — faithful per Chapter Approved 2025-26 Determine Victor, but it INFLATES durable round-5 survivors; the simulator's command-phase round-5 scoring is a load-bearing compensating error. CORRECTS the earlier "round-5 second-player-last-turn already modelled" phantom — the turn-ORDER was modelled, the scoring-TIMING was not, but fixing the timing regresses). Melee defender wound-allocation continuation `SWEG_MELEE_DEFENDER_ALLOC` (+0.42 — the defender-optimising allocator helps everyone symmetrically and washes). Cover save-cap `SWEG_COVER_SAVECAP` (+0.01 — real ±1-save-modifier-cap bug, cover + a +1-save ability stacked to an illegal +2, but too rare in the matchups to matter). Variable-Attacks roll `SWEG_ROLLATK` (+0.18 — regresses to the mean like rolled-damage). Secondary-denial movement-intent `SWEG_SECONDARY_DENY` (+0.45 — denying position secondaries hits mobile/shooting armies, widening the melee-over/shooting-under spread). Astra Militarum Leman Russ **Armoured Spearhead** lift `SWEG_AM_SPEARHEAD` (+0.06 — faithful, BSData-scoped to the Leman Russ Battle Tank only, both legs modelled; the extra tank hits do NOT convert to wins — the Astra Militarum under-representation is NOT a tank-ability gap). The workflow's `[TURRET WEAPON]` +1-to-hit candidate was a PHANTOM (mis-recalled 9th-edition Grinding Advance; the 10e datasheet has no such ability) — caught by Rule-10 Wahapedia grounding. HELD (built/proven, NOT adopted, NOT rejected): first-player RNG-stream `SWEG_FIRSTPLAYER_STABLE` — independent matchup+seed first-player draw for robust screening, but NOT mean-absolute-error-neutral (~+0.7; the headline is first-player-sensitive because going-first is over-rewarded), so it adopts only alongside a working going-first fix, which does not yet exist. The Astra Militarum UNDER-pole (~26.5) is the dominant residual and has no found faithful lever — it needs list/representation strategy (parked, a user decision), not autonomous lever-hunting.
- Fight alternation, BOTH variants — variant (a) full-doubling refuted pre-wave-220 (melee-disabled frame, weak evidence) and variant (b) round-scoped once-per-round REJECTED wave 245 on strong evidence: paired N=40 vs the sc10a anchor (18,480 matched games) read gated 6.77 → 7.96 (+1.19), decisive wrong-direction movers World Eaters +5.89 / Imperial Knights +6.58 / Adeptus Custodes +5.41 / Chaos Space Marines +4.62 / Drukhari −6.46. Extra fight activations inflate a melee surface the sim already over-rewards; the melee over-pole is NOT a fight-frequency under-model. Gate + code path deleted at wave-245 close per the housekeeping prevention rule.
- Dark Pacts coverage experiment — wave 209, REVERTED. The Chaos Space Marines under-output is NOT Dark-Pacts-coverage.
- Candidate A clustering-geometry positional fix — wave 94, REGRESSED 4.15 → 4.30 (helped over-shooters that already hold markers, not Imperial Knights / Daemons), reverted. See [[project-matchup-fidelity-diagnosis]].
- Squad-activation contained decision-overlay — wave 64-65, WASH (9.27 → 9.30); only the spillover half landed. See [[project-squad-activation-contained-wash]].
- Threat-priority targeting alone — wave 189, wrong-direction (Imperial Knights 25.52 → 28.21); the over-pole is compound.
- Anti-tank strength / squad-size maxing — wave 190, made Imperial Knights WORSE; the "kill the Knight harder" axis is exhausted.
- Fab band-aid on thin archetypes — dropping fabrications from under-performers WORSENS mean absolute error (they were compensating for archetype thinness). See [[project-fab-bandaid-on-thin-archetypes]].
- Primary-mission rotation as the over-pole lever — wave 187, REFUTED.
- Necrons Protocol of the Conquering Tyrant half-range gate (`SWEG_NECRON_CT_HALFRANGE`) — BUILT + REJECTED + REVERTED 2026-06-15 (Necrons-scoped N=80 into sc12a). The codex gates the hit-reroll on targets within half range; the sim drops the gate. Added a dispatch-time half-range proxy that does NOT fire the stratagem (saving the command point) when the attacker is beyond half range. WRONG-DIRECTION: Necrons 60.9 -> 63.3 (paired +2.46 indeterminate), headline 5.78 -> 5.91 (+0.13 WORSE). MECHANISM: saving the command point lets the AI spend it on a MORE valuable stratagem → Necrons stronger. So always-on Conquering Tyrant was a suboptimal CP sink, NOT an inflation — it is NOT the Necron +4.19 over-pole driver (REFUTED). META-LESSON (predicts the Aeldari Blitzing Firepower 12"-gate candidate will also backfire): range-gating a stratagem's FIRING is wrong-direction for an over-pole because the saved command point reallocates to a better stratagem. Clean fabrication-removal only works when there is NO CP reallocation — a passive over-applied buff (Grey Knights Fury detachment) or a keep-the-CP-spend-drop-the-effect stratagem (Aeldari Fire and Fade). The +4.19 Necron over-pole is elsewhere (representation, or another mechanic).

- Drukhari Ravager count 1 → 3 (wave-245 diagnostic candidate) — REJECTED unbuilt: the diagnostic agent's "real triple-Ravager meta" claim is unsourced and CONTRADICTS the template's documented composition (`code/archetypes.py` DRK-ARCH-1 comment: Warp Friends ~848-game Skysplinter lists run 3 Raiders + 1 Venom + 1 Ravager). Raising it to chase the win rate = forbidden metric-tuning. The list is faithful; the Drukhari under-residual is sim fidelity — see the Power from Pain open lever.
- Squad-level pre-game embark — wave 246, REJECTED + DELETED (revert `6cb1883`): paired N=40 read gated 8.36 (+1.61) with SIX decisive wrong-direction movers (T'au −9.48, Drukhari −8.85, Leagues of Votann −6.28 further under; Chaos Space Marines +6.63, Tyranids +6.07, Orks +5.04 further over). Whole-squad embark denies first-round shooting and board presence with no disembark-timing intelligence to recover the tempo. A piloting policy, not a codex rule — fidelity-first protection does not apply. The transport-embark lever family is closed as measured-rejected on this frame.
- Defensive gunline-hold (`SWEG_GUNLINE_HOLD`, fragile T<=4 INFANTRY shooters hold in cover vs an in-charge-reach melee threat instead of advancing) — REJECTED + DELETED 2026-06-15 (`diag_massbodies_ab` panel A/B): made the gunlines WORSE — Astra Militarum dWin -6.2 (15.6 -> 9.4), T'au -7.9, while the mobile melee gained (Chaos Daemons +9.5). LESSON: holding back makes a gunline PASSIVE — it cedes the board and the (often mobile) melee takes every objective; Astra Militarum must CONTEST, not hide. The visual fix is to make contesting bodies SURVIVE (screen) or KILL the melee on approach (focus-fire), NOT to hold. CANDIDATE banked (not built): `_highest_threat_enemy`'s ROLE_THREAT weights MELEE 1.0 / HORDE 0.6 below HEAVY 3.0 / SHOOTY 2.0, so a gunline DE-prioritises the approaching melee horde about to charge it — a focus-fire-on-incoming-melee lever is worth a future A/B (verify it reaches the MAIN shoot picker, not just stratagem targeting). See [[project-am-melee-coupling-visual-diag]].
- Focus-fire on the approaching melee (`SWEG_FOCUS_MELEE`, shoot-picker bias toward a high-melee enemy within Move+12") — BUILT + REJECTED + DELETED 2026-06-15 (`diag_massbodies_ab` panel A/B). The candidate above, built: the main shoot picker (`_do_shoot` ~11297, lowest-HP + screen/synapse/oath/transport biases) genuinely has no approaching-melee bias, so the gap was real — but focusing fire on it is WORSE: Astra Militarum dWin +0.0 (no help), the gunlines dropped (Adeptus Astartes -9.7, T'au -5.0), and the DURABLE melee GAINED (World Eaters +7.9, Death Guard +4.7). Reason: the melee over-pole is too durable to shoot off — focusing it wastes the gunline's fire on hard targets that survive anyway, ceding chaff-clearing and objectives. META-CONCLUSION (updated 2026-06-15, now FIVE levers — see screening below): the AI-lever family is EXHAUSTED on the melee over-pole / Astra Militarum under-pole — FIVE independent levers (mass-bodies, mass-tanks, gunline-hold, focus-fire, screening) all wash or backfire because the residual is the durability-in-contest REPRESENTATION/SCORING wall (off-table), not a positioning or targeting gap. You cannot out-position, out-hold, out-shoot, or out-screen a melee army the scoring over-rewards for winning contested markers. STOP chasing AI levers here; the floor for these poles needs the off-table representation work OR a re-anchor folding in the held faithful fixes. See [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]] and [[project-am-melee-coupling-visual-diag]].
- Defensive screening move-AI (`SWEG_SCREEN_AI`, a cheap INFANTRY body moves to interpose on the charge lane between an enemy melee threat and a valuable friendly gun/character — the "remaining faithful AM lever" flagged at the SWEG_AM_RECON park) — BUILT + REJECTED + DELETED 2026-06-15 (`diag_screen_ab` one-sided causal A/B, gunlines × melee, OFF byte-identical proven via `sim_motion_proof.py`). Substrate SUPPORTS it (SWEG_CHARGE_PATH already excludes a target whose approach passes within Engagement Range of a non-target enemy; the gap was purely the missing defensive MOVE). The lever FIRES actively (Astra Militarum 6.7 SCREEN intents/game, Votann 14.1) and is DECISIVELY WRONG-DIRECTION: every gunline WORSE (Astra Militarum 25.0 -> 16.7 = -8.3, T'au -4.2, Votann -4.3 despite the most screening) and the melee over-pole BETTER (Death Guard / World Eaters / Emperor's Children all +8.3, Orks +16.7). `_screen_blocks` verifies each screen genuinely blocks the charge → real backfire, not a bug. MECHANISM = the durability-in-contest wall: AM's cheap bodies ARE its objective-holders, so pulling one back to interpose cedes board presence; the melee charges the fragile screen, kills it, and takes the abandoned objectives anyway. The sim cannot separate dedicated screen-chaff from objective-bodies (no surplus chaff in the representation), so even screening loses the objective game. This RESOLVES the open "remaining faithful AM lever" note — screening tested, rejected; the AM under-pole is now confirmed off-table-only. See [[project-cover-symmetric-tax-refuted]] and [[project-am-melee-coupling-visual-diag]].
- In-range body-massing (`SWEG_MASS_BODIES`, low-ranged-DPA bodies mass onto objectives even in firing range) — REJECTED + DELETED 2026-06-14 (`diag_massbodies_ab`, paired N=64): the dominant under-pole Astra Militarum did NOT gain (presence −0.3, win −4.7) while the DURABLE over-pole rose (Death Guard +9.5 win, Chaos Daemons +2.5 presence). KEY INSIGHT: massing bodies onto objectives is a DURABILITY-biased tactic — fragile Astra Militarum Guardsmen pushed onto in-range (= near-enemy) markers are shot off before they score, while durable Plague Marines survive to hold; so it rewards the over-pole's strength and WIDENS the gap. The reframed Astra Militarum diagnosis: it under-contests not because its bodies don't try, but because they are too fragile to hold CONTESTED markers; its real board control is uncontested-marker count + screening + Orders, which the sim under-models. Was the `SWEG_MASS` PARKED-lever reopen attempt (new info = the cited Astra Militarum case); reopen now closed.
- Shoot uncontested objective-holders (`SWEG_SHOOT_HOLDERS`, the shoot-picker finishes the most-wounded enemy unit standing on ANY marker, NON-anti-armour weapons only) — BUILT + REJECTED + DELETED 2026-06-27 (pilot-loop study, `docs/PILOT_FINDINGS.md`). The pilot harness found this is the ONLY asymmetric under-pole lift (+2.5pp Astra Militarum vs Imperial Knights, army-A-only, one-directional) — but the faithful SYMMETRIC production gate REGRESSES the metric: N=80 paired vs sc17a gated 3.45 → 3.61 (+0.16 WRONG-DIRECTION), inflating the durable-brick over-poles (Death Guard +1.83, Chaos Space Marines +1.62, Necrons +1.1, Tyranids +1.5 — their bricks survive because opponents shoot chaff holders off markers) while the under-poles do NOT lift (Astra Militarum −0.2 flat, Drukhari −1.2 down). SAME over-pole-favouring asymmetry as the wave-189 / `SWEG_FOCUS_MELEE` / five-lever family — clearing chaff protects durable bricks. Adversarial verification (5-agent `verify-shoot-holders`) predicted this from the ledger before the screen AND caught an anti-tank-misdirection bug in the first cut (the corrected non-anti-armour version was the one screened). CONFIRMS the structural conclusion a third way (after the wave-260 self-veto and a human pilot failing to out-play it): piloting cannot close the under-pole gap; the on-table AI-lever family is exhausted. Byte-identity restored on revert. See [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]].
- Going-first / tempo going-second mechanisms — ALL REJECTED 2026-06-29 (do NOT re-propose; the going-first axis is now a declared structural floor, re-confirming the durability floor from the tempo angle). The simulator's going-first win rate is ~69% (N=150) versus a real Chapter Approved 2025-26 target of 49-52%, and it is BROAD across all five deployments, not a single bad map (`scripts/diag_going_first_by_map.py`: 64.9-84.5% at N=60/map; worst Hammer and Anvil 84.5%, amplified by its 36-inch travel distance; best Crucible 64.9%). Three faithful going-second mechanisms each REDUCE going-first but REGRESS the headline by transferring wins to the durable over-poles — paired vs the sc18a anchor at N=80: **`SWEG_OVERWATCH_MOVE`** (Fire Overwatch on a Normal/Advance move ending within 24 inches — completes the verbatim-cited `simulator.fire_overwatch` trigger the code only honoured on charges and reserve arrivals) **+0.40** (deflates aggressors Death Guard −1.5 / Chaos Daemons −1.3 but inflates the durable holders that overwatch, Emperor's Children +3.2 decisive); **`SWEG_KITE_MOVE`** (objective-aware kiting retreat) **+1.12** (shooters retreat, durable melee walks onto objectives — Imperial Knights / Thousand Sons / Tyranids up); **`SWEG_PROBE_RESERVE`** (general Strategic Reserves de-risk probe — reserve ~half the army and arrive Round 2+ to counter-attack) **+1.67, the worst** (Imperial Knights +12.6, Chaos Knights +14.4, Orks +15.9; gunlines cratered T'au −7.6 / Chaos Space Marines −8.1). The going-first over-reward IS the durability over-reward viewed through tempo: durable armies win the objective snapshot whether you attack the tempo via movement, reactive fire, or reserves — so no going-second mechanism is mean-absolute-error-positive. DISPOSITION: `SWEG_OVERWATCH_MOVE` KEPT built + held (a genuine fidelity correction, default-off, no new citation key); `SWEG_KITE_MOVE` already in tree (recovered earlier); `SWEG_PROBE_RESERVE` REMOVED (rejected measurement probe). Also shipped default-off this campaign: the Sweeping Engagement deployment restored at the correct 2000-point 44×60 footprint behind `SWEG_FULL_DEPLOY_ROTATION` (the stock map is 44×90, the wrong size for 2K, and so was excluded from the five-map rotation — adopting the six-map rotation is a frame-change requiring an N=80 re-anchor). See `docs/CURRENT_STATE.md` head + `docs/DURABILITY_OVERREWARD_INVESTIGATION.md`.
- Tank-screen — durable shoot-in-melee VEHICLE/MONSTER holds to soak a charge (`SWEG_TANK_SCREEN`, the hold fires when an enemy MELEE/DUAL unit is within 18 inches) — BUILT + REJECTED + DELETED 2026-06-30. The user's idea: a tank that keeps firing in melee (Big Guns Never Tire) interposes to soak the charge so the fragile gunline behind it survives to shoot — a tank-specific variant of the refuted wave-251 `SWEG_SCREEN_AI` body-screen. Paired N=40 vs sc20a: gated mean absolute error 3.32 → 3.82 (**+0.49 WRONG-DIRECTION**) with violent ±10pp swings — it HELPS gun-platform factions whose tanks screen well (T'au +6.2, Leagues of Votann +6.3, Adeptus Mechanicus +5.9, Tyranids +6.4, Genestealer Cults +9.7, Grey Knights +5.0) but CRATERS the factions whose durable units are MELEE monsters that should charge, not hold (Emperor's Children −9.7, Chaos Knights −9.4, Imperial Knights −8.0, Death Guard −5.3 — a predicate confound: `classify` returns DUAL/HEAVY not MELEE for Mortarion / Maulerfiend / War Dogs / Hive Tyrant). DECISIVE: Astra Militarum, the TARGET under-pole, went DOWN −3.94 — holding its Leman Russ tanks does NOT save its infantry gunline, it just forfeits the tank's mobility and objective pressure, FALSIFYING the core hypothesis. Even a clean predicate (excluding the melee monsters) would keep the chaotic gun-tank swings and the AM regression. Joins the metric-blocked piloting family (the durable-survivor over-reward + the refuted SCREEN_AI). Byte-identity restored on revert. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Astra Militarum advance-discipline "advance-when-far-from-objective" refinement (`needs_to_close > 1.5 * normal_move` un-suppresses the hold) — BUILT + REJECTED + REVERTED 2026-07-01. Motivated by an Astra-Militarum-vs-Chaos-Daemons trace showing gunline tanks hold 10–18" from centre objectives while Daemons score them uncontested; the refinement made distant tanks Advance-to-contest instead of hold-and-shoot. Re-screened N=100: it **COST THE ENTIRE LIFT** (Astra Militarum +5.75 → −2.00, BELOW the no-gate baseline 34.1 → 32.8; gated 2.64 → 3.03, +0.12 worse), turning 3 noise-scale dips into 17 real ones (Adeptus Custodes −8.00, Chaos Daemons −7.00, Adeptus Mechanicus −6.00, Thousand Sons −5.50). LESSON: the advance-discipline benefit IS the far-holding — a gunline tank far from its objective is exactly where holding to shoot pays most, and `needs_to_close > 1.5·normal_move` is the COMMON case, so un-suppressing it reverts to the harmful always-advance behaviour. The motivating Chaos Daemons dip was noise (self-cancelling, net −4/200, shrank with N). Do NOT re-attempt a distance-based un-suppress on the advance-discipline hold. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Chaos Daemons melee "score-what-you-hold" (`SWEG_MELEE_HOLD_OBJECTIVE`, a melee/DUAL unit standing on an objective it CONTROLS in rounds 4–5 holds instead of futile-charging a `_is_caging_brick` it cannot crack; catches the redundant holder the existing marginal-holder check misses) — BUILT + SCREENED + REFUTED 2026-07-04. Held default-off, byte-identical-off proven (0 flips, scoped Chaos-Daemons N=20 and full-frame N=20 both). Chaos-Daemons-scoped N=20 vs sc57a: **paired −0.03 (ci 0.60), gated 3.08 → 3.08 flat**, 15 games flip but net zero. MECHANISM (why flat): the round-by-round board (now-deterministic diag harness, seed 0 CD-vs-DG, a 51–57 loss) shows the fragile Daemon melee is NOT standing on controlled objectives — it is in OPEN GROUND futile-charging the durable target (Slaanesh swarm charging the Daemon Prince of Nurgle for 0, dying to DG fire; Khorne Bloodcrushers 10–15" off-objective charging the Plagueburst), so a "hold-what-you-stand-on" gate has nothing to bite. My initial one-seed board-read ("abandoning HELD objectives") was imprecise — the units never held. RE-DIAGNOSIS (the new hypothesis the refutation forced): the Chaos Daemons −8.4 under-pole (#2 under-pole after AM; sim 44.2 vs real 52.6, field-weighted) is a crater vs the DURABLE armies (Death Guard 36%, Imperial Knights 42%, Chaos Knights 42%) and is the FRAGILE-side mirror of the durability-over-reward wall — the archetype is sourced (5 sub-templates incl. the real Be'lakor Scintillating Legion), the anti-durability rules are faithful (Flamers "Flickering Flames" correctly has NO Devastating Wounds per BSData cache — a memory-based DW hypothesis REFUTED against source; Bloodthirster/Skarbrand if anything over-statted), Shadow of Chaos fires army-wide, and the god-legion detachment rules are honestly no-flag (Murdercall/Beguiling-Aura/Fates-in-Flux are transient/reactive with no schema hook). The live signal is the going-first asymmetry: Daemons 45.4% as army A vs 54.6% as B (−9.3, the largest negative A/B gap; global A/B is symmetric 49.5/48.9) — the durability-over-reward through tempo, the exact axis the 2026-06-29 going-second family (KITE +1.12 / OVERWATCH_MOVE +0.40 / PROBE_RESERVE +1.67) and `SWEG_STAGING` already proved metric-negative because the fixes inflate the durable over-poles. On-table AI-lever family EXHAUSTED for this pole too; it needs the off-table durability representation (Stage-2 owner fork), not another closing/tempo lever. Also this session: `scripts/diag_pilot_am_vs_ik.py` upgraded to reproduce the EXACT eval game (matches evaluate_vs_meta's `pair_seed` packing + seeds the global RNG + sets the primary mission) so pilot board-reads are deterministic run-to-run — the PILOT_PROTOCOL step-3 board-read now stands on a fixed game, not a fresh random one each render. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Chaos Daemons Principle-2 SCOPED PILOTING — the owner's "second piloting layer, faction-scoped so no cross-faction risk" thesis, TESTED for Daemons and found to have NO lever (2026-07-04). The architecture is sound and now BUILT: `"DAEMONS": "Chaos Daemons"` added to `simulator._SCOPED_LEVER_FACTION` (byte-identical off proven, 0 flips scoped N=20 vs sc57a — a Daemon army previously fell through the loop to `return False`, now matches and reads the default-off `SWEG_DAEMONS_<suffix>` to the same False). Rationale the thesis rests on and which HOLDS: Daemons' crater is concentrated in the DURABLE over-poles (DG 36/IK 42/CK 42), so a Daemon-scoped lift is doubly corrective in the closed matrix (Daemons up toward 52.6 AND those over-poles down in the Daemon cells), and a target-quality gate self-scopes to those over-pole matchups — unlike the faction-NEUTRAL family that fed the over-poles their own better play. BUT the empirics came back EMPTY: (1) `scripts/pilot_manual` battery, Daemons-as-A (rate-faithful, faction-scoped by construction) vs all three durable over-poles N=40 — **no charge/contest tactic robustly lifts.** `charge_futile` +0.0 across all three (the Daemon AI's futile charges are not the deciding loss); `surgical_contest` helped vs IK (+2.5, clean) but HURT vs DG (−7.5, the highest-weight worst matchup) and was flat vs CK — matchup-shape-dependent (IK is low-model elite so "one body flips it" applies; DG floods durable bodies so it doesn't), so it washes/regresses field-weighted; `kite`/`heavy_hold` catastrophic (−20 to −32, correctly — a melee swarm must not kite). (2) `SWEG_DAEMONS_STAGING` (the going-first over-extension lever, the −9.3 A/B-asymmetry candidate the battery can't test) screened **flat**: Chaos-Daemons-scoped N=20 vs sc57a **paired −0.05 (ci 1.42), 94 games change, net zero, gated 3.08 → 3.08** — staging fires actively on the fragile swarm but the churn is symmetric noise. CONCLUSION: Daemon-scoped piloting is exhausted on BOTH axes (target-selection AND positioning) — the pole is durability-representation, not a piloting gap, confirmed now from the faction-scoped angle too. The DAEMONS scope is KEPT as default-off Principle-2 infrastructure (the capstone's keep-rejected-scopes convention; SWEG_DAEMONS_NOMINATION/ANTITANK_HOLD are available but implausible for a melee/psychic army with negligible anti-tank shooting and were not screened — the battery's focus_threat/anti_knight_shoot were flat-to-negative). META for the "close in on 0 gated MAE via per-faction layers" thesis: the scoped-piloting layer DOES close piloting-gap poles (banked: AM_STAGING +1.66, GSC ×3 +3.38, ck_ranged_hold +11.27, votann_ranged_hold +10.37) but CANNOT close a durability-REPRESENTATION pole (Daemons here, AM before) — those need the off-table Stage-2 work. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].

## PARKED (not pursued now; needs NEW information to reopen — the line says what would reopen it)
- Order-issuer-by-squad `SWEG_ORDER_ISSUER_BY_SQUAD` — BUILT + HELD default-off 2026-07-14 (a genuine issuer-identity bug the 2026-07-07 AM deep audit MISSED). `code/orders.py::dispatch_orders` de-duplicated Voice-of-Command Order issuers by profile.NAME, silently collapsing two genuinely-DISTINCT same-datasheet Officer UNITS (two Cadian Command Squads / two Cadian Castellans) into one and dropping the second unit's entire Order allotment every Command phase — the same NAME-vs-squad_id class as the adopted `SWEG_LEADER_SQUAD_DEDUPE`. Fix re-keys the issuer dedup on `('squad', squad_id)` — the SAME key this function already uses for its target pool (orders.py:782) — so a 5-model Command Squad still folds to one issuer while two distinct squads each issue. Restores +18 issuers over 20 AM builds (~0.9/build, ~55% of games). Byte-identical off (else-branch is the prior name dedup verbatim); orders tests 68/68 green; run.py --cli + audit clean. SCREENED N=40 paired vs sc65a: **AM 29.8 → 30.3 (paired +0.52, ci 2.98 — SUB-DECISIVE; symmetrized 30.6→31.3, gatedSym 11.49→10.79 = −0.7 toward real); gated MAE 2.22→2.30 (+0.08); only CI-clear movers Grey Knights +0.69 / Drukhari +0.56 (AM-matchup collateral).** A real fidelity bug with a right-direction AM lift — but the AM read is ambiguous (the restored offensive/defensive orders hit the same objective-presence wall as the documented exhausted order channel: +1-to-hit lasguns / survive-there cover can't convert while AM holds markers ~10% of unit-rounds). HELD pending an N=80 confirm or a batch with a genuine board-presence lever; do NOT adopt on the ambiguous read. Confirmed keeper on FIDELITY grounds regardless. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Threat-range-aware staging discipline `SWEG_STAGING` — BUILT + HELD default-off (2026-07-02, the Gap-1 counterplay build; byte-identical off proven 0/168 flips vs base `1f9a8d0`; audit green, run.py --cli clean both states). The faction-neutral CLOSING-side counterpart of the ranged-hold family: a unit whose ENGAGE / CAPTURE / STEAL commit would end EXPOSED inside a significant enemy threat envelope (enemy Move + max(weapon range, charge reach), CAPPED at 30 inches so a board-wide gun does not blanket the map and freeze every closer — the rejected gunline-hold passivity that cap is built to avoid), while it cannot act this turn (charge home / claim the objective / bring a gun to bear) and no friendly mass is committing with it, STAGES at the envelope edge — the Advance sprint suppressed (for a melee unit the Advance would also forfeit its charge) and the Normal move capped at melee bubbles (ray-sphere) — but it STILL Normal-moves toward its goal, so board presence is never surrendered (the officer-front-line −2.19 / screen / gunline-hold passivity precedents). `Battle._precompute_staging_envelope` / `_staging_decision` / `_staging_can_act_this_turn` / `_staging_capped_target` in code/simulator.py `_do_move`; deterministic geometry over cached expected DPA, no RNG; cited `simulator.staging_discipline` (data/rule_citations.d/core_staging.json). Three overrides preserve mission play + coordination: (a) claiming/contesting an objective THIS turn or a live charge/shot commits freely (fall-back-only-when-wasted), (b) cheap chaff/screens are exempt, (c) a commit-mass release when friendly poised output ≥ 0.5 of the local enemy output. PILOT (4 games vs Chaos Knights `SWEG_CK_REALISM=1`, diverse closers): staging fires 13–50 events/game, heavily round 1 (Orks 45 units / Khorne Berzerkers / Screamers held at the threat edge instead of Advancing into the kill zone), tapering as units converge and commit; ALL events were forward-EDGE (0 frozen-in-place — board presence confirmed); Chaos Knights still won all four. CK-scoped probe N=20 (`SWEG_STAGING=1 SWEG_CK_REALISM=1`): Chaos Knights **86.5 → 87.5 (paired +0.95 AWAY from real 44.7, 143/840 games changed)** — staging does NOT close the gun-Knight gap; it slightly inflates the durable Knights (the ranged-hold family's failure mode: better opponent play helps the durable side, the durability-over-reward wall — durable-survivor over-reward confirmation, now from the closing side). Full-frame N=10 sanity: gated MAE 6.49 → 4.85 but raw mean |err| a WASH (6.98 → 6.83), 1512/4620 games changed, and the durable Knights inflate (Imperial Knights +6.4 / Chaos Knights +3.3 AWAY) while Death Guard −8.1 and Astra Militarum +6.0 move toward real — internally contradictory and high-variance at N=10, NOT robust. HELD default-off (the `SWEG_ADVANCE_DISCIPLINE` / artillery-hold / screen disposition class): faithful piloting, no empirical harm off, but does not close the diagnosed gap and shows the durable-Knight collateral. REOPEN: an N=80 re-anchor to test whether the gated-MAE improvement survives the noise, AND after the durability-over-reward is corrected structurally (staging turns net-positive only once the durable-survivor over-reward stops banking the opponents' better play). See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Focus-fire completion term `SWEG_FOCUS_FIRE` / `simulator.focus_fire_completion` — BUILT + HELD default-off (2026-07-03, the Gap-2 counterplay build's missing cross-activation half; byte-identical off proven by a `scripts/sim_motion_proof.py` fingerprint match against the unmodified base, sha256 `f858276c2792b7550d51eb1d9b8365577723e5e77b62f984cdb06ccc1ee964a9` identical before and after; audit green; run.py --cli clean). The cross-activation companion to the already-adopted `simulator.target_economics` factor (`code/simulator.py` `_target_economics_bonus`, around line 12661): a candidate already below its starting health (`target.current_health < target.profile.health` — 10e has no in-battle healing, so this is durable, monotonic proof friendly fire already landed on it, whether from this same army earlier this round or surviving over from a prior round) receives a bonus scaling from 1.0x (untouched) to 3.0x (near death) in both shooting pickers' `min()` target-priority chain (the squad split-fire planner `_plan_squad_fire`, around line 13121, and the per-model fallback picker in `_do_shoot`, around line 13555), gated on the same can-this-attacker-wound-it screen `_target_economics_bonus` uses (`_ranged_expected_wounds(attacker.profile, target) > 0`), so it composes with target economics even when `SWEG_TARGET_ECONOMICS` itself is left unset — a wounded target this weapon cannot scratch never gets an artificial score bump. CLASS-shaped by design (any already-wounded, currently-woundable candidate qualifies, never a single pre-designated unit), per the per-faction target-selection batch's lesson (Votann on-objective / Chaos Knights below-half class biases helped; the rejected T'au single-Spotted-target bias over-concentrated and dragged). Distinguished explicitly in the code comment from the already-REJECTED `SWEG_SHOOT_HOLDERS` (2026-06-27): that lever required standing on an objective and excluded anti-armour weapons; this one has neither restriction. PROBE (`SWEG_FOCUS_FIRE=1 SWEG_TARGET_ECONOMICS=1 SWEG_CK_REALISM=1`, `--use-archetype`, N=20, Chaos-Knights-scoped, 840 matched games via `scripts.paired_delta`): **Chaos Knights 87.5% → 88.5%, paired +1.05 (non-decisive, confidence interval ±1.39, 7 of 840 games flipped) — AWAY from real 44.7%**, the same durability-over-reward direction every other "faithful piloting improvement" in this family lands on (Staging, Chaos Knights/Votann ranged-hold, the Custodes offense batch). Companion pilot-log check (a throwaway 40-game fixed-seed instrument, Chaos Knights vs eight opponents at five seeds each, identical seeds both arms, counting TITANIC platforms dead per game): mean Knights-dead-per-game **UNCHANGED at 0.625 in both arms** (25 of 152 starting Titanic models dead each way) — the per-game distribution of deaths differs between arms (confirming the term genuinely fires and moves which specific games see a kill, consistent with the 7-flip paired result), but the aggregate kill rate does not rise. HONEST READING: the diagnosed scatter is real and this term measurably changes targeting behaviour, but making fire converge onto an already-wounded target does not by itself convert into more dead Knights at this sample size — either the pre-existing collective-crack gate (`SWEG_FOCUSFIRE`, default-on since wave 210, a different mechanism keyed on within-phase collective army capability rather than cross-activation health memory) was already capturing most of the achievable within-phase concentration, or the residual bottleneck is the same durability-in-contest substrate this whole "adopted-as-is, overshoots but improves the metric via opponent collateral" family keeps surfacing. HELD default-off pending a full N=80 re-anchor and a sharper instrument (a within-round target-concentration ratio would test the mechanism more directly than an end-of-game kill count at this small a sample). See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Persistent multi-round focus nomination `SWEG_PERSISTENT_NOMINATION` / `simulator.persistent_nomination` — BUILT + HELD default-off (2026-07-03, the capstone's second dispatched lever; byte-identical off proven by a `scripts/sim_motion_proof.py` fingerprint match against the unmodified base, sha256 `f858276c2792b7550d51eb1d9b8365577723e5e77b62f984cdb06ccc1ee964a9` identical with the gate unset AND with `=0`; audit green; run.py --cli clean both states). Reshapes the wave-101 collective focus-fire nominator (`Battle._nominate_focusfire_target`, code/simulator.py) — whose one-phase crack gate demands ~0.85 × 26 ≈ 22 expected wounds in a SINGLE Shooting phase, a bar durable bricks almost never clear, so it never fires and anti-tank falls to the lowest-health min-picker (the capstone activation-allocation finding) — into a CROSS-ROUND commitment: the new `Battle._nominate_persistent_target` (branch taken BEFORE the SWEG_FOCUSFIRE check, so the off path is untouched) nominates the highest-threat brick whose summed one-round expected wounds can crack it within `_PERSISTENT_CRACK_ROUNDS` = 3 rounds, PERSISTS the nominee on new army-level state `Army._persistent_nom_uid` (initialised in code/army.py; unlike `_focusfire_target_uid` it is NOT reset at Shooting-phase start) across rounds until the brick dies / stops being a brick / becomes un-woundable, then re-nominates the next brick. The contest-pool interaction the capstone flagged (`pool = contesting or candidates` in `_do_shoot`, which DROPS an off-objective Knight from every picker's pool and silently un-points the commitment) is fixed under the same gate: an off-objective nominee the acting model can legally reach is re-admitted to the pool. CLASS-shaped, not army-wide (the batch lesson): the default-on squad split-fire planner concentrates only `_is_antiarmour_weapon` models on the nominee; small arms keep their normal economy. Deterministic, no new random numbers. Cited `simulator.persistent_nomination` (data/rule_citations.d/persistent_nomination.json, the `simulator.focus_fire` / `target_economics` / `focus_fire_completion` artificial-intelligence-piloting family; registered in scripts/audit_rules.py). PROBE (a) (`SWEG_PERSISTENT_NOMINATION=1 SWEG_CK_REALISM=1`, `--use-archetype`, N=20, Chaos-Knights-scoped, 840 matched games via `scripts.paired_delta`): **Chaos Knights 85.3% → 86.7%, paired +1.37 (non-decisive, confidence interval ±3.51, 45 of 840 games changed) — AWAY from real 44.7%**, the same durability-over-reward direction the whole faithful-piloting family lands on. PROBE (b) Knights-dead-per-game (throwaway fixed-seed instrument, Chaos Knights vs eight diverse opponents × 12 seeds = 96 games per arm, identical seeds both arms, TITANIC platforms dead per game, `SWEG_CK_REALISM=1`): **0.448 OFF → 0.365 ON (43/376 → 35/376 started Titanic models) — DOWN, not up.** THE MECHANISM PROVABLY FIRES AND POINTS: a nomination-firing instrument (both sides run the gate) recorded the field nominating a Chaos Knights TITANIC in **237/240 (98.8 percent) of its shooting phases** and persisting onto it (the Chaos Knights side nominated an opposing brick in 92.5 percent) — the never-fires problem of the one-phase gate is CLOSED at the mechanism level; the field now commits its anti-tank to one Knight across rounds exactly as the doctrine describes. But the pointing STILL does not convert: the lever is symmetric, and the durable side banks the better play (Chaos Knights' own persistent pointing kills the opponents' crackable bricks faster than the field can grind a 26-wound chassis), so Knights die LESS and Chaos Knights win MORE — direct empirical confirmation of the capstone's honest ceiling: the remaining anti-Knight residual is attrition + range (the field's anti-tank dies or lacks reach before three committed rounds elapse), the durability/standoff structural wall, NOT pointing. PROBE (c) full-frame sanity N=10 (production default vs gate-on, paired, 4620 matched games): **gated mean absolute error 6.48 → 5.21 (−1.27)**; decisive movers Death Guard −8.63 (67.4 → 58.7, TOWARD real 47.6) and Leagues of Votann −8.75 (59.6 → 50.8, TOWARD real 48.0) — the ranged-hold over-poles deflate when their opponents commit anti-tank persistently — but **Astra Militarum −8.33 (26.2 → 17.9, AWAY from real 45.3): the deepest under-pole DEEPENS, its durable Leman Russ hulls becoming every opponent's standing nominee — the durable-banking watch confirmed**; the Knights themselves barely move (Imperial Knights +1.76 away, Chaos Knights −2.81 toward, both non-decisive). Headline improvement by redistribution with a decisive worsening on the headline under-pole — the family signature, not adoption evidence at N=10. HELD default-off (the `SWEG_ADVANCE_DISCIPLINE` / staging / focus-fire-completion disposition class): faithful piloting, byte-identical off, closes the diagnosed pointing gap mechanically, but does not close the gun-Knight over-pole and banks to the durable side. REOPEN after the durability-over-reward is corrected structurally — this lever, like every play-quality improvement in the family, turns net-positive only once the durable-survivor over-reward stops banking the opponents' better play. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Cadia Stands! `SWEG_AM_CADIA_STANDS` — BUILT + HELD default-off 2026-07-07 (the AM deep-audit's top unbuilt faithful lever). Wires the previously-inert Cadian Command Squad LeaderAbility (`leaders.py:1535`): a Cadian Shock Troops squad led by its Command Squad officer, on an objective its army CONTROLS, gains Benefit of Cover (+1 save, single-pip, ranged-only) against ranged attacks. Precomputed per-round as `Unit._cadia_stands_active` in `simulator.py` round-setup (led-by-Command-Squad via `leaders.in_range_leaders` proximity proxy + controlled-objective via `_obj_controller_at_round_start`), read on the save path in `code/units.py` beside the Recon-Element cover source. Verbatim rule re-verified on Wahapedia this session (the old "5+ invuln" code comment was stale); cited `simulator.cadia_stands` (data/rule_citations.d/astra_militarum.json), audit green, run.py --cli clean, byte-identical off (0 flips, scoped AM N=20). SCREENED FLAT: scoped AM N=20 vs sc57a **+0.03 (1 flip)**; N=40 combined with `SWEG_AM_STAGING=0` **+0.08**. WHY INERT (the audit's key structural finding): the lever protects Cadian Shock Troops **while they stand on a controlled objective**, but instrumentation shows AM units are on an objective only **~10% of unit-rounds** (Cadian Shock Troops ~4% at defaults, ~9% with the advance-suppression family off) — AM barely establishes objective PRESENCE, so a survive-on-the-objective buff has almost nothing to protect. Same wall the offensive-order channel hits from the other side: it is not that AM's bodies die on objectives, it is that they are rarely THERE. Held (the `SWEG_REEMBARK` disposition), faithful, preserved. REOPEN once AM's upstream objective-presence problem is fixed (bodies advance onto and hold objectives) — then the survive-there buff has something to bite. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- Chase-VP target-priority `SWEG_AM_CHASE_VP` — BUILT + HELD default-off 2026-07-07 (the owner's "give the AI a play-to-score weight instead of changing the scoring, which would be a knob" idea — a genuine unbuilt lever: `simulator._target_economics_bonus` only implements the kill-EFFICIENCY half ("can I hurt it"), and its own docstring notes "no separate victory-point-weighted term"). Adds `Battle._vp_yield_bonus` — a multiplicative ranged target-priority tilt composed into the `_do_shoot` `min()`-key beside the threat/economics/focus chain — that boosts a candidate whose DESTRUCTION banks VP: x1.8 MONSTER/VEHICLE (Bring It Down), x1.4 on-objective (deny primary), rising to x2.0 as the whole unit nears death (No Prisoners). AM-scoped (a faction-neutral target bias washes on closed-matrix symmetry — the SWEG_TAU_GUIDED lesson). Cited `simulator.vp_chase_targeting` (data/rule_citations.d/vp_chase_targeting.json), audit green, run.py --cli clean, byte-identical off (0 flips scoped AM N=20). DIAGNOSIS that motivated it (instrument): AM scores only ~9.1 secondary VP/game (real killy AM ~25-35), killing 43.6 enemy MODELS/game but only 1.3 of 5.8 enemy MONSTER/VEHICLE — it clears chaff without FINISHING units or cracking the durable targets its Bring It Down edge feeds; the sim over-scores primary (35.7 vs real ~29) and under-scores secondary (12.4 vs ~22.7), forcing firepower-army AM into the body-presence game it loses. RESULT: the mechanism WORKS (pilot battery N=40: AM mean VP up almost everywhere — DG 42.9→46, IK →39.1, GSC →34.5) but does NOT convert to net wins. Battery hard-replacement: +7.5 DG / +5 Necrons / −5 IK / −5 GSC / **−12.5 Orks** (chasing VP vs a melee horde ignores the incoming threats → overrun) = net-negative. Production SOFT-multiplier scoped eval N=40 vs sc57a: **AM 28.0 → 27.3, paired −0.65 (ci 0.91, 77 flips), gated 3.08 → 3.10** — still wrong-direction: the extra VP pads the margin in the far-behind matchups (IK/GSC AM loses anyway) and mis-prioritises vs melee, outweighing the DG/Necrons conversions. Held (faithful play-to-score heuristic, preserved). REOPEN after the durability/objective-presence over-reward is corrected structurally — like the whole held AI-lever family, chasing VP turns net-positive only once AM can actually contest the primary game its extra secondary VP is meant to complement. CONCLUSION: FIVE AM piloting channels now exhausted — offensive-orders, defensive-hold (Cadia Stands!), positioning (staging), claim-and-bank, and chase-VP — the −17.3 is a structural objective-presence floor (the fragile mirror of the durability-over-reward wall), not a piloting or fidelity gap. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].
- **Astra Militarum DEEP AUDIT (2026-07-07, four parallel agents + orchestrator instrumentation) — no metric-moving lever found; the −17.3 is upstream objective-presence, not a fidelity gap.** Angles + verdicts: (1) DATASHEETS faithful — tanks/artillery correctly statted, no main-gun mutex bug, invuln/FNP clean; the one real strip is infantry special/heavy weapons behind `SWEG_AM_BATTLELINE_SPECIALS` (default-off), but SCREENED it is **−0.25 metric-neutral** (the ledger's earlier "~+5pp" was an untested estimate that does NOT survive measurement) — faithful data-correction, adopt on fidelity grounds only, not a lift. (2) ORDERS / Voice of Command RULES faithful (roster, per-officer counts, buffs, 6" aura all sourced), but ISSUANCE under-fires — ~2.3 orders/round issued (instrument) vs ~9–11 fielded capacity, throttled by the 6" reach (only ~4.3 of ~14 squads reachable) + officer positioning; HOWEVER the offensive-order channel is a DOCUMENTED EXHAUSTED VEIN (Grizzled −7.31, order-trio inert, Duty-and-Honour +0.55) — +1-to-hit on lasguns cannot convert past the durability-in-contest wall, so raising coverage is only an unblocker for a Grizzled re-screen, not a standalone lift. (3) LIST/DETACHMENT — real meta detachment is Grizzled Company but flipping it regresses (needs orders it can't deliver); an unscreened list gap (Vanquisher/Field-Ordnance/Bullgryn 0/20) exists but the "too few officers" claim was WRONG (template fields ~8 officer model-instances / ~4-5 officer squads, order-rich). (4) AI-LEVER RE-VALIDATION — the four adopted default-on AM levers (ADVANCE_DISCIPLINE, FIRE_SUPPORT_HOLD, SECONDARY_PURSUIT, STAGING) are individually INERT now (subsumed — all feed the same `_suppress_advance` block) but COLLECTIVELY still load-bearing (all-four-off = AM −2.99 decisive, gated +0.17); STAGING alone is inert not wrong-sign (N=20 +0.65 was noise; N=40 +0.10). All rejected/default-off gates correctly off; the two reverted ones (CHARGE_DISCIPLINE, OFFICER_FRONT_LINE) have no live read site. Per-opponent profile: AM under 50% vs 20 of 21 factions (uniform systemic weakness, only T'au 49.4% near-even, craters even vs under-poles GSC 6.9% / Orks 31.9%). THE ROOT CAUSE localized: AM is on objectives only ~10% of unit-rounds — it barely establishes objective PRESENCE, so every lever that operates on objective-holding (clear-the-holder offence, survive-there defence, staging position) cannot bite. The productive channel remains DEFENSIVE-OBJECTIVE (only sticky objectives +1.87 ever moved AM — because it BANKS the marker without needing the body to stay), and the real AM lever is UPSTREAM: get bodies onto and holding objectives (a fragility/positioning problem, the AM mirror of the durability-in-contest wall). CLAIM-AND-BANK follow-up (2026-07-07) — REFUTED as a field-weighted lift: the pilot battery (army-A-scoped, N=40) tested sending AM's sticky Cadian Shock Troops to TOUCH-and-CLAIM markers they control on arrival (so sticky banks them even as the body dies). `sticky_race` alone is negative everywhere (−5 to −10, +0 vs Necrons); the `sticky_race + focus_threat` combination (claim markers WHILE finishing the enemy's crackable scoring bodies) is positive ONLY vs Death Guard (+10, 8 L→W / 4 W→L) and negative/flat vs every other opponent (Imperial Knights −10, Genestealer Cults −10, Orks −17.5, Necrons −2.5) — DG-specific (its crackable durable scoring-bodies are the sweet spot) and net-negative across the field, the same fragility wall that killed SWEG_MASS_BODIES (pushing fragile bodies forward to claim gets them killed for a marker gain that does not offset the lost body-work + the opponent out-scoring). CONCLUSION: all four AM piloting channels — offensive (orders/guns), defensive-hold (Cadia Stands!), positioning (staging), and claim-and-bank — are now exhausted; the −17.3 is a structural objective-presence floor on this frame, the fragile mirror of the durability-over-reward wall (the Stage-2 owner fork), not a piloting or fidelity gap. New reusable instrument approach in the report. See [[project-durability-overreward-structural]] and [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]].
- Mid-game re-embark `SWEG_REEMBARK` — BUILT + HELD default-off 2026-07-01. A disembarked unit was stuck on foot all game; `_maybe_reembark_after_move` (code/simulator.py, end of `_do_move`) lets it re-embark into a friendly transport within 3" after its move (not if in Engagement Range or the sole objective controller; guarded against fleeing/abandoning objectives). Full-matrix N=40 vs sc29a: NEAR-INERT (very low flips, no decisive movers, gated 2.58 → 2.69 within noise) — the 3" re-embark condition is rarely met on the archetype frame, so it barely fires and nudges the metric slightly negative. Held (wave-248 pattern), NOT adopted default-on. Cited `simulator.reembark` (extends `simulator.embark`); off byte-identical. REOPEN when: the archetype lists feature transports more heavily (more re-embark opportunities), OR after the durability-over-reward is corrected (re-embarking durable units survive to over-score, part of the wall). Verified faithful; the gap it fills is real, the metric impact is just structurally suppressed on this frame.
- **Ork Boyz infantry loadout stripping — FOUND + PARKED, 2026-06-29.** The BSData mapper drops the special-weapon swaps from the basic "1 Sergeant + 9 Troopers" battleline squads (their special weapons are nested where the loadout extractor misses them, unlike Tempestus Scions / Kasrkin which have a direct "w/ Special weapon" group the mapper catches). A cross-faction scan (2026-06-29) found the genuinely-stripped shooting battleline is narrow: **Astra Militarum Cadian Shock Troops + Death Korps of Krieg (FIXED via `SWEG_AM_BATTLELINE_SPECIALS`, default-off — faithful, ~+5pp vs the World Eaters / Orks craters; AM is the −20.7 dominant under-pole) AND Ork Boyz** (a few big shootas + the nob's options). The **Ork Boyz fix is PARKED, not applied**: Orks are **+3.2 OVER**, so arming Boyz pushes them further over-pole — wrong direction for the headline. Reopen if Orks become under-poled, or in a faithfulness-first pass that adopts all loadout-fidelity corrections regardless of metric direction. Everything else flagged "1-weapon" is naturally single-weapon and faithful (Necron Warriors, Tyranid gaunts, Daemon Horrors, Custodes Sagittarum, Aeldari Rangers); Marines / Sisters / Mechanicus / Drukhari / Scions / Kasrkin are correctly armed. So the stripping is NOT a broad multi-faction MAE driver — it is AM-localized plus this parked Ork case.
- Displacement Stage 2 swarm charge-to-contest (`SWEG_DISPLACE_SWARM`, default-OFF) — wave 238/239 N=80 paired A/B vs the 5.83 anchor: +0.07 WASH MISSING ITS TARGET (Adeptus Custodes −0.05 flat, Imperial Knights +0.83 wrong-direction; ~100 body-army flips = fires-but-churns). Code + tests kept (`724252e`); reopen for a paired re-test after a body-army composition change (e.g. the Chaos Space Marines real-list archetype reshape). See [[project-displacement-greenlit]].
- Candidate B AI massing body-armies onto markers (`SWEG_MASS`) — expected wash; if it washes, report as a one-Unit-per-model representation limit and STOP chasing it (no knob/nerf). See [[project-matchup-fidelity-diagnosis]].
- Imperial Knights / Daemons positional residual — likely a one-Unit-per-model representation limit; reopen only with a representation change, never a knob. See [[project-oc-contest-faithful]].
- **"Per-model representation re-model" — RETIRED as a pending solution, 2026-06-28** (project-owner-prompted review; full verdict at `docs/STRUCTURAL_REMODEL_PLAN.md` top block). It was never one change: the squad-frame piece (`SWEG_FILL_SQUADS`) is already done/inert; the clustering piece (`SWEG_MOVEPLAN`, built, default-off) is a one-env-var flip predicted to wash (helps both poles' hordes, doesn't change who survives the snapshot) and needs only a single confirm-and-close screen; the "fragile-army representation fix" everything else was "blocked behind" was never defined and is dropped. The contest math is faithful (10e counts only within-3" models), so there is no faithful representation fix beyond clustering. Stop quoting it. The genuinely open lead is the going-first / per-round-tempo over-reward (`SWEG_KITE`, built, unscreened) — NOT a re-model. Two screens close this out: `SWEG_MOVEPLAN` (confirm wash) and `SWEG_KITE` (the real lead).
- Stage 2 (equation fit) outputs — provisional until Stage 1 converges; `calibrated_points` / `equilibrium_points` are redo-able, do not treat as final.
- Chaos Lord "Chance for Glory" — the real rule is a once-per-battle self buff, NOT a +1-to-wound aura; current flag is a fabrication firing on nothing. Do NOT route it onto a squad until the effect is corrected.
- Dice-roll vectorization (numpy-batch the per-attack `random.randint` loop, `units.py:3485`) — 2-4× per-game, but it BREAKS determinism and invalidates every Common-Random-Numbers anchor log; reopen only if eval speed becomes a permanent hard constraint that the cheaper levers can't meet.
- PyPy / alternate runtime (the "Rust/faster runtime" angle) — 2-5× theoretical on the pure-Python hot path, but unproven Windows-spawn + dataclasses compatibility needs a real shakedown; the structural "big bet", deferred until the cheaper throughput levers are exhausted.
- Wave-248 Astra Militarum order/stratagem trio — all three BUILT, cited, unit-tested, default-OFF, and measured BYTE-INERT (zero flips, every faction) on the current archetype frame. (a) Ursula Creed two-Order `SWEG_CREED_TWO_ORDERS` (`3031bfa`) — reopens when the Order-allocation policy can double-Order one squad. (b) Reinforcements! `SWEG_REINFORCEMENTS` (`5b312b5`) — reopens when the eval AI reaches the dead-INFANTRY-REGIMENT + command-point + fire-gate + round-end trigger. (c) Fix Bayonets! engagement routing `SWEG_FIXBAYONETS` (`9f1a989`) — reopens when an Astra Militarum archetype fields a melee-statted squad (the melee-Order scorer branch is dead for all-ranged lists). META: order/stratagem fidelity is EXHAUSTED as an Astra-Militarum lever family; the 19.89 under-pole is per-model activation representation, not orders. See [[project-faction-residual-rootcause]] and [[project-ai-frozen-under-mae-first]].
- Astra Militarum archetype rebuild (`SWEG_AM_REALISM`, `aac02b8`, default-OFF) — BUILT + HELD for the next re-anchor (user-directed 2026-06-15). The OFF build was the hull-spam C-D-tier variant (7-8 vehicles, 59-75% points in hulls, ~20-33 infantry / 28-63 models); the gated rebuild fields the real Combined Arms shape (~48-92 infantry / 54-97 models, ~44-59% in vehicles), cited from Best Coast Pairings / tournament lists. FAITHFUL but METRIC-NEUTRAL: AM-scoped N=80 merged into sc12a (paired_delta --scoped) flipped 481 games for +0.90 +-3.52 on Astra Militarum (indeterminate), headline 5.78 -> 5.79 wash. PROOF body count is NOT the lever — even with real bodies Astra Militarum wins ~23%; the -19.9 under-pole is structural (durability-in-contest + under-modelled Orders / trading), not the list. Fold into the frame at the next re-anchor. SWEG_MASS_TANKS (push durable VEHICLE/MONSTER onto contested markers) was REJECTED as the AM fix — A/B moved AM only +1.6 but over-helped in-band T'au +15.9 (T'au is vehicle-dense + shooty; it may itself under-contest in the sim, a separate thread); gate deleted.
- Astra Militarum Recon Element cover, "Masters of Camouflage" (`SWEG_AM_RECON`, `f9ae727`, default-OFF) — BUILT + HELD for the re-anchor alongside the body rebuild. Army-wide Benefit of Cover (+1 save vs ranged) for AM INFANTRY/WALKER (cited `simulator.masters_of_camouflage`; the meta's top detachment + LVO XII champion, vs the offence detachment Combined Arms the sim otherwise models). Combined A/B (BOTH SWEG_AM_REALISM + SWEG_AM_RECON, AM-scoped N=80 merged into sc12a): FAITHFUL, marginally favourable — AM 22.2 -> 23.3 (+1.13 +-3.38 indeterminate, 480 flips), headline 5.78 -> 5.74 (-0.04, vs the body-only +0.01 wash; the cover adds the favourable direction). CONCLUSION: the -19.9 Astra Militarum under-pole is NOT the list and NOT the detachment (both now faithfully modelled, AM still ~23%). It is STRUCTURAL: (1) the objective game rewards winning CONTESTED markers (durability) which T3 bodies lose; (2) cover is ranged-only (faithful) so the bodies still die to the melee over-pole charging them; (3) no screening AI keeps chargers off AM's backline bodies (AM's real anti-melee tool, unmodelled). The remaining faithful AM lever WAS a SCREENING AI — now BUILT + TESTED + REJECTED (`SWEG_SCREEN_AI`, decisively wrong-direction, see the REVERTED section); the durability-in-contest scoring is the off-table representation/scoring wall, now confirmed as the ONLY remaining AM lever. See [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]] and [[project-cover-symmetric-tax-refuted]].
- Aeldari Fire and Fade fabrication removal (`SWEG_AELDARI_FNF_FAITHFUL`, default-OFF) — BUILT + A/B-CONFIRMED FAVOURABLE + HELD for the next re-anchor (2026-06-15). The Warhost stratagem Fire and Fade is a pure post-shoot D6+1" REPOSITION (zero combat-roll impact) but the sim grants a fabricated `transient_reroll_hits_shooting` offensive proxy (the `Stratagem.Fire and Fade` citation itself admits it is "mechanically unrelated" to the rule). The gate keeps the faithful command-point spend (the stratagem IS used every round in real play) but drops the fabricated buff — fidelity-first fabrication removal, right-direction for the +7.52 Aeldari over-pole. Aeldari-scoped N=80 merged into sc12a (paired_delta --scoped): Aeldari 52.2 -> 50.2 (paired -1.93 +-4.01, 705 flips, CI spans 0 = indeterminate decisiveness but consistent point estimate), headline 5.78 -> 5.74 (-0.04), all other factions flat. KEPT on fidelity-first (a fabrication removal is adopted regardless of metric magnitude when not decisively harmful; here it is favourable). OFF byte-identical (sim_motion_proof fingerprint unchanged). Aeldari is NOT the elite-low-model representation wall (63.7 models, normal count) so the residual is partially addressable on-table; Fire and Fade is the first removal. Found by the 2026-06-15 Aeldari over-pole audit (also flagged: Blitzing Firepower fires [SUSTAINED HITS 1] without the real 12" range gate = next candidate; Lightning-Fast Reactions +1-save proxy for -1-to-hit = minor). See [[project-aeldari-fire-and-fade-fabrication]].
- Grey Knights Fury of Titan deep-strike gate (`SWEG_GK_FURY_FAITHFUL`, default-OFF) — BUILT + A/B-CONFIRMED FAVOURABLE + HELD for the next re-anchor (2026-06-15). Teleport Strike Force applies `reroll_hit_ones`+`reroll_wound_ones` ARMY-WIDE ALWAYS-ON, but real Fury of Titan gates both on a unit that arrived via Deep Strike THAT TURN (the detachment notes admit the gate was dropped as an approximation). New detachment field `reroll_ones_requires_deep_strike` (set on Teleport Strike Force) + a `_fresh_arrivals`-keyed "arrived this round" check in `effective_buffs` (per-activation buff cache → no stale state) apply the rerolls only to freshly-arrived units when the gate is set. GK-scoped N=80 merged into sc12a (paired_delta --scoped): Grey Knights 55.7 -> 46.2 (paired -9.48 +-3.97 DECISIVE), headline 5.78 -> 5.66 (-0.12). FAITHFUL + favourable; KEPT on fidelity-first. CAVEAT — crosses zero: GK was +4.70 over, the faithful gate lands it ~mild under (gated |residual| still smaller, ~4.7 -> ~2.8, so net toward target), because the always-on reroll was also PROXYING GK's unmodelled psychic / teleport-redeploy mechanics (flagged in the detachment notes). Closed-matrix cost: opponents drift up (Necrons +0.39, Adeptus Custodes +0.29, Tyranids +0.24, Adeptus Astartes +0.40 — over-poles worsen slightly; often washes at a re-priced re-anchor). FOLLOW-ON: model GK psychic/teleport to lift the revealed under-side. OFF byte-identical (sim_motion_proof unchanged). Found by the 2026-06-15 over-pole fabrication triage (top of 3: Necrons half-range Conquering Tyrant + AdMech-clean were the other two). See [[project-grey-knights-fury-always-on]].
- Wave-249 tabling-decides-on-Victory-Points (`SWEG_TABLING_VP`, `f59930f`) — BUILT, cited (verbatim Wahapedia matched-play, `data/rule_citations.d/core_win_condition.json`), default-OFF, near-inert. `_decide_winner`'s survivor-count auto-win contradicts the cited play-out-all-rounds behaviour, but the N=40 paired screen flipped only ~21/18,480 games (the play-out fix already aligns Victory Points with the tabling outcome): +0.05 wash, melee cluster flat, two tiny wrong-direction movers (World Eaters +0.36, Genestealer Cults +0.41). Faithful so kept; near-inert with a tiny adverse tilt so held off, not flipped off a 21-flip read. Reopen = run with `SWEG_TABLING_VP=1` at the next natural N=80 re-anchor (free) and decide the flip on the eighty-battle read. The hypothesis that the auto-win inflates the melee over-pole is REFUTED.
- Positional-secondary pursuit dormancy fix `SWEG_TAC_TRACK_LIVE` — BUILT + HELD default-off (2026-07-08, five-facet-audit follow-up). DIAGNOSIS (3-battle instrumented check): `_choose_secondary_track`/`SWEG_TAC_DECK` (default-on since wave 210) already runs the real Fixed-vs-Tactical choice and deals a genuine 2-card hand — NOT dormant — but the movement layer that would put a spare unit into a held card's scoring position (`_assign_card_pursuit`, plus the Establish Locus/Recover Assets positional filters) all read `_secondary_pursuit_enabled`, which defaults True for exactly one faction (Astra Militarum) and False for every other army; a T'au Empire army held Engage on All Fronts/Cleanse/Establish Locus/Defend Stronghold with 30/20/12/4/3 spare chaff across five activations and never once received a `pursue_target`. FIX: `_secondary_pursuit_enabled` also returns True when `SWEG_TAC_TRACK_LIVE=1`, for both armies — widens the existing, already-cited pursuit/positional-filter machinery from Astra-Militarum-only to any army the real track choice already put on Tactical; touches neither `_choose_secondary_track` nor any `code/secondaries.py` scorer. MECHANISM CHECK (6 battles gate-on): pursue-assignment events went from 0/12 army-observations at production defaults to nonzero for 8 of 10 mobile TACTICAL-track armies (Necrons 8, Orks 4, Tyranids 7, T'au Empire 8, Chaos Space Marines 4, Adeptus Mechanicus 4, World Eaters 6, Genestealer Cults 1); mean secondary Victory Points did NOT show a clean lift at this small N (12.9 → 12.25, noise-dominated). HELD, not adopted — per `docs/LEVER_PROTOCOL.md`'s settled "Faction-NEUTRAL piloting" family (washes or feeds the durable side) and the pre-existing wave-121/122 finding that this same isolated pursuit mechanism did not raise Behind Enemy Lines/Cleanse achieve rates when screened unscoped, do not flip default-on without a fresh N=40+ screen. REOPEN after the durability-over-reward wall is corrected structurally, or alongside a fix for the separate, un-touched card-coverage gap (only ~7 of the 19 drawable Tactical cards are pursuit-routed today).

## MELEE OVER-POLE — mechanisms RULED OUT (do not re-attempt without new information)
- Model-count list-inflation as the melee over-pole driver — REFUTED wave 249 by HARD-SOURCED real lists. A first diagnostic estimated the archetype builds 2-3× real model count; sourcing the actual May-2026 competitive lists (Best Coast Pairings via Grimhammer) found the top two over-poles build UNDER real count (World Eaters real ~67 vs sim ~61; Death Guard real ~48-58 vs sim ~39). Raising `SEED_FRACTION_BY_FACTION` for these MENU factions to cut model count = forbidden metric-tuning (no cited anchor for fewer models). Durability mis-application (`diag_durability`) and kill-efficiency (`diag_overshooter`) also ruled out. See [[project-list-realism-deflattered-baseline]] and [[project-one-unit-per-model-amplification]].
- Per-model FIGHT-phase activation as a SHARED cause — REFUTED 2026-06-14 (`diag_melee_activation`): in-band Adeptus Astartes carries the same per-model melee footprint (strikes/1kpt, free-move/1kpt) and is correctly rated. Missing-melee-counterplay also REFUTED (`diag_melee_counterplay`): overwatch + Fall Back fire MORE against the over-pole than vs Marines, which over-performs anyway.
- Deep-strike alpha as the melee over-pole cause — REFUTED 2026-06-14 by a one-sided causal A/B (`diag_ds_ab`, probe gate `SWEG_FORCE_ONBOARD`, N=64): only Death Guard relies on its alpha (-10.9 win%); Emperor's Children wins +17.2% MORE on-board; Daemons flat. "Benefits from alpha" does not track the over-pole (in-band T'au relies on it most, -15.6). The tempo correlation was spurious. CONCLUSION: no single Stage-1 mechanism — the cluster is heterogeneous; diagnose PER FACTION with the under-pole mirror in view (closed matrix). See [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]].
- NOTED TO FIX LATER (not a melee lever, possible under-pole lever): the deploy-AI reserve split (`simulator.py` ~7404, SWEG_DEPLOY_AI) is FACTION-BLIND and over-reserves Emperor's Children (+17.2% better on-board). Harmless for an over-pole faction, but the same bug likely mis-deploys a deep-strike-capable UNDER-pole faction (e.g. Drukhari) where smarter reserve choice would raise it toward target. Check when working any such under-pole.
- Going-second family "frame-confounded" hypothesis — RESOLVED 2026-07-08: the rejections STAND. Re-screened `SWEG_OVERWATCH_MOVE` full-frame N=20 against the unbiased sc59a frame: headline flat (gated −0.03 at matched N), nothing decisive, and Death Guard moves +3.60 AWAY from real (the durable side banks reactive fire) — the same durability-banking signature as the original rejection, now on clean measurement. The going-second family is closed for good; `SWEG_OVERWATCH_MOVE` stays built + held as faithful fidelity. Also screened: `SWEG_AELDARI_BLITZ_RANGE` (the 12-inch gate, keep-the-CP-spend-drop-the-effect shape) is INERT (0 flips gate-on, scoped N=20 — eval games never present a beyond-12-inch pick) — HELD default-off as free fidelity to fold at the next re-anchor.
- MAKE TERRAIN REAL (owner-approved 2026-07-09) — the last unfaithful subsystem: shooting has NO line of sight (cover is an angle-independent positional save-pip, the audited symmetric ~46% tax) and movement tunnels through walls (issue 52). Four gated stages designed (geometry substrate, movement collision, LoS shooting, then threat-layer planner integration); stage T3 is frame-change-class (fresh anchor + re-validation). FALSIFIABLE HYPOTHESIS on record: real occlusion is fragile armies' survival mechanism, so both remaining poles (AM −20, DG +16) should move toward real — judged by the facet instruments, not the headline. Full design docs/TERRAIN_REALISM_PROPOSAL.md.
- THREAT-PROJECTION LAYER (owner-proposed 2026-07-09) — PARKED-designed, the first substrate-class piloting proposal: per-enemy damage bubbles (guns at range + move-and-charge melee with the real 2D6 gradient) summed into a positional field, derived (knob-free) per-unit tolerance, charge scoring that subtracts P(kill)×target-threat and adds counter-charge reach — unifies the AM turn-1 exposure, Orks march, EC reserve-arrival, and adjacent-Berzerker charge blindness as ONE class. Mechanism-difference vs the settled positional family: operates on BOTH sides (fragile declines, durable/killy commits), which no rejected single-sided nudge did; falsifier = the walked-into-it rate, not the headline. Full design in docs/THREAT_LAYER_PROPOSAL.md; build is Tier-3 gated work pending the sc61a re-anchor + owner go.
- Lightning-Fast Reactions phase-scope fix (`SWEG_AELDARI_LFR_PHASE`) — BUILT + HELD-faithful default-off 2026-07-08 (commit 5b4a16e): replaces the wrong whole-round +1-save with the real ranged-only −1-to-hit via the Stealth/Smokescreen composition site, mechanism-checked (melee attacks correctly lose all protection gate-on). MEASURED: 0-flip off; scoped Aeldari N=40 −0.09 flat. Fold-in rider at the next re-anchor alongside SWEG_AELDARI_BLITZ_RANGE.
- Aeldari over-pole board-read pilot — VERDICT: LARGELY HONEST RESIDUAL (2026-07-08). Four lopsided wins vs calibrated (gated-0.00) opponents read round-by-round: no over-generous Aeldari mechanism decides any of them — the wins are faithful model-count/mobility objective breadth vs fewer-bigger-unit rosters, plus one OPPONENT-side Custodes overkill misplay (settled target-economics family, do not reopen). Concentration confirms the standing conclusion: Aeldari deflates as its opponents get fixed, not by touching Aeldari. One real crumb: Lightning-Fast Reactions is ROUND-scoped and proactively fired in the sim vs the verbatim single-PHASE reactive rule (stratagems.py:156-162, simulator.py:4719-4733/5667-5683) — a small verified over-credit, fix dispatched fidelity-first (expect flat). OPS FOOTGUN found: importing scripts.evaluate_vs_meta without PYTHONHASHSEED=0 triggers its self-relaunch guard and silently runs a FULL default eval — added to LEVER_PROTOCOL §5.
- OC-CONCENTRATION piloting (consolidate_oc / oc_stack, pilot battery instrument) — REFUTED-hypothesis 2026-07-08, the AM five-facet audit's one untested direction: consolidate flat on all four matchups (leaving hopeless contests costs nothing — thin contests were never the loss channel), stack DECISIVELY harmful (−10/−22.5/−35 vs Astartes/GSC/Death Guard) with the strict-control mechanism count FALLING (8→3, 9→3 on Death Guard seeds) — stacked arrivals die before their Objective Control registers, the fragility wall confirmed from the allocation side after the bodies (mass-bodies) and claim (sticky-race) sides. Presence-without-control survives its most direct counter; AM's deficit is OC-mass-that-survives, off-table on this frame. The 2026-07-08 five-facet comparative audit (all facets measured vs field baselines; survival confirmed stats-faithful; four proposed human lines executed and none flip their games) STANDS AS THE STAGE-2 BOUNDARY DOCUMENT for the Astra Militarum pole.
- Missing-tempo-cost (army SPEED unpriced) as the Death Guard / Astra Militarum axis driver — REFUTED 2026-07-08 by a read-only instrumented study (32 games, speed spectrum): speed IS priced — Pearson(mean Move, round-2 primary) = +0.918, first-reach round −0.785, fast armies hold ~1.7 markers round 2 vs slow ~0.8, and Death Guard's Move-5 arrival timing matches hand arithmetic exactly (`_move_toward` clamps to `effective_move`, geometry.py:216-247). What the data re-confirmed instead is the settled durability-over-reward-via-tempo family: fragile-fast armies grab markers early then get ground OFF them while Death Guard posts the highest round-3 primary — do not re-derive; the only open path is the frame-confounded going-second re-screens.
- Cover asymmetry (cover is ranged-only + positional/angle-independent `cover_at(target.position)` + the AI hugs cover, so shooters are taxed and melee bypasses) as the melee over-pole / Astra Militarum under-pole driver — REFUTED 2026-06-15 by direct measurement (`diag_cover`, read-only `SWEG_COVER_INSTR` instrument, since reverted; serial panel, ~46k shooting activations). Cover is a real but SYMMETRIC ~46% output tax: by-DEFENDER cov%out over-pole mean 46.5% (Death Guard 53.3 / World Eaters 43.7 / Emperor's Children 48.0 / Chaos Daemons 41.0) vs field mean 46.1% (Astra Militarum 42.1 / T'au 49.4 / Orks 50.8 / Custodes 45.8 / Imperial Knights 42.3) — the melee over-pole are NOT shot into cover more than anyone else (two of the four sit BELOW field; shooty Orks/T'au sit ABOVE; under-pole AM dead-centre). By-ATTACKER, AM 51.0 is modestly taxed but T'au (the other gunline) 43.9 is below field — they split, so a cover-tax-on-shooters does not track the under-pole either. An angle-dependent-cover + shooter-outpositioning build would lift all shooters and strip all huggers ~uniformly → no relative-win-rate (gated-MAE) movement by closed-matrix logic. The cover lever family is closed. See [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]] and [[project-cover-symmetric-tax-refuted]].
- Objective-holder DISPLACEMENT (the owner's "clear the enemy unit controlling the marker, specifically" idea, built per-faction to dodge the symmetric wash) — REFUTED 2026-07-05. Two variants in the `pilot_manual` shoot-target hook: `clear_holder` (concentrate the WHOLE army's fire on the single enemy unit on a marker we CONTEST, our-OC>0 but <=theirs, to flip it) and `shoot_holders_any` (the ledger's retired SWEG_SHOOT_HOLDERS reproduced — most-wounded enemy on ANY marker, non-anti-armour). Rate-faithful army-A-scoped battery, N=40: **flat-to-noise for the prime candidate (Astra Militarum, the biggest under-pole) in BOTH durable matchups** — clear_holder AM-vs-DG +0.0 (1-1 flips), AM-vs-IK +0.0, Orks-vs-IK +0.0; shoot_holders_any AM-vs-IK +0.0, AM-vs-DG +2.5 (ONE game, one-directional, noise-scale). The contest/surgical combos CRATER AM (-25 to -30: moving AM bodies off position to contest gets them shot off and cedes their own markers). This reproduces the ledger's own SHOOT_HOLDERS finding — its celebrated "+2.5 asymmetric AM-vs-IK" was a single-game artifact (the symmetric production gate was AM -0.2 flat). MECHANISM (why it can't work): the fragile under-pole cannot DISLODGE a durable holder either way — it lacks the firepower to CLEAR a T10/18W Knight or FNP Plague-Marine holder even concentrating, and it lacks the surviving bodies to OUT-OC it (its bodies die approaching). Redirecting fire onto the holder does not raise total effectiveness because fire is fungible — the deficit is total firepower/survivability vs durable targets, NOT target ALLOCATION. So the DG/IK/CK-over ↔ AM/Daemons-under durability axis is confirmed NOT an AI target-selection gap, from the displacement angle too.
- NO SINGLE AGGREGATE MECHANISM (2026-07-05 re-confirmation of the wave-234 "diagnose per faction" conclusion, from fresh angles prompted by the owner's rejection of the "durability" label). Correlated the per-faction sc57a error against army stats and game-shape, all falsified: (1) DURABILITY/toughness — Aeldari (T3.1, softest army) is +8.2 OVER, Knights over-perform on the LOWEST damage output (98/95); opposite profiles, both over. (2) SECONDARY VP — uniform ~12/faction (`diag_prim_sec_by_faction`), symmetric, does not discriminate (the global 12.4-vs-22.7 under-score is a level issue, not a per-faction one). (3) PRIMARY VP level — tracks win-vs-spread, not the error. (4) ATTRITION / survival / killing (`diag_attrition`) — the three biggest over-poles (DG/IK/CK) KILL THE LEAST (34-35% enemy destroyed by r5) and Orks (under) SURVIVE THE MOST (64% alive); survival != scoring, neither correlates. The one real pattern is that durable objective-HOLDERS retain OC-on-marker (DG scores highest primary 38.3 while killing least) — but that is the DG/Knights story only and explicitly NOT Aeldari (a separate killy/mobile datasheet-strength gap). Measured GLOBAL game-shape distortions (`diag_signatures` N=60): going-first 61.7% (real ~50%), primary 35.7 (real ~29), secondary 12.4 (real ~22.7), FLAT round-2-5 primary trajectory (no late-game attrition decay) — all REAL but SYMMETRIC (don't pick a faction). CONCLUSION stands: the poles are heterogeneous and faction-specific; the productive path is per-faction fidelity/list/piloting diagnosis (which banks wins — Magnus/Orks/EC/GSC), NOT a unified durability/scoring knob. New reusable diagnostics: `scripts/diag_prim_sec_by_faction.py`, `scripts/diag_attrition.py`. See [[project-melee-overpole-shared-cause-refuted]] and [[project-durability-overreward-structural]].

## OPEN LEVER — melee over-pole archetype COMPOSITION fidelity (wave 250+, now cited)
- The real list-realism gap for the melee cluster is composition / detachment, NOT model count, and is metric-direction-NEUTRAL (genuinely faithful). World Eaters: three sourced lists are Khârn-anchored (130 pt), no Angron (500 pt) — correct `code/archetypes.py` "Berzerker Warband" to match (STRONGEST-cited single case, start here). Chaos Daemons: meta is Be'lakor Scintillating Legion (Tzeentch), not the sim's four mono-god sub-archetypes (bigger reshape, needs its own sourcing). Emperor's Children: "Slaaneshi Excess" detachment does not exist in 10e (real: Coterie of the Conceited / Court of the Phoenician / Peerless Bladesmen). FRAME-LEVEL (archetype lists feed every eval) + user-reserved list zone → handle deliberately, A/B before/after, cite the sourced lists in the archetype comment. NOTE: Emperor's Children seeding Slaanesh daemon datasheets is FAITHFUL 10e codex design, NOT a faction-boundary bug (a research over-claim refuted in code). The melee over-pole may ultimately be the per-model representation limit — composition fidelity is the last faithful lever before that conclusion.

## FORBIDDEN (user-ruled out of bounds — never propose)
- Re-fitting stats / overrides / lists to force win rates — metric-tuning, poisons Stage 2; the win-rate gap is sim-fidelity, not stats. See [[project-winrate-gap-is-sim-fidelity-not-stats]].
- Make-way / horde-AI-nerf to free Knights through walls — the walls are a faithful horde strategy; fix faithfully (friendly pass-through, pathfind-around). See [[project-physical-board-control-avenue2]].
- Gating a faithful 10e mechanic OFF to protect the metric — fidelity-first; a rising headline is expected + authorised. See [[project-fidelity-first-rebuild-authorized]].
- The "re-calibration" conclusion (declaring the sim done by re-fitting to the target) — killed by user ruling.

## OPEN LEVERS (ranked; the live to-do — authority for ordering is the `docs/CURRENT_STATE.md` head)
1. **Throughput tooling.** Wave-218 suite (paired / Common-Random-Numbers delta, sequential early-stop, regression sentinel, gated caches, robust wrapper) — LANDED + watchdog-verified, shipped in pull request #64. **NEXT (user-approved 2026-06-09):** matchup-scoping (a `--factions` filter that runs only a changed faction's row + column — ~42 of 462 cells — and merges against a full saved anchor; about 10× on single-faction band waves, composes with the paired method) plus two trivial infra tweaks (a worker-count override flag, and chunksize 8 → 50-100). Full buildable spec is in `LOOP_QA.md`. See [[feedback-paired-crn-low-n-ab]].
2. **Random-fill re-base (greenlit).** `_random_fill` `add_unit`-loop → `add_squad(chosen, size)`, gated `SWEG_FILL_SQUADS`, N=80 A/B (big re-base, low Common-Random-Numbers benefit), then re-verify collision + the band levers on the corrected frame. See [[project-one-unit-per-model-amplification]].
3. **Band program on the squad frame (gated 7.05) — full plan in `docs/BAND_PROGRAM_PLAN.md`.** The residual is one systemic bias (sim rewards kill/durability/OC-by-count, under-rewards soft win conditions) — BUT the soft mechanics are ALREADY BUILT (battle-shock `_run_battleshock_phase`, reserves/deep-strike/Cult-Ambush `_reserves`/`decide_deepstrike_drops`/`cult_ambush_resurgence`, action economy `_unit_can_perform_action`), all verified in-code. **So this is DIAGNOSE-FIRST: the gap is fidelity / AI-piloting / tuning of existing mechanics, not missing builds** (the research agent over-claimed "missing" 3× — vehicle-action-ban, battle-shock, reserves — all refuted vs code; ground mechanic-existence in the CODE, research only for the WHY). Cross-faction key: closed matrix, raising the under-pole LOWERS the over-pole — never a direct elite nerf. Method per residual: scoped diagnostic (`--factions`, now cheap) + board-state inspection → localize the gap → targeted faithful fix. Order by residual: **Daemons −21 + Genestealer Cults −20.5** (deep-strike ON → why under? arrival-AI/objective-grab/Corrupt-Realspace) first, then **Imperial Knights +17** (positional OC-reach on squad frame, [[project-oc-contest-faithful]]), then Sororitas/CSM/Astra Militarum, then Tyranids/remaining over-pole (re-measure post-matrix). See [[project-faction-residual-rootcause]] and [[project-ai-piloting-top-lever]].
4. **Screen the held kiting lever `SWEG_KITING` (owner-flagged to-do, 2026-07-07).** N=80 paired, kiting-on versus the shared off arm, against a FRESH re-anchor — a fresh one is mandatory anyway: the `SWEG_CHARGE_TRADE` adoption changed the default frame, the sc52a-and-later anchor logs are not on this checkout, and there are uncommitted army-builder changes (EVAL_PROTOCOL rules 1/3/6 — batch it with the wave's other screens, one re-anchor). Decision rule agreed with the owner: KEEP default-on if the read is a wash (within roughly ±0.2 gated); LEAVE HELD if decisively worse. Context: the rail-less sibling `SWEG_KITE_MOVE` read +1.12 at N=80 via the durability over-reward; this lever adds the objective-marker, victory-point, chaff, and can-hurt-it rails specifically against that failure mode. Never enable both kite levers together. The behaviour itself is real play (owner-attested; the gunline ranged-hold family is its standing cousin), so a bad read localizes to the scoring substrate, not to whether players kite.
